/* =====================================================================
 *  TURBO HIGHWAY  ·  Juego de coches arcade pseudo-3D (canvas 2D)
 *
 *  CÓMO USARLO
 *   1. Abre cualquier página en Chrome (mejor una en blanco o normal).
 *   2. Pulsa F12 → pestaña "Consola".
 *   3. Pega TODO este script y pulsa Enter.
 *   4. Haz clic en la pantalla del juego para darle el foco del teclado.
 *
 *  CONTROLES
 *   ↑ / W ........ Acelerar          ESPACIO / SHIFT ... Nitro
 *   ↓ / S ........ Frenar            P ................. Pausa
 *   ← → / A D .... Girar             M ................. Sonido on/off
 *   R ............ Reiniciar         ESC ............... Pausa / Salir
 *   C ............ Garaje (skins de coche)
 *   Q ............ Volver al menú (también hay botones en pausa y al terminar)
 *   N ............ Mapas: Autopista · Neo Ciudad (futurista) · Gran Premio (F1)
 *   Cámara: al acelerar (↑) se aleja y sube; al frenar (↓) se acerca.
 *
 *  OBJETIVO: no te quedes sin tiempo. Cruza los checkpoints para ganar
 *  segundos, esquiva el tráfico, adelanta rozando coches para ganar
 *  puntos y nitro. ¡Cada vuelta cambia el escenario!
 * ===================================================================== */
(function () {
  'use strict';

  // Si ya hay una partida abierta, se cierra antes de crear la nueva.
  if (window.__TURBO_GAME__ && typeof window.__TURBO_GAME__.destroy === 'function') {
    try { window.__TURBO_GAME__.destroy(); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
  }
  if (!document || !document.documentElement) { console.error('[Turbo Highway] No hay DOM disponible.'); return; }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  Utilidades
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  const U = {
    limit: (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(v, b)),
    rnd: (a, b) => a + Math.random() * (b - a),
    rndInt: (a, b) => Math.floor(a + Math.random() * (b - a + 1)),
    pick: (arr) => arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)],
    interp: (a, b, p) => a + (b - a) * p,
    increase: (s, inc, max) => { const r = (s + inc) % max; return r < 0 ? r + max : r; },
    percentRemaining: (n, total) => (n % total) / total,
    easeIn: (a, b, p) => a + (b - a) * p * p,
    easeOut: (a, b, p) => a + (b - a) * (1 - (1 - p) * (1 - p)),
    easeInOut: (a, b, p) => a + (b - a) * ((-Math.cos(p * Math.PI) / 2) + 0.5),
    fog: (d, k) => 1 / Math.exp(d * d * k),
    overlap: (x1, w1, x2, w2, pct) => {
      const half = (pct || 1) / 2;
      return !((x1 + w1 * half) < (x2 - w2 * half) || (x1 - w1 * half) > (x2 + w2 * half));
    }
  };
  const TAU = Math.PI * 2;
  const hex = (h) => { const n = parseInt(h.slice(1), 16); return [(n >> 16) & 255, (n >> 8) & 255, n & 255]; };
  const mixc = (a, b, t) => [a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * t, a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t, a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * t];
  const css = (c, a) => (a === undefined
    ? 'rgb(' + (c[0] | 0) + ',' + (c[1] | 0) + ',' + (c[2] | 0) + ')'
    : 'rgba(' + (c[0] | 0) + ',' + (c[1] | 0) + ',' + (c[2] | 0) + ',' + a + ')');
  const shade = (c, f) => [Math.min(255, c[0] * f), Math.min(255, c[1] * f), Math.min(255, c[2] * f)];
  const thousands = (n) => String(Math.floor(n)).replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, (typeof cfg !== 'undefined' && cfg.lang === 'en') ? ',' : '.');

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  Constantes del motor pseudo-3D
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  const STEP = 1 / 60;
  const segmentLength = 200;
  const rumbleLength = 3;
  const roadWidth = 2000;
  const lanes = 3;
  const fov = 100;
  const cameraHeight = 1000; // solo fija la posición lógica del coche respecto a "position"
  const drawDistance = 300;
  const fogDensity = 2.6;    // menos niebla: los coches lejanos se distinguen mejor
  const cameraDepth = 1 / Math.tan((fov / 2) * Math.PI / 180);
  const playerZ = cameraHeight * cameraDepth;
  // Cámara dinámica: ↑ la aleja y la sube, ↓ la acerca y la baja
  const CAM_R = 0.82;       // el coche queda a ~91% de la altura de la pantalla
  const CAM_BASE_H = 1800;  // altura por defecto (bastante por encima del coche)
  const CAM_RANGE = 550;    // cuánto sube/baja con ↑ / ↓
  const maxSpeed = segmentLength / STEP;
  const accel = maxSpeed / 5;
  const breaking = -maxSpeed;
  const decel = -maxSpeed / 5;
  const offRoadDecel = -maxSpeed / 2;
  const offRoadLimit = maxSpeed / 4;
  const centrifugal = 0.28;
  const playerW = 0.3;
  const KMH = 270 / maxSpeed;
  const MAP_STRIDE = 4;
  const M = 60; // margen extra para el temblor de pantalla

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  Escenarios (se mezclan suavemente al cambiar de vuelta)
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------

  function blendTheme(a, b, t) {
    const o = {};
    for (const k in a) o[k] = Array.isArray(a[k]) ? mixc(a[k], b[k], t) : a[k] + (b[k] - a[k]) * t;
    return o;
  }

  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');

  canvas.style.cssText = 'display:block;width:100%;height:100%;outline:none;';
  overlay.appendChild(canvas);
  (document.body || document.documentElement).appendChild(overlay);
  let ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });
  const mainCtx = ctx;
  if (!ctx) { overlay.remove(); console.error('[Turbo Highway] Canvas 2D no disponible.'); return; }

  let W = 0, H = 0, DPR = 1;
  const gy = () => H * (0.5 + CAM_R / 2); // y del suelo bajo el coche del jugador
  function resize() {
    DPR = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    W = window.innerWidth || 800; H = window.innerHeight || 600;
    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.floor(W * DPR));
    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.floor(H * DPR));
  }

  // Ajustes del jugador (se guardan en el navegador)
  const CFG_DEF = { vol: 1, music: 1, skid: true, weather: true, shake: true, names: true, rain: 0.28, snow: 0.22, lang: 'es' };
  const cfg = Object.assign({}, CFG_DEF);
  try {
    const rc = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('turbo_highway_cfg') || 'null');
    if (rc && typeof rc === 'object') {
      if (rc.lang === 'en' || rc.lang === 'es') cfg.lang = rc.lang;
      if (typeof rc.vol === 'number' && isFinite(rc.vol)) cfg.vol = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, rc.vol));
      if (typeof rc.music === 'number' && isFinite(rc.music)) cfg.music = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, rc.music));
      ['rain', 'snow'].forEach(function (k) { if (typeof rc[k] === 'number' && isFinite(rc[k])) cfg[k] = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, rc[k])); });
      if (cfg.rain + cfg.snow > 1) cfg.snow = Math.max(0, 1 - cfg.rain);
      ['skid', 'weather', 'shake', 'names'].forEach(function (k) { if (typeof rc[k] === 'boolean') cfg[k] = rc[k]; });
    }
  } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
  function saveCfg() { try { window.localStorage.setItem('turbo_highway_cfg', JSON.stringify(cfg)); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ } }

  let trMap = null, trRe = null, trCache = Object.create(null), trN = 0;
  function buildTr() {
    trCache = Object.create(null); trN = 0; trMap = null; trRe = null;
    if (cfg.lang !== 'en') return;
    trMap = Object.create(null);
    const keys = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < I18N_EN.length; i++) { const k = I18N_EN[i][0].toLowerCase(); trMap[k] = I18N_EN[i][1]; keys.push(k); }
    keys.sort(function (a, b) { return b.length - a.length; });
    trRe = new RegExp('(?<![\\p{L}\\p{N}])(?:' + keys.map(function (k) { return k.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&'); }).join('|') + ')(?![\\p{L}\\p{N}])', 'giu');
  }
  function trCase(m, t) {
    if (m === m.toUpperCase() && m !== m.toLowerCase()) return t.toUpperCase();
    if (t === t.toUpperCase() && t !== t.toLowerCase()) t = t.toLowerCase();
    const f = m.charAt(0);
    if (f !== f.toLowerCase()) return t.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + t.slice(1);
    if (f !== f.toUpperCase()) return t.charAt(0).toLowerCase() + t.slice(1);
    return t;
  }
  function tr(s) {
    if (!trRe || typeof s !== 'string' || s.length < 2) return s;
    let c = trCache[s];
    if (c !== undefined) return c;
    c = s.replace(trRe, function (m) { const t = trMap[m.toLowerCase()]; return t === undefined ? m : trCase(m, t); });
    if (trN > 600) { trCache = Object.create(null); trN = 0; }
    trCache[s] = c; trN++;
    return c;
  }
  function setLang(l) { cfg.lang = l === 'en' ? 'en' : 'es'; buildTr(); saveCfg(); }
  buildTr();
  // Monedas y tienda (se guardan en el navegador)
  const BASE_KMH = 200, KMH_STEP = 20, MAX_LVL = 10, NITRO_MULT = 1.25, ABS_MAX_KMH = 500;
  const UPG_COST = [60, 120, 200, 300, 420, 560, 720, 900, 1100, 1350];
  const COIN_LAP = [40, 50, 70], COIN_CP = [8, 10, 14];
  const INF_CODE = 'infcoins123456789', MP_MAX_KMH = 350, MP_KMH = 300;
  const shop = { coins: 0, lvl: 0, inf: false };
  try {
    const rs = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('turbo_highway_shop') || 'null');
    if (rs && typeof rs === 'object') {
      if (typeof rs.coins === 'number' && isFinite(rs.coins) && rs.coins > 0) shop.coins = Math.floor(rs.coins);
      if (typeof rs.lvl === 'number' && isFinite(rs.lvl)) shop.lvl = Math.max(0, Math.min(MAX_LVL, Math.floor(rs.lvl)));
      shop.inf = rs.inf === true;
    }
  } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
  function saveShop() { try { window.localStorage.setItem('turbo_highway_shop', JSON.stringify(shop)); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ } }
  const kmhOfLvl = (l) => BASE_KMH + KMH_STEP * l;
  let topSpeed = BASE_KMH / KMH, nitroMult = NITRO_MULT, runCoins = 0;
  let codeEdit = false, codeText = '', shopMsg = { until: 0, s: '', ok: true };
  function addCoins(n) { if (mpOn) return 0; runCoins += n; if (!shop.inf) { shop.coins += n; saveShop(); } return n; }
  function sMsg(s, ok) { shopMsg = { until: tAnim + 4, s: s, ok: ok !== false }; }
  function buyUpgrade() {
    if (shop.lvl >= MAX_LVL) { sMsg('Ya tienes la velocidad al máximo', false); return; }
    const cost = UPG_COST[shop.lvl];
    if (!shop.inf && shop.coins < cost) { sMsg('Te faltan ' + (cost - shop.coins) + ' monedas', false); Sound.tone(180, 0.12, 0.12, 'square'); return; }
    if (!shop.inf) shop.coins -= cost;
    shop.lvl++; saveShop();
    sMsg('¡Mejora comprada! Velocidad máxima: ' + kmhOfLvl(shop.lvl) + ' km/h');
    Sound.tone(660, 0.1, 0.12, 'square', 990);
  }
  function redeemCode() {
    const c = codeText.trim().toLowerCase();
    codeEdit = false;
    if (!c) { sMsg('Escribe un código primero', false); return; }
    if (c === INF_CODE) {
      if (shop.inf) { sMsg('Ese código ya está activado', false); return; }
      shop.inf = true; saveShop(); codeText = '';
      sMsg('¡Código activado: monedas infinitas!'); Sound.tone(520, 0.12, 0.14, 'square', 1040);
    } else { sMsg('Código no válido', false); Sound.tone(180, 0.12, 0.12, 'square'); }
  }
  const MUSIC = {
    highway: { bpm: 118, roots: [45, 41, 48, 43], chords: [[57, 60, 64], [53, 57, 60], [55, 60, 64], [55, 59, 62]] },
    city: { bpm: 104, roots: [38, 34, 41, 36], chords: [[50, 53, 57], [50, 53, 58], [48, 53, 57], [48, 52, 55]] },
    desert: { bpm: 96, roots: [43, 40, 45, 38], chords: [[55, 58, 62], [52, 55, 59], [57, 60, 64], [50, 53, 57]] },
    f1: { bpm: 128, roots: [40, 36, 38, 35], chords: [[52, 55, 59], [52, 55, 60], [54, 57, 62], [54, 59, 62]] }
  };
  const mtof = (m) => 440 * Math.pow(2, (m - 69) / 12);
  const Sound = {
    ac: null, muted: false, master: null, o1: null, o2: null, f: null, g: null, windG: null, windF: null, noiseBuf: null, ns: null,
    init() {
      if (this.ac) return;
      try {
        const AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
        if (!AC) return;
        const ac = new AC();
        this.ac = ac;
        this.master = ac.createGain(); this.master.gain.value = this.muted ? 0 : 0.55 * cfg.vol; this.master.connect(ac.destination);
        this.o1 = ac.createOscillator(); this.o1.type = 'sawtooth';
        this.o2 = ac.createOscillator(); this.o2.type = 'square';
        this.f = ac.createBiquadFilter(); this.f.type = 'lowpass'; this.f.frequency.value = 500;
        this.g = ac.createGain(); this.g.gain.value = 0;
        this.o1.connect(this.f); this.o2.connect(this.f); this.f.connect(this.g); this.g.connect(this.master);
        this.o1.start(); this.o2.start();
        const len = Math.floor(ac.sampleRate);
        const buf = ac.createBuffer(1, len, ac.sampleRate);
        const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) d[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
        this.noiseBuf = buf;
        this.ns = ac.createBufferSource(); this.ns.buffer = buf; this.ns.loop = true;
        this.windF = ac.createBiquadFilter(); this.windF.type = 'bandpass'; this.windF.frequency.value = 700; this.windF.Q.value = 0.7;
        this.windG = ac.createGain(); this.windG.gain.value = 0;
        this.ns.connect(this.windF); this.windF.connect(this.windG); this.windG.connect(this.master);
        this.ns.start();
      } catch (e) { this.ac = null; }
    },
    resume() {
      try { if (this.ac && this.ac.state === 'suspended') { const p = this.ac.resume(); if (p && p.catch) p.catch(function () {}); } } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    },
    update(freq, vol, wind, windFreq) {
      if (!this.ac) return;
      try {
        const t = this.ac.currentTime;
        this.o1.frequency.setTargetAtTime(freq, t, 0.04);
        this.o2.frequency.setTargetAtTime(freq * 0.5, t, 0.04);
        this.f.frequency.setTargetAtTime(300 + freq * 4, t, 0.05);
        this.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(vol, t, 0.05);
        this.windG.gain.setTargetAtTime(wind, t, 0.1);
        this.windF.frequency.setTargetAtTime(windFreq, t, 0.1);
      } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    },
    noise(dur, vol, freq) {
      if (!this.ac || !this.noiseBuf) return;
      try {
        const ac = this.ac, t = ac.currentTime;
        const s = ac.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = this.noiseBuf;
        const fl = ac.createBiquadFilter(); fl.type = 'lowpass'; fl.frequency.value = freq;
        const g = ac.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
        s.connect(fl); fl.connect(g); g.connect(this.master);
        s.start(t); s.stop(t + dur + 0.05);
      } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    },
    tone(freq, dur, vol, type, slideTo, delay) {
      if (!this.ac) return;
      try {
        const ac = this.ac, t = ac.currentTime + (delay || 0);
        const o = ac.createOscillator(); o.type = type || 'square';
        o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t);
        if (slideTo) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(slideTo, t + dur);
        const g = ac.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
        o.connect(g); g.connect(this.master);
        o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.05);
      } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    },
    crash() { this.noise(0.55, 0.9, 900); this.tone(90, 0.3, 0.4, 'sawtooth', 40); },
    beep(hi) { this.tone(hi ? 880 : 440, hi ? 0.4 : 0.14, 0.22, 'square'); },
    checkpoint() { this.tone(660, 0.12, 0.2, 'square', 0, 0); this.tone(880, 0.12, 0.2, 'square', 0, 0.1); this.tone(1320, 0.25, 0.2, 'square', 0, 0.2); },
    bonus() { this.tone(900, 0.08, 0.15, 'triangle', 1400); },
    mg: null, mNext: 0, mStep: 0,
    mNote(type, freq, t, dur, vol, cutoff, slideTo) {
      const ac = this.ac, o = ac.createOscillator(), g = ac.createGain();
      o.type = type; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t);
      if (slideTo) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(slideTo, t + dur);
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol, t + 0.012); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
      if (cutoff) { const f = ac.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = 'lowpass'; f.frequency.value = cutoff; o.connect(f); f.connect(g); } else o.connect(g);
      g.connect(this.mg); o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.05);
    },
    mNoise(t, dur, vol, type, freq) {
      if (!this.noiseBuf) return;
      const ac = this.ac, s = ac.createBufferSource(), f = ac.createBiquadFilter(), g = ac.createGain();
      s.buffer = this.noiseBuf; f.type = type; f.frequency.value = freq;
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
      s.connect(f); f.connect(g); g.connect(this.mg); s.start(t, Math.random() * 0.5); s.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
    },
    mStepPlay(song, n, t, d) {
      const st = n % 16, bar = Math.floor(n / 16) % song.chords.length, ch = song.chords[bar], root = song.roots[bar];
      if (st % 4 === 0) this.mNote('sine', 120, t, 0.16, 0.2, 0, 42);
      if (st === 4 || st === 12) this.mNoise(t, 0.11, 0.05, 'bandpass', 1900);
      if (st % 2 === 1) this.mNoise(t, 0.04, 0.02, 'highpass', 7500);
      if (st === 0 || st === 3 || st === 6 || st === 8 || st === 11 || st === 14) this.mNote('sawtooth', mtof(root), t, d * 2.2, 0.1, 520);
      if (st % 2 === 0) {
        const idx = [0, 1, 2, 1, 0, 1, 2, 2][(n / 2 | 0) % 8];
        this.mNote('triangle', mtof(ch[idx] + 12), t, d * 1.8, 0.035, 2600);
      }
      if (st === 0) for (let k = 0; k < ch.length; k++) this.mNote('triangle', mtof(ch[k]), t, d * 15, 0.022, 1400);
    },
    musicTick(mapId, level) {
      if (!this.ac) return;
      try {
        const ac = this.ac;
        if (!this.mg) { this.mg = ac.createGain(); this.mg.gain.value = 0; this.mg.connect(this.master); this.mNext = ac.currentTime + 0.1; this.mStep = 0; }
        this.mg.gain.setTargetAtTime(level, ac.currentTime, 0.4);
        if (ac.state !== 'running') { this.mNext = ac.currentTime + 0.1; return; }
        const song = MUSIC[mapId] || MUSIC.highway, d = 60 / song.bpm / 4;
        if (this.mNext < ac.currentTime - 0.2) this.mNext = ac.currentTime + 0.05;
        let guard = 0;
        while (this.mNext < ac.currentTime + 0.3 && guard++ < 16) { this.mStepPlay(song, this.mStep, this.mNext, d); this.mNext += d; this.mStep++; }
      } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    },
    rainN: null,
    rainUpdate(vol) {
      if (!this.ac || !this.noiseBuf) return;
      try {
        const ac = this.ac, t = ac.currentTime;
        if (!this.rainN) {
          const s = ac.createBufferSource(), f = ac.createBiquadFilter(), f2 = ac.createBiquadFilter(), g = ac.createGain();
          s.buffer = this.noiseBuf; s.loop = true; f.type = 'highpass'; f.frequency.value = 1500; f2.type = 'lowpass'; f2.frequency.value = 7000; g.gain.value = 0;
          s.connect(f); f.connect(f2); f2.connect(g); g.connect(this.master); s.start(0, Math.random() * 0.5);
          this.rainN = { g: g };
        }
        this.rainN.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(vol, t, 0.3);
      } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    },
    sir: null,
    sirenUpdate(vol) {
      if (!this.ac) return;
      try {
        const ac = this.ac, t = ac.currentTime;
        if (!this.sir) { const o = ac.createOscillator(), g = ac.createGain(); o.type = 'triangle'; g.gain.value = 0; o.connect(g); g.connect(this.master); o.start(); this.sir = { o: o, g: g }; }
        this.sir.o.frequency.setTargetAtTime(780 + 240 * Math.sin(t * 5.4), t, 0.03);
        this.sir.g.gain.setTargetAtTime(vol, t, 0.12);
      } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    },
    horn: null,
    hornOn() {
      if (!this.ac || this.horn) return;
      try {
        const ac = this.ac, t = ac.currentTime;
        const o1 = ac.createOscillator(), o2 = ac.createOscillator(), g = ac.createGain(), fl = ac.createBiquadFilter();
        o1.type = 'sawtooth'; o1.frequency.value = 392; o2.type = 'square'; o2.frequency.value = 494;
        fl.type = 'lowpass'; fl.frequency.value = 1800;
        g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.2, t + 0.02);
        o1.connect(fl); o2.connect(fl); fl.connect(g); g.connect(this.master);
        o1.start(t); o2.start(t);
        this.horn = { o1: o1, o2: o2, g: g };
      } catch (e) { this.horn = null; }
    },
    hornOff() {
      const h = this.horn;
      if (!h) return;
      this.horn = null;
      try {
        const t = this.ac.currentTime;
        h.g.gain.cancelScheduledValues(t); h.g.gain.setValueAtTime(Math.max(0.0001, h.g.gain.value), t); h.g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.08);
        h.o1.stop(t + 0.1); h.o2.stop(t + 0.1);
      } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    },
    toggleMute() {
      this.muted = !this.muted;
      try { if (this.master) this.master.gain.value = this.muted ? 0 : 0.55 * cfg.vol; } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    },
    applyVol() { try { if (this.master) this.master.gain.value = this.muted ? 0 : 0.55 * cfg.vol; } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ } },
    destroy() {
      try { if (this.ac) { const p = this.ac.close(); if (p && p.catch) p.catch(function () {}); } } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
      this.horn = null; this.mg = null; this.sir = null; this.rainN = null; this.ac = null;
    }
  };

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  Pista
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  const ROAD = {
    LENGTH: { NONE: 0, SHORT: 25, MEDIUM: 50, LONG: 100 },
    HILL: { NONE: 0, LOW: 20, MEDIUM: 40, HIGH: 60 },
    CURVE: { NONE: 0, EASY: 2, MEDIUM: 4 }
  };
  let segments = [], trackLength = 0, mapPts = [], cpIdx = [];
  const startIdx = 6;

  function lastY() { return segments.length === 0 ? 0 : segments[segments.length - 1].p2.world.y; }
  function addSegment(curve, y) {
    const n = segments.length;
    segments.push({
      index: n,
      p1: { world: { y: lastY(), z: n * segmentLength }, camera: {}, screen: {} },
      p2: { world: { y: y, z: (n + 1) * segmentLength }, camera: {}, screen: {} },
      curve: curve, sprites: [], cars: [], clip: 0, fog: 1, looped: false,
      dark: Math.floor(n / rumbleLength) % 2 === 1, gantry: null
    });
  }
  function addRoad(enter, hold, leave, curve, y) {
    const startY = lastY(), endY = startY + (y || 0) * segmentLength, total = enter + hold + leave;
    let n;
    for (n = 0; n < enter; n++) addSegment(U.easeIn(0, curve, n / enter), U.easeInOut(startY, endY, n / total));
    for (n = 0; n < hold; n++) addSegment(curve, U.easeInOut(startY, endY, (enter + n) / total));
    for (n = 0; n < leave; n++) addSegment(U.easeInOut(curve, 0, n / leave), U.easeInOut(startY, endY, (enter + hold + n) / total));
  }
  const addStraight = (n) => { n = n || ROAD.LENGTH.MEDIUM; addRoad(n, n, n, 0, 0); };
  const addHill = (n, h) => { addRoad(n, n, n, 0, h); };
  const addCurve = (n, c, h) => { addRoad(n, n, n, c, h || 0); };
  function addLowRollingHills() {
    const n = ROAD.LENGTH.SHORT, h = ROAD.HILL.LOW, c = ROAD.CURVE.EASY;
    addRoad(n, n, n, 0, h / 2); addRoad(n, n, n, 0, -h); addRoad(n, n, n, c, h);
    addRoad(n, n, n, 0, 0); addRoad(n, n, n, -c, h / 2); addRoad(n, n, n, 0, 0);
  }
  function addSCurves() {
    const n = ROAD.LENGTH.MEDIUM, C = ROAD.CURVE, Hh = ROAD.HILL;
    addRoad(n, n, n, -C.EASY, Hh.NONE); addRoad(n, n, n, C.MEDIUM, Hh.MEDIUM); addRoad(n, n, n, C.EASY, -Hh.LOW);
    addRoad(n, n, n, -C.EASY, Hh.MEDIUM); addRoad(n, n, n, -C.MEDIUM, -Hh.MEDIUM);
  }
  function addBumps() {
    const n = 10;
    [5, -2, -5, 8, 5, -7, 5, -2].forEach(function (h) { addRoad(n, n, n, 0, h); });
  }
  function addDownhillToEnd(n) { addRoad(n, n, n, -ROAD.CURVE.EASY, -lastY() / segmentLength); }

  function addSprite(n, sp) { if (n >= 0 && n < segments.length) segments[n].sprites.push(sp); }
  function addTree(n, side, near) {
    const r = Math.random();
    let sp;
    if (r < 0.52) sp = { t: 'pine', w: 1.0, cw: 0.13, v: Math.random() };
    else if (r < 0.84) sp = { t: 'oak', w: 1.1, cw: 0.15, v: Math.random() };
    else sp = { t: 'bush', w: 0.6, cw: 0, v: Math.random() };
    sp.offset = side * (near + sp.w / 2);
    addSprite(n, sp);
  }
  function populateHighway() {
    const N = segments.length;
    let n, side;
    for (n = 8; n < N - 6; n += 3) {
      for (side = -1; side <= 1; side += 2) if (Math.random() < 0.78) addTree(n, side, 1.28 + Math.pow(Math.random(), 2) * 1.4);
    }
    for (n = 5; n < N - 6; n += 3) {
      for (side = -1; side <= 1; side += 2) if (Math.random() < 0.7) addTree(n + U.rndInt(0, 2), side, 2.8 + Math.random() * 7);
    }
    for (n = 10; n < N - 4; n += 7) {
      side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
      const r = Math.random();
      if (r < 0.45) addSprite(n, { t: 'bush', w: 0.55, cw: 0, v: Math.random(), offset: side * (1.25 + Math.random() * 1.6) });
      else if (r < 0.65) addSprite(n, { t: 'rock', w: 0.5, cw: 0.4, v: Math.random(), offset: side * (1.32 + Math.random() * 0.5) });
    }
    for (n = 2; n < N - 2; n += 12) {
      addSprite(n, { t: 'post', w: 0.07, cw: 0, v: 0, offset: -1.2 });
      addSprite(n, { t: 'post', w: 0.07, cw: 0, v: 0, offset: 1.2 });
    }
    for (n = 40; n < N - 40; n += U.rndInt(70, 130)) {
      side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
      const b = U.pick(BILLBOARDS);
      addSprite(n, { t: 'board', w: 1.3, cw: 0.2, v: 0, txt: b.txt, col: b.col, offset: side * (1.95 + Math.random() * 0.3) });
    }
  }
  // ---- Desierto: decorado ---------------------------------------------------
  const CACT_A = [58, 128, 78], CACT_B = [76, 150, 92], CACT_S = [40, 94, 60];
  const SAND_ROCK = [190, 120, 84], SAND_ROCK_S = [142, 82, 62];
  const DESERT_SIGNS = [
    { txt: 'MOTEL', col: [214, 72, 52] }, { txt: 'DINER', col: [236, 168, 40] }, { txt: 'GAS', col: [40, 110, 190] },
    { txt: 'ROUTE', col: [60, 60, 70] }, { txt: 'OASIS', col: [30, 150, 130] }, { txt: 'WEST', col: [150, 70, 40] }
  ];
  function populateDesert() {
    const N = segments.length;
    let n, side;
    // moles lejanas primero
    for (n = 14; n < N - 10; n += 17) {
      side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
      if (Math.random() < 0.7) { const w = U.rnd(3, 6); addSprite(n, { t: 'butte', w: w, cw: 0, v: Math.random(), offset: side * (7 + w / 2 + Math.random() * 12) }); }
    }
    for (n = 8; n < N - 6; n += 4) {
      for (side = -1; side <= 1; side += 2) {
        const r = Math.random();
        if (r < 0.34) addSprite(n + U.rndInt(0, 3), { t: 'cactus', w: 0.9 + Math.random() * 0.8, cw: 0.12, v: Math.random(), offset: side * (1.5 + Math.pow(Math.random(), 1.5) * 6) });
        else if (r < 0.5) addSprite(n + U.rndInt(0, 3), { t: 'scrub', w: 0.5 + Math.random() * 0.3, cw: 0, v: Math.random(), offset: side * (1.35 + Math.random() * 4) });
        else if (r < 0.58) addSprite(n + U.rndInt(0, 3), { t: 'rock', w: 0.5 + Math.random() * 0.5, cw: 0.4, v: Math.random(), sand: true, offset: side * (1.4 + Math.random() * 1.2) });
      }
    }
    for (n = 2; n < N - 2; n += 12) {
      addSprite(n, { t: 'post', w: 0.07, cw: 0, v: 0, offset: -1.2 });
      addSprite(n, { t: 'post', w: 0.07, cw: 0, v: 0, offset: 1.2 });
    }
    for (n = 50; n < N - 40; n += U.rndInt(90, 160)) {
      side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
      const b = U.pick(DESERT_SIGNS);
      addSprite(n, { t: 'board', w: 1.2, cw: 0.2, v: 0, txt: b.txt, col: b.col, offset: side * (1.9 + Math.random() * 0.3) });
    }
  }
  function buildMap() {
    const N = segments.length, pts = [];
    let h = 0, x = 0, y = 0, i;
    for (i = 0; i < N; i++) {
      h += segments[i].curve * 0.0017; x += Math.cos(h); y += Math.sin(h);
      if (i % MAP_STRIDE === 0) pts.push([x, y]);
    }
    const n = pts.length, ex = pts[n - 1][0] - pts[0][0], ey = pts[n - 1][1] - pts[0][1];
    for (i = 0; i < n; i++) { pts[i][0] -= ex * i / (n - 1); pts[i][1] -= ey * i / (n - 1); }
    let minx = Infinity, maxx = -Infinity, miny = Infinity, maxy = -Infinity;
    pts.forEach(function (p) { minx = Math.min(minx, p[0]); maxx = Math.max(maxx, p[0]); miny = Math.min(miny, p[1]); maxy = Math.max(maxy, p[1]); });
    const sz = Math.max(maxx - minx, maxy - miny, 1e-6), s = 1 / sz;
    const ox = (1 - (maxx - minx) * s) / 2, oy = (1 - (maxy - miny) * s) / 2;
    mapPts = pts.map(function (p) { return [(p[0] - minx) * s + ox, (p[1] - miny) * s + oy]; });
  }
  // ---- Neo Ciudad: decorado -------------------------------------------------
  const TOWER_COLS = [[26, 32, 62], [38, 28, 70], [20, 44, 72], [48, 38, 84], [30, 38, 84], [22, 26, 50]];
  const NEON_COLS = [[0, 240, 255], [255, 45, 180], [180, 255, 60], [255, 210, 63], [150, 90, 255]];
  const HOLO_TXT = ['NEO', 'CYBER', 'HYPER', 'AI-X', '2099', 'VOLT', 'ORBIT', 'NOVA'];
  function populateCity() {
    const N = segments.length;
    let n, side, i;
    // lo más lejano se añade primero para que se dibuje por debajo
    for (n = 4; n < N - 4; n += 5) {
      for (side = -1; side <= 1; side += 2) {
        if (Math.random() < 0.8) {
          const w = U.rnd(2, 4.2);
          addSprite(n + U.rndInt(0, 3), { t: 'tower', w: w, cw: 0, v: Math.random(), col: U.pick(TOWER_COLS), offset: side * (4.4 + w / 2 + Math.random() * 9) });
        }
      }
    }
    for (n = 6; n < N - 4; n += 4) {
      for (side = -1; side <= 1; side += 2) {
        if (Math.random() < 0.82) {
          const w = U.rnd(1.1, 2.2);
          addSprite(n + U.rndInt(0, 2), { t: 'tower', w: w, cw: 0.5, v: Math.random(), col: U.pick(TOWER_COLS), offset: side * (1.55 + w / 2 + Math.pow(Math.random(), 2) * 1.6) });
        }
      }
    }
    for (n = 25; n < N - 25; n += U.rndInt(45, 80)) {
      side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
      addSprite(n, { t: 'holo', w: 1.5, cw: 0, v: Math.random(), txt: U.pick(HOLO_TXT), col: U.pick(NEON_COLS), offset: side * (2.5 + Math.random() * 0.7) });
    }
    for (n = 3, i = 0; n < N - 2; n += 8, i++) {
      const col = NEON_COLS[i % 2 === 0 ? 0 : 1];
      addSprite(n, { t: 'npost', w: 0.07, cw: 0, v: 0, col: col, offset: -1.22 });
      addSprite(n, { t: 'npost', w: 0.07, cw: 0, v: 0, col: col, offset: 1.22 });
    }
    for (n = 30; n < N - 30; n += U.rndInt(55, 95)) {
      addSprite(n, { t: 'arch', w: 2.5, cw: 0, v: Math.random(), col: U.pick(NEON_COLS), offset: 0 });
    }
  }

  // ---- Gran Premio (F1): decorado -------------------------------------------
  const F1_BRANDS = [
    { txt: 'APEX', col: [214, 30, 40] }, { txt: 'VELOX', col: [20, 90, 200] }, { txt: 'PETRO', col: [250, 200, 20] },
    { txt: 'KINETIC', col: [30, 30, 36] }, { txt: 'ZENITH', col: [0, 150, 120] }, { txt: 'TURBO', col: [240, 110, 20] },
    { txt: 'ORBIT', col: [120, 60, 200] }, { txt: 'NOVA', col: [250, 250, 250] }
  ];
  function populateF1() {
    const N = segments.length;
    let n, side, i;
    for (n = 10; n < N - 10; n += 6) {
      side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
      if (Math.random() < 0.55) addTree(n, side, 7 + Math.random() * 9);
    }
    for (n = 12; n < N - 12; n += 14) {
      for (side = -1; side <= 1; side += 2) {
        const near = n < 300 || Math.random() < 0.5;
        if (near && Math.random() < 0.82) {
          const w = U.rnd(1.4, 2.0);
          addSprite(n, { t: 'stand', w: w, cw: 0.5, v: Math.random(), col: U.pick(F1_BRANDS).col, offset: side * (2.5 + w / 2) });
        }
      }
    }
    for (n = 20; n < N - 20; n += 26) {
      side = (n / 26 | 0) % 2 === 0 ? -1 : 1;
      addSprite(n, { t: 'lamp', w: 0.07, cw: 0, v: 0, offset: side * 2.1 });
    }
    for (n = 6, i = 0; n < N - 8; n += 9, i++) {
      const b = F1_BRANDS[i % F1_BRANDS.length];
      for (side = -1; side <= 1; side += 2) addSprite(n, { t: 'adboard', w: 0.84, cw: 0, v: 0, txt: b.txt, col: b.col, offset: side * 1.86 });
    }
    for (n = 4; n < N - 2; n += 3) {
      for (side = -1; side <= 1; side += 2) addSprite(n, { t: 'tires', w: 0.3, cw: 0.2, v: Math.random(), col: ((n / 3) | 0) % 6 < 3 ? [240, 240, 240] : [222, 36, 40], offset: side * 1.5 });
    }
    for (n = 60; n < N - 50; n += U.rndInt(130, 220)) {
      const b = U.pick(F1_BRANDS);
      addSprite(n, { t: 'arch', w: 2.5, cw: 0, v: Math.random(), f1: true, txt: b.txt, col: b.col, offset: 0 });
    }
  }

  // ---- Trazados --------------------------------------------------------------
  const rd = (n, curve, hill) => { addRoad(n, n, n, curve, hill || 0); };
  function layoutHighway() {
    const L = ROAD.LENGTH, Hh = ROAD.HILL, C = ROAD.CURVE;
    addStraight(L.MEDIUM);
    addHill(L.SHORT, Hh.LOW);
    addLowRollingHills();
    addCurve(L.MEDIUM, C.MEDIUM, Hh.LOW);
    addSCurves();
    addStraight(L.LONG);
    addBumps();
    addCurve(L.LONG, -C.EASY, Hh.MEDIUM);
    addCurve(L.MEDIUM, C.MEDIUM, -Hh.LOW);
    addHill(L.MEDIUM, Hh.HIGH);
    addCurve(L.MEDIUM, -C.MEDIUM, 0);
    addDownhillToEnd(200);
  }
  // Neo Ciudad: autopista elevada de alta velocidad con rampas, chicanes y grandes barridos
  function layoutCity() {
    const L = ROAD.LENGTH, Hh = ROAD.HILL, C = ROAD.CURVE;
    addStraight(L.MEDIUM);
    rd(35, -C.MEDIUM, Hh.MEDIUM);
    rd(50, 0, Hh.MEDIUM);
    rd(50, C.MEDIUM, -Hh.MEDIUM);
    rd(35, C.EASY, -Hh.LOW);
    addSCurves();
    addStraight(L.LONG);
    rd(25, -C.MEDIUM, 0); rd(25, C.MEDIUM, 0); rd(25, -C.MEDIUM, 0); rd(25, C.MEDIUM, 0);
    addStraight(L.MEDIUM);
    addHill(L.MEDIUM, Hh.HIGH);
    rd(50, -C.EASY, -Hh.MEDIUM);
    rd(40, C.MEDIUM, 0);
    addLowRollingHills();
    addDownhillToEnd(150);
  }
  // Desierto: rectas interminables, dunas onduladas y grandes curvas abiertas
  function layoutDesert() {
    const L = ROAD.LENGTH, Hh = ROAD.HILL, C = ROAD.CURVE;
    addStraight(L.LONG);
    addLowRollingHills();
    rd(60, C.EASY, Hh.LOW);
    rd(50, -C.EASY, 0);
    addStraight(L.MEDIUM);
    addHill(L.MEDIUM, Hh.HIGH);
    rd(70, -C.MEDIUM, -Hh.LOW);
    rd(40, C.MEDIUM, 0);
    addBumps();
    addStraight(L.LONG);
    rd(80, C.EASY, Hh.MEDIUM);
    rd(60, -C.MEDIUM, -Hh.MEDIUM);
    addSCurves();
    addLowRollingHills();
    rd(50, C.MEDIUM, Hh.LOW);
    addStraight(L.MEDIUM);
    addDownhillToEnd(160);
  }
  // Gran Premio: circuito técnico con rectas largas, horquillas, chicanes y eses
  function layoutF1() {
    const L = ROAD.LENGTH, Hh = ROAD.HILL, C = ROAD.CURVE;
    addStraight(L.LONG);
    addStraight(L.MEDIUM);
    rd(34, 5, 0);                       // horquilla 1
    addStraight(L.SHORT);
    rd(20, -3, 0); rd(20, 3, 0);        // chicane
    addStraight(L.MEDIUM);
    rd(40, -C.MEDIUM, Hh.LOW);          // curva rápida cuesta arriba
    rd(25, C.EASY, 0);
    rd(30, 5, -Hh.LOW);                 // horquilla 2 cuesta abajo
    addStraight(L.MEDIUM);
    rd(25, -3, 0); rd(25, 3, 0); rd(25, -3, 0); rd(25, 3, 0);   // eses
    rd(50, C.EASY, Hh.LOW);
    rd(50, -C.EASY, -Hh.LOW);
    addStraight(L.LONG);                // recta de atrás
    rd(36, -5, 0);                      // horquilla 3
    rd(30, C.EASY, 0);
    addStraight(L.SHORT);
    addDownhillToEnd(60);
  }

  // ---- Definición de los mapas ----------------------------------------------

  function buildTrack(map) {
    segments = [];
    map.layout();
    trackLength = segments.length * segmentLength;
    const N = segments.length;
    cpIdx = [startIdx, Math.floor(N * 0.25), Math.floor(N * 0.5), Math.floor(N * 0.75)];
    segments[cpIdx[0]].gantry = 'start';
    for (let i = 1; i < cpIdx.length; i++) segments[cpIdx[i]].gantry = 'check';
    map.populate();
    placeCoins();
    buildMap();
  }
  // Monedas giratorias sueltas por la carretera (+1 cada una)
  let coinList = [];
  function placeCoins() {
    coinList = [];
    const N = segments.length, lanes = [-0.55, 0, 0.55];
    for (let n = 36; n < N - 24; n += U.rndInt(120, 220)) {
      const lane = U.pick(lanes), wave = Math.random() < 0.4, cnt = U.rndInt(3, 5);
      for (let k = 0; k < cnt; k++) {
        const off = U.limit(lane + (wave ? Math.sin(k * 0.9) * 0.3 : 0), -0.85, 0.85);
        const sp = { t: 'coin', w: 0.2, cw: 0, v: 0, ph: k * 0.55, offset: off, got: false };
        addSprite(n + k * 4, sp); coinList.push(sp);
      }
    }
  }
  function resetCoins() { for (let i = 0; i < coinList.length; i++) coinList[i].got = false; }
  function findSegment(z) { return segments[Math.floor(z / segmentLength) % segments.length]; }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  Estado del juego

  let state = 'menu', prevState = 'menu';
  let position = 0, speed = 0, playerX = 0, playerY = 0;
  let time = 50, dist = 0, bonusScore = 0, lap = 1, nextCp = 1, nitro = 1, best = 0, newRecord = false, overtakes = 0;
  let steerVis = 0, spinT = 0, spinDir = 1, shake = 0, whiteFlash = 0, crashCd = 0, bgPx = 0, tAnim = 0;
  let cdTimer = 0, cdShown = 4, nitroOn = false, braking = false, playerPx = 200;
  let mapIdx = 0, skinIdx = 0, zoom = 0, camH = CAM_BASE_H, camD = cameraDepth * CAM_BASE_H / CAM_R, garageFrom = 'menu';
  const hits = [];
  let mouseX = -1, mouseY = -1;
  const over = (x, y, w, h) => mouseX >= x && mouseX <= x + w && mouseY >= y && mouseY <= y + h;
  let curMap = MAPS[0], mapFrom = 'menu';
  // Multijugador local (pantalla dividida)
  let mpOn = false, mpView = false, PL = [], curP = 0, gp = 0, fpv = false, mode = 'solo', lobbyFrom = 'menu';
  const FPV_H = 540, FPV_D = 250, FPV_ZOOM = 1.32;
  const MP_COLORS = [[47, 216, 255], [255, 157, 46]];

    KeyW: [0, 'up'], KeyS: [0, 'down'], KeyA: [0, 'left'], KeyD: [0, 'right'], Space: [0, 'nitro'], ShiftLeft: [0, 'nitro'],

  };
  const vpW = () => (mpOn && !mpView ? W / 2 : W);

  let particles = [], pops = [];
  const keysSP = { left: false, right: false, up: false, down: false, nitro: false };
  let keys = keysSP;
  const stars = [];
  const clouds = [];
  let i0;
  for (i0 = 0; i0 < 90; i0++) stars.push({ x: Math.random(), y: Math.random() * 0.46, r: U.rnd(0.6, 1.8), f: U.rnd(1, 4), p: U.rnd(0, TAU) });

  function saveBuilds() {

      b.plate = typeof r.plate === 'string' ? r.plate.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9 -]/g, '').slice(0, 8) : 'TURBO'; b.plateStyle = (r.plateStyle | 0) >= 0 && (r.plateStyle | 0) < PLATE_STYLES.length ? (r.plateStyle | 0) : 0;

  // Imagen propia (se reduce y se guarda en el navegador)
  let fileInput = null, pickFor = 0, gMsg = { until: 0, s: '' };
  function imgMsg(s) { gMsg = { until: tAnim + 4, s: s }; }
  function setImageFromURL(i, url, save) {
    if (typeof Image === 'undefined') return;
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload = function () {
      try {
        const iw = im.naturalWidth || im.width, ih = im.naturalHeight || im.height;
        if (!(iw > 0 && ih > 0)) throw new Error('vacía');
        const k = Math.min(1, 420 / Math.max(iw, ih)), w = Math.max(1, Math.round(iw * k)), h = Math.max(1, Math.round(ih * k));
        const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h;
        c.getContext('2d').drawImage(im, 0, 0, w, h);
        builds[i].img = c;
        if (save) {
          try {
            let d = c.toDataURL('image/png');
            if (d.length > 1500000) d = c.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.82);
            window.localStorage.setItem('turbo_highway_img' + i, d);
          } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
          imgMsg('¡Imagen cargada!');
        }
      } catch (e) { if (save) imgMsg('No se pudo usar esa imagen'); }
    };
    im.onerror = function () { if (save) imgMsg('Ese archivo no es una imagen válida'); };
    im.src = url;
  }
  function readImageFile(i, f) {
    try {
      if (!f || !/^image\//.test(f.type || '')) { imgMsg('Elige un archivo de imagen (PNG, JPG, GIF…)'); return; }
      const fr = new FileReader();
      fr.onload = function () { setImageFromURL(i, String(fr.result), true); };
      fr.onerror = function () { imgMsg('No se pudo leer el archivo'); };
      fr.readAsDataURL(f);
    } catch (e) { imgMsg('No se pudo leer el archivo'); }
  }
  function pickImage(i) {
    pickFor = i;
    try {
      if (!fileInput) {
        fileInput = document.createElement('input');
        fileInput.type = 'file'; fileInput.accept = 'image/*'; fileInput.style.display = 'none';
        fileInput.addEventListener('change', function () {
          const f = fileInput.files && fileInput.files[0];
          if (f) readImageFile(pickFor, f);
          try { fileInput.value = ''; } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
        });
        document.body.appendChild(fileInput);
      }
      fileInput.click();
    } catch (e) { imgMsg('No se pudo abrir el selector de archivos'); }
  }
  function clearImage(i) {
    builds[i].img = null;
    try { window.localStorage.removeItem('turbo_highway_img' + i); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
  }
  [0, 1].forEach(function (i) { try { window.localStorage.removeItem('turbo_highway_img' + i); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ } });

  function touch(i) { builds[i].custom = true; saveBuilds(); }
  function resetExtras(b) {

  }
  const bests = {};

  const score = () => Math.floor(dist) + bonusScore;

  function placeCars() {
    let i;
    for (i = 0; i < segments.length; i++) { segments[i].cars.length = 0; segments[i].skid = null; }
    cars = [];
    const N = segments.length;
    const tr = curMap.traffic, palette = tr.colors.map(hex);
    for (i = 0; i < tr.count; i++) {
      const idx = U.rndInt(30, N - 1);
      const type = U.pick(tr.types);
      const car = {
        offset: U.pick([-0.68, -0.02, 0.66]) + U.rnd(-0.1, 0.1),
        z: idx * segmentLength + U.rnd(0, segmentLength - 1),
        speed: maxSpeed * U.rnd(tr.vmin, tr.vmax), color: U.pick(palette), type: type, racing: !!tr.racing,
        w: type === 'van' ? 0.34 : 0.3, percent: 0, rels: [0, 0]
      };
      car.percent = U.percentRemaining(car.z, segmentLength);
      segments[idx].cars.push(car); cars.push(car);
    }
  }

  // ---- Persecución policial ----
  let chase = { t: 0, busted: 0, level: 1, cops: [], lvlT: 0, near: 0, acc: 0, caught: false };
  const isChase = () => mode === 'police' && !mpOn;
  function spawnCop(behind) {
    const z = U.increase(position + playerZ, -behind - U.rnd(0, 600), trackLength), seg = findSegment(z), n = chase.cops.length, side = n % 2 ? 1 : -1;
    const car = {
      police: true, skin: POLICE_SKIN, bias: side * U.rnd(0.2, 0.45), offset: U.limit(playerX + side * 0.4, -0.8, 0.8), z: z, speed: speed,
      color: [238, 240, 246], type: 'player', racing: false, w: 0.3, percent: U.percentRemaining(z, segmentLength), rels: [0, 0]
    };
    seg.cars.push(car); cars.push(car); chase.cops.push(car);
  }
  function chaseStart() {
    chase = { t: 0, busted: 0, level: 1, cops: [], lvlT: 0, near: 0, acc: 0, caught: false };
    spawnCop(8000); spawnCop(11000);
  }
  function copGap(car) {
    let g = car.z - (position + playerZ);
    g = ((g % trackLength) + trackLength) % trackLength;
    return g > trackLength / 2 ? g - trackLength : g;
  }
  function copAI(car, dt, seg, refs) {
    const D = DIFFS[diffIdx], cap = topSpeed * Math.min(D.copMax, D.copBase + D.copStep * chase.level);
    let g = copGap(car);
    if (g > -160) { car.z = U.increase(position + playerZ, -160, trackLength); g = -160; car.speed = Math.min(car.speed, speed); }
    else if (g < -14000) { car.z = U.increase(position + playerZ, -7000, trackLength); g = -7000; car.speed = speed; }
    const target = g < -700 ? cap : Math.min(cap, Math.max(speed * 1.03, speed + 300));
    car.speed += U.limit(target - car.speed, -dt * 14000, dt * 1600);
    const tx = U.limit(playerX + car.bias, -0.8, 0.8), step = dt * 0.75 * D.aggr;
    car.offset += U.limit(tx - car.offset, -step, step) + updateCarOffset(car, seg, refs) * 0.5;
    car.offset = U.limit(car.offset, -1.05, 1.05);
  }
  function chaseUpdate(dt) {
    const D = DIFFS[diffIdx], c = chase;
    c.t += dt; c.lvlT += dt;
    if (c.lvlT >= 18 && c.level < 6) { c.lvlT = 0; c.level++; showBanner('¡MÁS POLICÍAS!', 'Nivel de búsqueda ' + c.level, '#6fa8ff'); }
    const want = Math.min(D.copN, 1 + Math.ceil(c.level * 0.7));
    while (c.cops.length < want) spawnCop(7000);
    let rate = 0, near = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < c.cops.length; i++) {
      const g = copGap(c.cops[i]);
      if (g > -1500 && g < -100 && Math.abs(c.cops[i].offset - playerX) < 0.6) { near++; rate += (0.2 + 0.07 * c.level) * D.aggr * (0.4 + 0.6 * (1 - (-g - 100) / 1400)); }
    }
    c.near = near;
    c.busted = U.limit(c.busted + (near ? Math.min(rate, 1.6) : -0.16) * dt, 0, 1);
    c.acc += dt * 12 * c.level;
    if (c.acc >= 1) { const k = Math.floor(c.acc); bonusScore += k; c.acc -= k; }
    if (c.busted >= 1 && state === 'play') { c.caught = true; endGame(); }
  }
  const fmtT = (t) => Math.floor(t / 60) + ':' + ('0' + Math.floor(t % 60)).slice(-2);
  function startGame() {
    rollWeather();
    topSpeed = (mpOn ? MP_KMH : kmhOfLvl(shop.lvl)) / KMH; nitroMult = mpOn ? MP_MAX_KMH / MP_KMH : NITRO_MULT; runCoins = 0; resetCoins();
    if (mpOn) { startMP(); return; }
    keys = keysSP; curP = 0;
    placeCars();
    position = 0; speed = 0; playerX = 0; time = mode === 'police' ? 1e9 : DIFFS[diffIdx].start; dist = 0; bonusScore = 0; lap = 1; nextCp = 1; nitro = 1; overtakes = 0;
    spinT = 0; shake = 0; whiteFlash = 0; crashCd = 0; newRecord = false;
    particles.length = 0; pops.length = 0; banner = null;
    themeFrom = cur; themeTo = curMap.themes[0]; themeT = 0;
    resetKeysExceptNone();
    if (mode === 'police') chaseStart();
    state = 'countdown'; cdTimer = 3.6; cdShown = 4;
  }
  function resetKeysExceptNone() {
    const all = [keysSP, keys].concat(PL.map(function (p) { return p.keys; }));
    all.forEach(function (k) { k.left = k.right = k.up = k.down = k.nitro = false; });
  }
  // Nombre del piloto (solo en el modo individual; el multijugador no lo usa)
  let playerName = '', nameDraft = '', viewPref = 'third';
  try { if (window.localStorage.getItem('turbo_highway_view') === 'first') viewPref = 'first'; } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
  try { playerName = String(window.localStorage.getItem('turbo_highway_name') || '').slice(0, 12); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
  const DIFFS = [
    { id: 'easy', name: 'FÁCIL', col: [80, 220, 120], start: 50, cp: 22, cpMin: 12, copBase: 0.92, copStep: 0.02, copMax: 1.0, copN: 3, aggr: 0.75 },
    { id: 'medium', name: 'MEDIO', col: [255, 210, 63], start: 40, cp: 17, cpMin: 9, copBase: 0.97, copStep: 0.03, copMax: 1.08, copN: 4, aggr: 1 },
    { id: 'hard', name: 'DIFÍCIL', col: [255, 90, 90], start: 32, cp: 13, cpMin: 7, copBase: 1.0, copStep: 0.035, copMax: 1.16, copN: 5, aggr: 1.25 }
  ];
  let diffIdx = 1;
  try { const k = parseInt(window.localStorage.getItem('turbo_highway_diff'), 10); if (isFinite(k) && k >= 0 && k < DIFFS.length) diffIdx = k; } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
  function setDiff(k) { diffIdx = ((k % DIFFS.length) + DIFFS.length) % DIFFS.length; try { window.localStorage.setItem('turbo_highway_diff', String(diffIdx)); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ } }
  function changeDiff(d) { setDiff(diffIdx + d); Sound.tone(520, 0.06, 0.12, 'square'); }
  // ---- Clima ----
  const WEATHERS = [
    { id: 'clear', name: 'DESPEJADO', icon: 'sun', col: [255, 210, 63] },
    { id: 'rain', name: 'LLUVIA', icon: 'rain', col: [90, 170, 255] },
    { id: 'snow', name: 'NIEVE', icon: 'snow', col: [200, 235, 255] }
  ];
  let weatherIdx = 0;
  function rollWeather() {
    const r = Math.random();
    weatherIdx = r < cfg.rain ? 1 : r < cfg.rain + cfg.snow ? 2 : 0;
    WX.flash = 0; WX.nextFlash = 5 + Math.random() * 8; WX.splash.length = 0;
  }
  const WX = { drops: [], flakes: [], splash: [], flash: 0, nextFlash: 6, thunder: 0 };
  function weatherInit() {
    for (let i = 0; i < 170; i++) WX.drops.push({ x: Math.random(), y: Math.random(), z: Math.random(), gy: 0.62 + Math.random() * 0.38 });
    for (let i = 0; i < 140; i++) WX.flakes.push({ x: Math.random(), y: Math.random(), z: Math.random(), ph: Math.random() * TAU });
  }
  function weatherTick(dt) {
    const id = WEATHERS[weatherIdx].id;
    if (id === 'clear') { WX.flash = 0; WX.splash.length = 0; return; }
    if (!WX.drops.length) weatherInit();
    if (id === 'rain') {
      for (let i = 0; i < WX.drops.length; i++) {
        const d = WX.drops[i];
        d.y += dt * (1.6 + d.z * 1.3);
        if (d.y >= d.gy) {
          if (WX.splash.length < 50 && d.z > 0.35) WX.splash.push({ x: d.x, y: d.gy, t: 0 });
          d.y = -Math.random() * 0.25; d.x = Math.random(); d.gy = 0.62 + Math.random() * 0.38;
        }
      }
      for (let i = WX.splash.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { WX.splash[i].t += dt * 4.5; if (WX.splash[i].t >= 1) WX.splash.splice(i, 1); }
      WX.nextFlash -= dt;
      if (WX.nextFlash <= 0 && (state === 'play' || state === 'menu')) {
        WX.flash = 1; WX.nextFlash = 9 + Math.random() * 14;
        WX.thunder = 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.8;
      }
      WX.flash = Math.max(0, WX.flash - dt * 2.4);
      if (WX.thunder > 0) { WX.thunder -= dt; if (WX.thunder <= 0) Sound.noise(1.8, 0.28, 220); }
    } else {
      for (let i = 0; i < WX.flakes.length; i++) {
        const f = WX.flakes[i];
        f.ph += dt * (0.9 + f.z); f.y += dt * (0.1 + f.z * 0.17); f.x += Math.sin(f.ph) * dt * 0.035 - dt * 0.012;
        if (f.y > 1.03) { f.y = -0.03; f.x = Math.random(); }
        if (f.x < -0.03) f.x += 1.06;
      }
    }
  }
  function drawWeather() {
    const id = WEATHERS[weatherIdx].id;
    if (id === 'clear' || !cfg.weather) return;
    const ui = Math.max(0.85, Math.min(W / 1280, H / 720)), hy = H * 0.45;
    ctx.save();
    if (id === 'rain') {
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(28,42,68,0.27)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      const fg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, hy - H * 0.16, 0, hy + H * 0.12);
      fg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(150,170,195,0)'); fg.addColorStop(0.55, 'rgba(150,170,195,0.24)'); fg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(150,170,195,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle = fg; ctx.fillRect(0, hy - H * 0.16, W, H * 0.28);
      const sp = U.limit(speed / maxSpeed, 0, 1.2), slant = 0.1 + 0.32 * sp, n = Math.min(WX.drops.length, Math.ceil(WX.drops.length * U.limit(0.45 + W / 1280 * 0.55, 0.5, 1)));
      ctx.lineCap = 'round';
      for (let pass = 0; pass < 2; pass++) {
        ctx.strokeStyle = pass ? 'rgba(215,232,255,0.55)' : 'rgba(190,210,240,0.28)'; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, (pass ? 1.5 : 1) * ui);
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
          const d = WX.drops[i];
          if ((d.z > 0.5) !== (pass === 1)) continue;
          const x = d.x * W, y = d.y * H, len = (12 + 26 * d.z) * ui;
          ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.lineTo(x - len * slant, y + len);
        }
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, ui);
      for (let i = 0; i < WX.splash.length; i++) {
        const sp2 = WX.splash[i], gy = sp2.y * H;
        if (gy < H * 0.6) continue;
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(225,238,255,' + ((1 - sp2.t) * 0.5).toFixed(3) + ')';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(sp2.x * W, gy, (2 + 9 * sp2.t) * ui * sp2.y, (0.8 + 2.4 * sp2.t) * ui * sp2.y, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
      }
      if (WX.flash > 0.01) { ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(235,240,255,' + (0.5 * WX.flash).toFixed(3) + ')'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
    } else {
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(215,228,250,0.1)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      const fg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, hy - H * 0.2, 0, hy + H * 0.16);
      fg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(235,242,255,0)'); fg.addColorStop(0.55, 'rgba(235,242,255,0.34)'); fg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(235,242,255,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle = fg; ctx.fillRect(0, hy - H * 0.2, W, H * 0.36);
      const n = Math.min(WX.flakes.length, Math.ceil(WX.flakes.length * U.limit(0.45 + W / 1280 * 0.55, 0.5, 1)));
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const f = WX.flakes[i], r = (1 + 2.8 * f.z) * ui;
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + (0.5 + 0.45 * f.z).toFixed(2) + ')';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(f.x * W, f.y * H, r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      }
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function openModes() { if (state === 'menu') state = 'modes'; }
  function closeModes() { if (state === 'modes') state = 'menu'; }
  let pendMode = 'solo';
  function pickMode(m) { if (state !== 'modes') return; pendMode = m; state = 'diff'; }
  function closeDiff() { if (state === 'diff') state = 'modes'; }
  function confirmDiff() {
    if (state !== 'diff') return;
    setDiff(diffIdx);
    if (pendMode === 'duo') { lobbyFrom = 'modes'; mode = 'solo'; state = 'lobby'; return; }
    mode = pendMode; loadBest();
    mpOn = false; keys = keysSP; curP = 0;
    nameDraft = playerName; state = 'name';
  }
  function confirmName() {
    if (state !== 'name') return;
    playerName = nameDraft.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 12);
    try { window.localStorage.setItem('turbo_highway_name', playerName); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    try { window.localStorage.setItem('turbo_highway_view', viewPref); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    setDiff(diffIdx);
    fpv = viewPref === 'first';
    mpOn = false; keys = keysSP; curP = 0; startGame();
  }
  function cancelName() { if (state === 'name') state = 'diff'; }
  // Nombres del multijugador (uno por coche, se eligen en el lobby)
  let mpNames = ['', ''], nameEdit = -1;
  try { const r = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('turbo_highway_mpnames') || 'null'); if (Array.isArray(r)) mpNames = [String(r[0] || '').slice(0, 12), String(r[1] || '').slice(0, 12)]; } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
  function saveMpNames() { try { window.localStorage.setItem('turbo_highway_mpnames', JSON.stringify(mpNames)); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ } }
  function endNameEdit() { nameEdit = -1; mpNames = mpNames.map(function (n) { return n.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); }); saveMpNames(); }
  function drawTag(text, x, y, fs) {
    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = 0.9; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
    ctx.font = 'bold ' + fs + 'px "Segoe UI","Helvetica Neue",Arial,sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
    text = tr(text);
    ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(2, fs * 0.28); ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)'; ctx.strokeText(text, x, y);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.fillText(text, x, y);
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function openLobby() {
    if (state !== 'menu') return;
    lobbyFrom = 'menu'; mode = 'solo'; state = 'lobby';
  }
  function closeLobby() { if (state === 'lobby') { endNameEdit(); state = lobbyFrom === 'modes' ? 'diff' : 'menu'; lobbyFrom = 'menu'; } }
  function lobbyStart() { if (state === 'lobby') { endNameEdit(); mpOn = true; startGame(); } }
  function changeMPSkin(i, d) { applyPreset(i, builds[i].preset + d); Sound.tone(520, 0.06, 0.12, 'square'); }
  function goMenu() {
    if (state !== 'pause' && state !== 'over' && state !== 'play' && state !== 'countdown') return;
    if (!mpOn && score() > best) { best = score(); saveBest(); }
    resetKeysExceptNone();
    mpOn = false; keys = keysSP; curP = 0; mpView = false; weatherIdx = 0;
    particles = []; pops = []; banner = null; zoom = 0;
    shake = 0; whiteFlash = 0; spinT = 0; crashCd = 0; nitroOn = false; braking = false;
    themeFrom = cur; themeTo = curMap.themes[curMap.menuTheme]; themeT = 0;
    placeCars();
    state = 'menu';
  }
  function openGarage() { if (mpOn) return; if (state === 'menu' || state === 'over') { garageFrom = state; gp = 0; gYaw = 0.25; gHold = 0; state = 'garage'; } }
  function editBuild(i) { if (state === 'lobby') { garageFrom = 'lobby'; gp = i; gYaw = 0.25; gHold = 0; state = 'garage'; } }
  function closeGarage() { if (state === 'garage') { plateEdit = false; state = garageFrom; skinIdx = builds[0].preset; saveSkin(); saveBuilds(); } }
  function selectMap(i, quiet) {
    i = ((i % MAPS.length) + MAPS.length) % MAPS.length;
    mapIdx = i; curMap = MAPS[i]; saveMap();
    buildTrack(curMap); placeCars(); loadBest();
    position = 0; playerX = 0;
    particles.length = 0;
    themeFrom = themeTo = cur = curMap.themes[curMap.menuTheme]; themeT = 1;
    if (!quiet) Sound.tone(440, 0.08, 0.12, 'square', 660);
  }
  function openSettings() { if (state === 'menu') state = 'settings'; }
  function closeSettings() { if (state === 'settings') { if (nameEditS) saveName(); state = 'menu'; saveCfg(); } }
  function openShop() { if (state === 'menu') { state = 'shop'; codeEdit = false; } }
  function closeShop() { if (state === 'shop') { codeEdit = false; state = 'menu'; } }
  function openControls() { if (state === 'menu') state = 'controls'; }
  function closeControls() { if (state === 'controls') state = 'menu'; }
  function quitGame() { destroy(); }
  function toggleSound() { Sound.init(); Sound.resume(); Sound.toggleMute(); }
  function openMaps() { if (mpOn) return; if (state === 'menu' || state === 'over') { mapFrom = state; state = 'maps'; } }
  function closeMaps() { if (state === 'maps') { state = mapFrom; saveMap(); } }
  function changeMap(d) { selectMap(mapIdx + d); }
  function changeSkin(d) { applyPreset(gp, builds[gp].preset + d); Sound.tone(520, 0.06, 0.12, 'square'); }
  function endGame() {
    state = 'over';
    const s = score();
    if (s > best) { best = s; newRecord = true; saveBest(); } else newRecord = false;
    Sound.beep(false);
  }
  function pauseGame() { if (state === 'play' || state === 'countdown') { Sound.hornOff(); prevState = state; state = 'pause'; resetKeysExceptNone(); } }
  function resumeGame() { if (state === 'pause') state = prevState; }
  function showBanner(text, sub, color) { banner = { text: text, sub: sub || '', color: color || '#fff', t: 0, dur: 1.5 }; }
  function pop(text, color) { pops.push({ t: text, c: color || '#fff', life: 1.3, y: 0 }); if (pops.length > 6) pops.shift(); }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  Lógica
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  function updateCarOffset(car, carSeg, refs) {
    const lookahead = 20, carW = car.w;
    let close = false;
    for (let r = 0; r < refs.length; r++) if ((carSeg.index - refs[r].index) <= drawDistance) { close = true; break; }
    if (!close) return 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < lookahead; i++) {
      const seg = segments[(carSeg.index + i) % segments.length];
      let dir;
      if (!mpOn && seg === refs[0] && car.speed > speed && U.overlap(playerX, playerW, car.offset, carW, 1.2)) {
        if (playerX > 0.5) dir = -1; else if (playerX < -0.5) dir = 1; else dir = (car.offset > playerX) ? 1 : -1;
        return dir / i * (car.speed - speed) / maxSpeed;
      }
      for (let j = 0; j < seg.cars.length; j++) {
        const other = seg.cars[j];
        if (car.speed > other.speed && U.overlap(car.offset, carW, other.offset, other.w, 1.2)) {
          if (car.offset > 0.5) dir = -1; else if (car.offset < -0.5) dir = 1; else dir = (car.offset > other.offset) ? 1 : -1;
          return dir / i * (car.speed - other.speed) / maxSpeed;
        }
      }
    }
    if (car.offset < -0.9) return 0.1;
    if (car.offset > 0.9) return -0.1;
    return 0;
  }
  function moveCars(dt, playerSeg) {
    const refs = mpOn && PL.length === 2
      ? [findSegment(PL[0].position + playerZ), findSegment(PL[1].position + playerZ)]
      : [playerSeg];
    for (let i = 0; i < cars.length; i++) {
      const car = cars[i];
      const oldSeg = findSegment(car.z);
      if (car.police) copAI(car, dt, oldSeg, refs); else car.offset += updateCarOffset(car, oldSeg, refs);
      car.z = U.increase(car.z, dt * car.speed, trackLength);
      car.percent = U.percentRemaining(car.z, segmentLength);
      const newSeg = findSegment(car.z);
      if (oldSeg !== newSeg) {
        const k = oldSeg.cars.indexOf(car);
        if (k >= 0) oldSeg.cars.splice(k, 1);
        newSeg.cars.push(car);
      }
    }
  }
  function checkOvertakes() {
    if (state !== 'play') return;
    for (let i = 0; i < cars.length; i++) {
      const car = cars[i];
      if (car.police) continue;
      let rel = car.z - (position + playerZ);
      rel = ((rel % trackLength) + trackLength) % trackLength;
      if (rel > trackLength / 2) rel -= trackLength;
      if (car.rels[curP] > 0 && rel <= 0 && car.rels[curP] < segmentLength * 40) onOvertake(car);
      car.rels[curP] = rel;
    }
  }
  function onOvertake(car) {
    const d = Math.abs(car.offset - playerX);
    overtakes++;
    if (d < 0.5) { bonusScore += 150; nitro = Math.min(1, nitro + 0.1); Sound.bonus(); }
    else if (d < 1.1) { bonusScore += 50; }
  }
  function spawnParticle(x, y, vx, vy, life, r, c, g, grow) {
    if (particles.length > 220) particles.shift();
    particles.push({ x: x, y: y, vx: vx, vy: vy, life: life, max: life, r: r, c: c, g: g || 0, grow: grow || 0 });
  }
  function onCrash(sev, pushX, kind) {
    playerX += pushX;
    if (crashCd > 0) return;
    crashCd = 0.5;
    shake = Math.max(shake, 10 + sev * 26);
    whiteFlash = Math.min(0.6, 0.15 + sev * 0.5);
    Sound.crash();
    for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
      const a = U.rnd(Math.PI, TAU), v = U.rnd(120, 520) * (0.4 + sev);
      spawnParticle(vpW() / 2 + U.rnd(-playerPx * 0.3, playerPx * 0.3), gy() - H * 0.02, Math.cos(a) * v, Math.sin(a) * v, U.rnd(0.4, 0.9), U.rnd(2, 5),
        Math.random() < 0.5 ? [255, 190, 60] : [255, 120, 40], 600, 0);
    }
    for (let k = 0; k < 10; k++) {
      spawnParticle(vpW() / 2 + U.rnd(-playerPx * 0.4, playerPx * 0.4), gy() - H * 0.04, U.rnd(-60, 60), U.rnd(-160, -50), U.rnd(0.8, 1.5), U.rnd(14, 30), [150, 150, 158], -20, 1.5);
    }
    if (kind === 'car' && sev > 0.25) { spinT = 0.9; spinDir = pushX >= 0 ? 1 : -1; }
  }
  function checkCheckpoints(prevIdx, newIdx) {
    const N = segments.length;
    const delta = (newIdx - prevIdx + N) % N;
    if (delta === 0 || delta > N / 2) return;
    const off = (cpIdx[nextCp] - prevIdx + N) % N;
    if (off > 0 && off <= delta) {
      if (nextCp === 0) {
        lap++;
        bonusScore += 500;
        const cL = addCoins(COIN_LAP[diffIdx]);
        resetCoins();
        themeFrom = cur; themeTo = curMap.themes[(lap - 1) % curMap.themes.length]; themeT = 0;
        const bonus = Math.max(DIFFS[diffIdx].cpMin, DIFFS[diffIdx].cp - lap);
        if (mode !== 'police' || mpOn) time += bonus;
        showBanner('VUELTA ' + lap, (mode === 'police' && !mpOn ? '' : '+' + bonus + ' s   ·   ') + '+500 pts' + (cL ? '   ·   +' + cL + ' monedas' : ''), '#ffd23f');
      } else {
        const bonus = Math.max(DIFFS[diffIdx].cpMin, DIFFS[diffIdx].cp - lap);
        if (mode !== 'police' || mpOn) time += bonus;
        bonusScore += 200;
        const cC = addCoins(COIN_CP[diffIdx]);
        showBanner('CHECKPOINT', (mode === 'police' && !mpOn ? '+200 pts' : '+' + bonus + ' segundos') + (cC ? '   ·   +' + cC + ' monedas' : ''), '#7dff6a');
      }
      Sound.checkpoint();
      nextCp = (nextCp + 1) % cpIdx.length;
    }
  }
  function update(dt, moveTraffic) {
    const ps = findSegment(position + playerZ);
    const accel = topSpeed / 5, curveK = Math.sqrt(topSpeed / maxSpeed);
    const speedPercent = speed / topSpeed;
    const sp1 = Math.min(speedPercent, 1);
    const dx = dt * 2 * sp1;
    const prevIdx = ps.index;

    if (moveTraffic !== false) moveCars(dt, ps);
    checkOvertakes();
    if (isChase()) chaseUpdate(dt);
    position = U.increase(position, dt * speed, trackLength);
    bgPx += ps.curve * sp1 * dt * vpW() * 0.05;

    if (keys.left) playerX -= dx; else if (keys.right) playerX += dx;
    playerX -= dx * speedPercent * ps.curve * centrifugal * curveK;

    nitroOn = keys.nitro && nitro > 0.005;
    braking = keys.down && speed > 0 && !nitroOn;
    if (nitroOn) {
      speed += accel * 2.4 * dt;
      nitro = Math.max(0, nitro - dt * 0.26);
    } else {
      if (keys.up) speed += accel * dt; else if (keys.down) speed += breaking * dt; else speed += decel * dt;
      nitro = Math.min(1, nitro + dt * (keys.up ? 0.045 : 0.02));
    }
    if (!nitroOn && speed > topSpeed) speed = Math.max(topSpeed, speed - accel * 1.6 * dt);
    if (nitroOn) speed = Math.min(speed, topSpeed * nitroMult);

    const off = playerX < -1 || playerX > 1;
    const cs = [ps];
    { const nSeg = segments.length, nw = findSegment(position + playerZ).index; let ns = (nw - ps.index + nSeg) % nSeg; if (ns > 6) ns = 0; for (let k = 1; k <= ns; k++) cs.push(segments[(ps.index + k) % nSeg]); }
    if (off) {
      if (speed > offRoadLimit) speed += offRoadDecel * dt;
      shake = Math.max(shake, 2 + 5 * sp1);
      if (speed > 800 && Math.random() < 0.8) {
        const side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
        spawnParticle(vpW() / 2 + side * playerPx * 0.36, gy(), U.rnd(-90, 90), U.rnd(-200, -60), U.rnd(0.4, 0.8), U.rnd(10, 22),
          mixc(cur.grassL, [150, 120, 80], 0.6), -40, 1.4);
      }
      outer: for (let k = 0; k < cs.length; k++) {
        const sg = cs[k];
        for (let i = 0; i < sg.sprites.length; i++) {
          const s = sg.sprites[i];
          if (s.cw > 0 && U.overlap(playerX, playerW, s.offset, s.cw, 1)) {
            const sev = Math.min(1, speed / maxSpeed);
            speed = Math.min(speed, maxSpeed * 0.2);
            position = U.increase(sg.p1.world.z, -playerZ, trackLength);
            onCrash(sev, s.offset > 0 ? -0.3 : 0.3, 'tree');
            break outer;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    carsLoop: for (let k = 0; k < cs.length; k++) {
      const cl = cs[k].cars;
      for (let i = 0; i < cl.length; i++) {
        const car = cl[i];
        if ((car.pidx === curP && car.isPlayer) || car.police) continue;
        if (speed > car.speed && U.overlap(playerX, playerW, car.offset, car.w, 0.85)) {
          const sev = (speed - car.speed) / maxSpeed;
          speed = car.speed * (car.speed / speed);
          position = U.increase(car.z, -playerZ, trackLength);
          onCrash(sev, playerX > car.offset ? 0.14 : -0.14, 'car');
          break carsLoop;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!mpOn) {
      for (let k = 0; k < cs.length; k++) {
        const sl = cs[k].sprites;
        for (let i = 0; i < sl.length; i++) {
          const co = sl[i];
          if (co.t === 'coin' && !co.got && U.overlap(playerX, playerW, co.offset, 0.34, 1)) {
            co.got = true; addCoins(1); pop('+1', '#ffd23f'); Sound.tone(1100, 0.07, 0.1, 'square', 1700);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    if (nitroOn && Math.random() < 0.7) {
      const side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
      spawnParticle(vpW() / 2 + side * playerPx * 0.3, gy() + H * 0.005, U.rnd(-30, 30), U.rnd(80, 200), U.rnd(0.15, 0.3), U.rnd(3, 6), [255, 170, 60], 0, 0.5);
    }
    if (braking && speed > maxSpeed * 0.4 && Math.random() < 0.5) {
      spawnParticle(vpW() / 2 + (Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1) * playerPx * 0.38, gy() + H * 0.01, U.rnd(-40, 40), U.rnd(-60, -10), U.rnd(0.3, 0.6), U.rnd(8, 16), [190, 190, 200], -10, 1.2);
    }

    // marcas de frenado: al frenar fuerte, derrapar o girar a tope a gran velocidad
    if (cfg.skid && !off && ((braking && speed > maxSpeed * 0.35) || (spinT > 0 && speed > maxSpeed * 0.15) || (sp1 > 0.95 && Math.abs(steerVis) > 0.6 && Math.abs(ps.curve) > 1.5))) {
      const arr = ps.skid || (ps.skid = []);
      arr.push({ o: playerX - 0.1, t: tAnim }, { o: playerX + 0.1, t: tAnim });
      if (arr.length > 8) arr.splice(0, arr.length - 8);
    }

    playerX = U.limit(playerX, -3, 3);
    speed = U.limit(speed, 0, topSpeed * (nitroMult + 0.01));

    checkCheckpoints(prevIdx, findSegment(position + playerZ).index);
    dist += speed * dt * 0.00625;
    if (mpOn || mode !== 'police') {
      time -= dt;
      if (time <= 0) { time = 0; if (mpOn) PL[curP].out = true; else endGame(); }
    }
  }
  function attract(dt) {
    speed = Math.min(speed + accel * dt, maxSpeed * 0.55);
    const ps = findSegment(position + playerZ);
    position = U.increase(position, dt * speed, trackLength);
    playerX = Math.sin(tAnim * 0.5) * 0.5;
    bgPx += ps.curve * 0.55 * dt * vpW() * 0.05;
  }
  function countdown(dt) {
    cdTimer -= dt;
    const n = Math.ceil(cdTimer - 0.6);
    if (n !== cdShown && n >= 1) { cdShown = n; Sound.beep(false); }
    if (cdTimer <= 0.6) { state = 'play'; if (isChase()) showBanner('¡HUYE!', 'La policía te persigue', '#6fa8ff'); else showBanner('¡YA!', '', '#7dff6a'); Sound.beep(true); }
  }
  function overUpdate(dt) {
    const ps = findSegment(position + playerZ);
    speed = Math.max(0, speed - maxSpeed * 0.45 * dt);
    moveCars(dt, ps);
    position = U.increase(position, dt * speed, trackLength);
    bgPx += ps.curve * Math.min(speed / maxSpeed, 1) * dt * vpW() * 0.05;
    nitroOn = false; braking = false;
  }
  function updateCamera(dt) {
    let target = 0;
    if (state === 'play' || state === 'countdown') {
      if (keys.up || keys.nitro) target += 1;
      if (keys.down) target -= 1;
    }
    zoom += (target - zoom) * Math.min(1, dt * 2.5);
    camH = CAM_BASE_H + CAM_RANGE * zoom;
    camD = cameraDepth * camH / CAM_R;
    if (fpv && state !== 'menu' && state !== 'garage' && state !== 'maps' && state !== 'controls' && state !== 'lobby' && state !== 'name' && state !== 'modes' && state !== 'diff' && state !== 'settings' && state !== 'shop') { camH = FPV_H; camD = FPV_D; }
  }
  function tickPlayerFx(dt) {
    shake *= 0.9; if (shake < 0.25) shake = 0;
    whiteFlash = Math.max(0, whiteFlash - dt * 1.6);
    crashCd = Math.max(0, crashCd - dt);
    if (spinT > 0) spinT = Math.max(0, spinT - dt);
    if (themeT < 1) themeT = Math.min(1, themeT + dt / 4);
    steerVis += (((keys.right ? 1 : 0) - (keys.left ? 1 : 0)) - steerVis) * 0.18;
    updateCamera(dt);
    if (banner) { banner.t += dt; if (banner.t >= banner.dur) banner = null; }
    for (let i = pops.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { pops[i].life -= dt; pops[i].y -= dt * 40; if (pops[i].life <= 0) pops.splice(i, 1); }
    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const p = particles[i];
      p.life -= dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vy += p.g * dt;
      if (p.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
    }
  }
  // ---- Multijugador local: estado separado por jugador ----
  function makePlayer(i) {
    const sk = builds[i];
    return {
      position: 0, speed: 0, playerX: i === 0 ? -0.42 : 0.42, playerY: 0, time: DIFFS[diffIdx].start, dist: 0, bonusScore: 0, lap: 1, nextCp: 1, nitro: 1, overtakes: 0,
      steerVis: 0, spinT: 0, spinDir: 1, shake: 0, whiteFlash: 0, crashCd: 0, bgPx: 0, nitroOn: false, braking: false, playerPx: 200,
      fpv: false, zoom: 0, camH: CAM_BASE_H, camD: cameraDepth * CAM_BASE_H / CAM_R, banner: null, pops: [], particles: [],
      themeFrom: cur, themeTo: curMap.themes[0], themeT: 0, cur: cur,
      keys: { left: false, right: false, up: false, down: false, nitro: false },
      skin: i, out: false,
      rival: { pidx: i, isPlayer: true, offset: 0, z: playerZ, speed: 0, color: sk.color, type: sk.type || 'player', w: 0.3, percent: 0, skin: sk, seg: null }
    };
  }
  function storeP(i) {
    const p = PL[i];
    p.position = position; p.speed = speed; p.playerX = playerX; p.playerY = playerY; p.time = time; p.dist = dist; p.bonusScore = bonusScore;
    p.lap = lap; p.nextCp = nextCp; p.nitro = nitro; p.overtakes = overtakes; p.steerVis = steerVis; p.spinT = spinT; p.spinDir = spinDir;
    p.shake = shake; p.whiteFlash = whiteFlash; p.crashCd = crashCd; p.bgPx = bgPx; p.nitroOn = nitroOn; p.braking = braking; p.playerPx = playerPx;
    p.fpv = fpv; p.zoom = zoom; p.camH = camH; p.camD = camD; p.banner = banner; p.pops = pops; p.particles = particles;
    p.themeFrom = themeFrom; p.themeTo = themeTo; p.themeT = themeT; p.cur = cur; p.keys = keys;
  }
  function loadP(i) {
    const p = PL[i];
    position = p.position; speed = p.speed; playerX = p.playerX; playerY = p.playerY; time = p.time; dist = p.dist; bonusScore = p.bonusScore;
    lap = p.lap; nextCp = p.nextCp; nitro = p.nitro; overtakes = p.overtakes; steerVis = p.steerVis; spinT = p.spinT; spinDir = p.spinDir;
    shake = p.shake; whiteFlash = p.whiteFlash; crashCd = p.crashCd; bgPx = p.bgPx; nitroOn = p.nitroOn; braking = p.braking; playerPx = p.playerPx;
    fpv = p.fpv; zoom = p.zoom; camH = p.camH; camD = p.camD; banner = p.banner; pops = p.pops; particles = p.particles;
    themeFrom = p.themeFrom; themeTo = p.themeTo; themeT = p.themeT; cur = p.cur; keys = p.keys;
    curP = i;
  }
  function syncRivals() {
    for (let i = 0; i < PL.length; i++) {
      const p = PL[i], r = p.rival;
      r.z = U.increase(p.position, playerZ, trackLength); r.offset = p.playerX; r.speed = p.speed;
      r.percent = U.percentRemaining(r.z, segmentLength);
      const sg = findSegment(r.z);
      if (r.seg !== sg) {
        if (r.seg) { const k = r.seg.cars.indexOf(r); if (k >= 0) r.seg.cars.splice(k, 1); }
        sg.cars.push(r); r.seg = sg;
      }
    }
  }
  function startMP() {
    placeCars();
    mpOn = true; curP = 0;
    PL = [makePlayer(0), makePlayer(1)];
    loadP(0);
    syncRivals();
    resetKeysExceptNone();
    state = 'countdown'; cdTimer = 3.6; cdShown = 4;
  }
  function overUpdateP(dt) {
    const ps = findSegment(position + playerZ);
    speed = Math.max(0, speed - maxSpeed * 0.45 * dt);
    position = U.increase(position, dt * speed, trackLength);
    bgPx += ps.curve * Math.min(speed / maxSpeed, 1) * dt * vpW() * 0.05;
    nitroOn = false; braking = false;
  }
  function endMP() { state = 'over'; resetKeysExceptNone(); Sound.beep(false); }
  function tickMP(dt) {
    const was = state;
    if (state === 'play' || state === 'over') moveCars(dt, null);
    for (let p = 0; p < 2; p++) {
      loadP(p);
      tickPlayerFx(dt);
      if (state === 'countdown') { if (p === 0) countdown(dt); }
      else if (state === 'play') { if (PL[p].out) overUpdateP(dt); else update(dt, false); }
      else if (state === 'over') overUpdateP(dt);
      storeP(p);
    }
    if (was === 'countdown' && state === 'play') { loadP(1); showBanner('¡YA!', '', '#7dff6a'); storeP(1); }
    syncRivals();
    if (state === 'play' && PL[0].out && PL[1].out) endMP();
  }
  function tick(dt) {
    tAnim += dt;
    weatherTick(dt);
    if (mpOn && PL.length === 2 && (state === 'play' || state === 'countdown' || state === 'over')) { tickMP(dt); return; }
    tickPlayerFx(dt);
    if (state === 'menu' || state === 'garage' || state === 'maps' || state === 'controls' || state === 'lobby' || state === 'name' || state === 'modes' || state === 'diff' || state === 'settings' || state === 'shop') attract(dt);
    else if (state === 'countdown') countdown(dt);
    else if (state === 'play') update(dt);
    else if (state === 'over') overUpdate(dt);
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  Dibujo: utilidades
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  let FOG = [0, 0, 0], DIM = 1, C = null;
  function T(c, vis, f) {
    const k = DIM * (f === undefined ? 1 : f);
    const r = FOG[0] + (c[0] * k - FOG[0]) * vis, g = FOG[1] + (c[1] * k - FOG[1]) * vis, b = FOG[2] + (c[2] * k - FOG[2]) * vis;
    return 'rgb(' + (r < 0 ? 0 : r > 255 ? 255 : r | 0) + ',' + (g < 0 ? 0 : g > 255 ? 255 : g | 0) + ',' + (b < 0 ? 0 : b > 255 ? 255 : b | 0) + ')';
  }
  const visOf = (fog) => Math.min(1, fog * 1.6);
  function rr(c, x, y, w, h, r) {
    r = Math.max(0, Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2));
    c.beginPath();
    c.moveTo(x + r, y); c.lineTo(x + w - r, y); c.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + r);
    c.lineTo(x + w, y + h - r); c.quadraticCurveTo(x + w, y + h, x + w - r, y + h);
    c.lineTo(x + r, y + h); c.quadraticCurveTo(x, y + h, x, y + h - r);
    c.lineTo(x, y + r); c.quadraticCurveTo(x, y, x + r, y); c.closePath();
  }
  function poly(x1, y1, x2, y2, x3, y3, x4, y4, color) {
    ctx.fillStyle = color;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x1, y1); ctx.lineTo(x2, y2); ctx.lineTo(x3, y3); ctx.lineTo(x4, y4); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  }
  function txt(s, x, y, size, color, align, weight, italic) {
    s = tr(s);
    ctx.font = (italic === false ? '' : 'italic ') + (weight || 'bold') + ' ' + size + 'px "Segoe UI","Helvetica Neue",Arial,sans-serif';
    ctx.textAlign = align || 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)'; ctx.fillText(s, x + size * 0.05, y + size * 0.06);
    ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.fillText(s, x, y);
  }
  function project(p, camX, camY, camZ) {
    p.camera.x = (p.world.x || 0) - camX;
    p.camera.y = p.world.y - camY;
    p.camera.z = p.world.z - camZ;
    const sc = cameraDepth / p.camera.z;
    p.screen.scale = sc;
    p.screen.x = Math.round(W / 2 + sc * p.camera.x * W / 2);
    p.screen.y = Math.round(H / 2 - sc * p.camera.y * H / 2);
    p.screen.w = Math.round(sc * roadWidth * W / 2);
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  Dibujo: fondo
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  function ridge(xx, f1, f2, f3, p) {
    const a = TAU * xx / 2400;
    return 0.55 * (1 - Math.abs(Math.sin(a * f1 + p))) + 0.3 * (1 - Math.abs(Math.sin(a * f2 + p * 2))) + 0.15 * (1 - Math.abs(Math.sin(a * f3 + p * 3)));
  }
  function drawMountains(col, amp, mult, f1, f2, f3, p, base) {
    ctx.fillStyle = css(col);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-M, base + 6);
    for (let x = -M; x <= W + M; x += 8) ctx.lineTo(x, base - amp * ridge(x + bgPx * mult, f1, f2, f3, p));
    ctx.lineTo(W + M, base + 6); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  }
  function drawMesas(col, hMax, mult, seed, base) {
    const cw = Math.max(70, H * 0.15), off = bgPx * mult;
    const i0 = Math.floor((off - M) / cw) - 1, i1 = Math.ceil((off + W + M) / cw);
    ctx.fillStyle = css(col);
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) {
      const hh = hMax * (0.18 + 0.82 * hash1(i, seed)), bw = cw * (0.55 + 0.7 * hash1(i, seed + 1)), x0 = i * cw - off, tp = hash1(i, seed + 2) < 0.3 ? 0.3 : 0.1;
      ctx.moveTo(x0 - bw * 0.2, base + 6);
      ctx.lineTo(x0 + bw * 0.04, base - hh * 0.72); ctx.lineTo(x0 + bw * tp * 0.5, base - hh);
      ctx.lineTo(x0 + bw * (1 - tp), base - hh); ctx.lineTo(x0 + bw * 0.96, base - hh * 0.7);
      ctx.lineTo(x0 + bw * 1.2, base + 6); ctx.closePath();
    }
    ctx.fill();
  }
  function drawCloud(x, y, r) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, TAU); ctx.arc(x + r * 0.9, y + r * 0.15, r * 0.8, 0, TAU); ctx.arc(x - r * 0.9, y + r * 0.2, r * 0.7, 0, TAU);
    ctx.arc(x + r * 1.7, y + r * 0.3, r * 0.55, 0, TAU); ctx.arc(x - r * 1.7, y + r * 0.35, r * 0.5, 0, TAU);
    ctx.fill();
  }
  function hash1(a, b) { const v = Math.sin(a * 127.1 + b * 311.7) * 43758.5453; return v - Math.floor(v); }
  function drawSkyline(col, hMax, mult, seed, base, litA, litCol) {
    const cw = Math.max(30, H * 0.065), off = bgPx * mult;
    const i0 = Math.floor((off - M) / cw), i1 = Math.ceil((off + W + M) / cw);
    ctx.fillStyle = css(col);
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) {
      const hh = hMax * (0.22 + 0.78 * hash1(i, seed)), bw = cw * (0.84 + 0.16 * hash1(i, seed + 1));
      ctx.rect(i * cw - off, base - hh, bw, hh + 6);
    }
    ctx.fill();
    if (litA > 0.02) {
      const ws = cw * 0.17;
      ctx.fillStyle = css(litCol, (0.62 * litA).toFixed(3));
      ctx.beginPath();
      for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) {
