    txt(label, x, y - 2 * ui, 15 * ui, '#ffffff', 'left', '900', false);
    if (sub) txt(sub, x, y + 13 * ui, 11.5 * ui, '#7f95b8', 'left', 'bold', false);
    const sw = 56 * ui, sh = 28 * ui, sx = x + w - sw, sy = y - 16 * ui;
    rr(ctx, sx, sy, sw, sh, sh / 2); ctx.fillStyle = on ? 'rgba(80,220,120,0.9)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.16)'; ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5 * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = on ? '#ffffff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)'; ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx + (on ? sw - sh / 2 : sh / 2), sy + sh / 2, sh * 0.38, 0, TAU); ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.fill();
    txt(on ? 'SÍ' : 'NO', sx + (on ? sh * 0.55 : sw - sh * 0.5), sy + sh / 2 + 4.5 * ui, 11 * ui, on ? '#0b3d1c' : '#9fb4d6', 'center', '900', false);
    hit(x - 10 * ui, y - 26 * ui, w + 20 * ui, 44 * ui, function () { fn(); Sound.tone(520, 0.05, 0.1, 'square'); saveCfg(); });
  }
  function toggleFullscreen() {
    try {
      const el = overlay || canvas;
      if (document.fullscreenElement) { const p = document.exitFullscreen(); if (p && p.catch) p.catch(function () {}); }
      else if (el.requestFullscreen) { const p = el.requestFullscreen(); if (p && p.catch) p.catch(function () {}); }
    } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
  }
  function drawSettings(ui) {
    dim(0.68);
    const ox = (W - 1280 * ui) / 2, oy = (H - 720 * ui) / 2;
    const X = (v) => ox + v * ui, Y = (v) => oy + (v + 30) * ui;
    panel(X(140), Y(-18), 1000 * ui, 650 * ui, ui);
    txt('AJUSTES', X(640), Y(60), 44 * ui, '#ffffff', 'center', '900');
    txt('Personaliza el juego a tu gusto · se guarda solo', X(640), Y(84), 14 * ui, '#cfe0ff', 'center', 'bold', false);
    const x0 = X(180), w0 = 440 * ui, x1 = X(660), w1 = 440 * ui;
    // --- columna izquierda ---
    txt('JUGADOR', x0, Y(112), 12 * ui, '#ffb347', 'left', '900', false);
    txt('Tu nombre', x0, Y(142), 15 * ui, '#ffffff', 'left', '900', false);
    const nx = x0 + 150 * ui, ny = Y(122), nw = w0 - 150 * ui, nh = 32 * ui;
    rr(ctx, nx, ny, nw, nh, 9 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)'; ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineWidth = (nameEditS ? 3 : 1.5) * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = nameEditS ? '#ffd23f' : over(nx, ny, nw, nh) ? '#ffffff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)'; ctx.stroke();
    if (playerName || nameEditS) txt(playerName + (nameEditS && Math.floor(tAnim * 2) % 2 === 0 ? '|' : ''), nx + nw / 2, ny + nh / 2 + 6 * ui, 17 * ui, '#ffffff', 'center', '900', false);
    else txt('Clic para escribir', nx + nw / 2, ny + nh / 2 + 5 * ui, 13 * ui, 'rgba(159,180,214,0.8)', 'center', 'bold', false);
    hit(nx, ny, nw, nh, function () { nameEditS = true; nameFresh = true; });
    txt('SONIDO', x0, Y(188), 12 * ui, '#ffb347', 'left', '900', false);
    slider('Volumen general', cfg.vol, x0, Y(220), w0, ui, function (v) { cfg.vol = v; Sound.applyVol(); }, 190);
    slider('Música', cfg.music, x0, Y(264), w0, ui, function (v) { cfg.music = v; }, 190);
    toggleRow('Sonido activado', 'Silencia todo el juego (tecla M)', !Sound.muted, x0, Y(314), w0, ui, function () { Sound.init(); Sound.resume(); Sound.toggleMute(); });
    txt('VISTA', x0, Y(366), 12 * ui, '#ffb347', 'left', '900', false);
    toggleRow('Empezar en 1ª persona', 'Si no, empiezas viendo el coche desde fuera', viewPref === 'first', x0, Y(398), w0, ui, function () { viewPref = viewPref === 'first' ? 'third' : 'first'; try { window.localStorage.setItem('turbo_highway_view', viewPref); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ } });
    txt('IDIOMA', x0, Y(460), 12 * ui, '#ffb347', 'left', '900', false);
    button('Español', x0, Y(472), 210 * ui, 40 * ui, ui, function () { setLang('es'); }, cfg.lang !== 'en');
    button('English', x0 + 230 * ui, Y(472), 210 * ui, 40 * ui, ui, function () { setLang('en'); }, cfg.lang === 'en');
    // --- columna derecha ---
    txt('EFECTOS', x1, Y(112), 12 * ui, '#ffb347', 'left', '900', false);
    toggleRow('Marcas de frenado', 'Huellas de las ruedas en el asfalto', cfg.skid, x1, Y(152), w1, ui, function () { cfg.skid = !cfg.skid; });
    toggleRow('Lluvia y nieve', 'Si lo apagas, el clima no se dibuja', cfg.weather, x1, Y(198), w1, ui, function () { cfg.weather = !cfg.weather; });
    toggleRow('Sacudida de cámara', 'Al chocar o ir muy rápido', cfg.shake, x1, Y(244), w1, ui, function () { cfg.shake = !cfg.shake; });
    toggleRow('Nombres sobre los coches', 'El nombre del piloto encima del coche', cfg.names, x1, Y(290), w1, ui, function () { cfg.names = !cfg.names; });
    txt('CLIMA ALEATORIO', x1, Y(344), 12 * ui, '#ffb347', 'left', '900', false);
    slider('Prob. de lluvia', cfg.rain, x1, Y(376), w1, ui, function (v) { cfg.rain = v; if (cfg.rain + cfg.snow > 1) cfg.snow = 1 - cfg.rain; }, 150);
    slider('Prob. de nieve', cfg.snow, x1, Y(420), w1, ui, function (v) { cfg.snow = v; if (cfg.rain + cfg.snow > 1) cfg.rain = 1 - cfg.snow; }, 150);
    txt('Despejado: ' + Math.round(Math.max(0, 1 - cfg.rain - cfg.snow) * 100) + '%  ·  se sortea solo al empezar cada partida', x1, Y(448), 11.5 * ui, '#7f95b8', 'left', 'bold', false);
    button('PANTALLA COMPLETA', X(300), Y(546), 220 * ui, 40 * ui, ui, toggleFullscreen);
    button('RESTABLECER', X(535), Y(546), 190 * ui, 40 * ui, ui, function () { const lg = cfg.lang; Object.assign(cfg, CFG_DEF); cfg.lang = lg; Sound.applyVol(); saveCfg(); });
    button('LISTO', X(740), Y(546), 240 * ui, 40 * ui, ui, closeSettings, true);
  }
  function coinIcon(x, y, r) {
    ctx.save();
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(x - r * 0.3, y - r * 0.3, r * 0.1, x, y, r);
    g.addColorStop(0, '#fff3a0'); g.addColorStop(0.55, '#ffc928'); g.addColorStop(1, '#d98a00');
    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, r * 0.14); ctx.strokeStyle = '#9a5a00'; ctx.stroke();
    ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, r * 0.12); ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(154,90,0,0.7)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r * 0.62, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(154,90,0,0.85)'; ctx.fillRect(x - r * 0.08, y - r * 0.34, r * 0.16, r * 0.68);
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function drawShop(ui) {
    dim(0.8);
    const ox = (W - 1280 * ui) / 2, oy = (H - 720 * ui) / 2;
    const X = (v) => ox + v * ui, Y = (v) => oy + v * ui;
    const px = X(240), py = Y(50), pw = 800 * ui, ph = 616 * ui;
    // panel negro
    rr(ctx, px, py, pw, ph, 18 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5,5,9,0.97)'; ctx.fill();
    let g = ctx.createLinearGradient(px, 0, px + pw, 0); g.addColorStop(0, '#ff2d95'); g.addColorStop(0.5, '#ffd400'); g.addColorStop(1, '#2fd8ff');
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5 * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = g; ctx.stroke();
    // cabecera
    ctx.save(); rr(ctx, px, py, pw, ph, 18 * ui); ctx.clip();
    g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, py, 0, py + 72 * ui); g.addColorStop(0, '#1c1c28'); g.addColorStop(1, '#0a0a10');
    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(px, py, pw, 72 * ui);
    g = ctx.createLinearGradient(px, 0, px + pw, 0); g.addColorStop(0, '#ff2d95'); g.addColorStop(0.5, '#ffd400'); g.addColorStop(1, '#2fd8ff');
    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(px, py + 70 * ui, pw, 3 * ui);
    ctx.restore();
    coinIcon(px + 40 * ui, py + 36 * ui, 17 * ui);
    txt('TIENDA', px + 70 * ui, py + 50 * ui, 38 * ui, '#ffffff', 'left', '900');
    // saldo
    const bw = 170 * ui, bx = px + pw - 70 * ui - bw - 10 * ui, by = py + 16 * ui;
    rr(ctx, bx, by, bw, 40 * ui, 12 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = '#000000'; ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineWidth = 2 * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,210,63,0.7)'; ctx.stroke();
    coinIcon(bx + 24 * ui, by + 20 * ui, 12 * ui);
    txt(shop.inf ? '∞' : thousands(shop.coins), bx + bw - 14 * ui, by + 28 * ui, 22 * ui, '#ffd23f', 'right', '900', false);
    // cerrar
    const cx0 = px + pw - 58 * ui, cy0 = py + 16 * ui, cs0 = 40 * ui, chv = over(cx0, cy0, cs0, cs0);
    rr(ctx, cx0, cy0, cs0, cs0, 10 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = chv ? '#ff5470' : '#d62a48'; ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineWidth = 2 * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.stroke();
    ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineWidth = 4 * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(cx0 + 12 * ui, cy0 + 12 * ui); ctx.lineTo(cx0 + 28 * ui, cy0 + 28 * ui); ctx.moveTo(cx0 + 28 * ui, cy0 + 12 * ui); ctx.lineTo(cx0 + 12 * ui, cy0 + 28 * ui); ctx.stroke();
    hit(cx0, cy0, cs0, cs0, closeShop);

    // ---- mejora de velocidad ----
    const lx = px + 26 * ui, lw = pw - 52 * ui;
    txt('MEJORAS', lx, py + 104 * ui, 12 * ui, '#ffb347', 'left', '900', false);
    const cy1 = py + 116 * ui, ch1 = 150 * ui;
    rr(ctx, lx, cy1, lw, ch1, 14 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0d13'; ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.5 * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.14)'; ctx.stroke();
    // icono
    const is = 96 * ui, ix = lx + 18 * ui, iy = cy1 + 27 * ui;
    rr(ctx, ix, iy, is, is, 14 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = '#050508'; ctx.fill(); ctx.lineWidth = 2 * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(47,216,255,0.6)'; ctx.stroke();
    ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineWidth = 6 * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(47,216,255,0.9)';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ix + is / 2, iy + is * 0.62, is * 0.34, Math.PI * 0.85, Math.PI * 2.15); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd23f'; ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(ix + is * 0.56, iy + is * 0.18); ctx.lineTo(ix + is * 0.36, iy + is * 0.54); ctx.lineTo(ix + is * 0.5, iy + is * 0.54); ctx.lineTo(ix + is * 0.42, iy + is * 0.84); ctx.lineTo(ix + is * 0.66, iy + is * 0.44); ctx.lineTo(ix + is * 0.52, iy + is * 0.44); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    // textos
    const tx = ix + is + 22 * ui, maxed = shop.lvl >= MAX_LVL;
    txt('VELOCIDAD MÁXIMA', tx, cy1 + 44 * ui, 24 * ui, '#ffffff', 'left', '900');
    txt('Nivel ' + shop.lvl + ' / ' + MAX_LVL, tx, cy1 + 66 * ui, 13 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'left', 'bold', false);
    for (let i = 0; i < MAX_LVL; i++) {
      const pxx = tx + i * 27 * ui, on = i < shop.lvl;
      rr(ctx, pxx, cy1 + 76 * ui, 22 * ui, 10 * ui, 4 * ui);
      if (on) { const pg = ctx.createLinearGradient(pxx, 0, pxx + 22 * ui, 0); pg.addColorStop(0, '#2fd8ff'); pg.addColorStop(1, '#ff2d95'); ctx.fillStyle = pg; } else ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)';
      ctx.fill();
    }
    const cur0 = kmhOfLvl(shop.lvl), nxt = kmhOfLvl(Math.min(MAX_LVL, shop.lvl + 1));
    txt(cur0 + ' km/h', tx, cy1 + 120 * ui, 22 * ui, '#ffffff', 'left', '900', false);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff'; ctx.beginPath(); const ax = tx + 118 * ui, ay = cy1 + 113 * ui;
    ctx.moveTo(ax, ay - 7 * ui); ctx.lineTo(ax + 14 * ui, ay); ctx.lineTo(ax, ay + 7 * ui); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    txt(maxed ? 'MÁX' : nxt + ' km/h', tx + 142 * ui, cy1 + 120 * ui, 22 * ui, maxed ? '#ff9d2e' : '#2fd8ff', 'left', '900', false);
    // botón comprar
    const bbw = 190 * ui, bbh = 70 * ui, bbx = lx + lw - bbw - 20 * ui, bby = cy1 + (ch1 - bbh) / 2;
    const cost = maxed ? 0 : UPG_COST[shop.lvl], can = shop.inf || shop.coins >= cost, bhv = over(bbx, bby, bbw, bbh);
    rr(ctx, bbx, bby, bbw, bbh, 12 * ui);
    if (maxed) ctx.fillStyle = '#1a1a22';
    else if (can) { const bg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, bby, 0, bby + bbh); bg.addColorStop(0, bhv ? '#ffe68a' : '#ffd23f'); bg.addColorStop(1, bhv ? '#ffbf2e' : '#f2a900'); ctx.fillStyle = bg; }
    else ctx.fillStyle = '#2a1418';
    ctx.fill(); ctx.lineWidth = 2 * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = maxed ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)' : can ? '#ffffff' : 'rgba(255,90,90,0.6)'; ctx.stroke();
    if (maxed) txt('MÁXIMO', bbx + bbw / 2, bby + bbh / 2 + 8 * ui, 22 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'center', '900', false);
    else {
      txt('COMPRAR', bbx + bbw / 2, bby + 26 * ui, 13 * ui, can ? '#201400' : '#ff9a9a', 'center', '900', false);
      coinIcon(bbx + bbw / 2 - 34 * ui, bby + 48 * ui, 10 * ui);
      txt(shop.inf ? '0' : thousands(cost), bbx + bbw / 2 - 18 * ui, bby + 55 * ui, 21 * ui, can ? '#201400' : '#ff9a9a', 'left', '900', false);
    }
    hit(bbx, bby, bbw, bbh, buyUpgrade);

    // ---- velocidad actual ----
    const cy2 = cy1 + ch1 + 14 * ui, ch2 = 84 * ui;
    rr(ctx, lx, cy2, lw, ch2, 14 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0d13'; ctx.fill(); ctx.lineWidth = 1.5 * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.14)'; ctx.stroke();
    txt('Tu velocidad: ' + cur0 + ' km/h  ·  con nitro hasta ' + Math.round(cur0 * NITRO_MULT) + ' km/h', lx + 20 * ui, cy2 + 30 * ui, 15 * ui, '#ffffff', 'left', '900', false);
    const tbx = lx + 20 * ui, tbw = lw - 40 * ui, tby = cy2 + 46 * ui;
    rr(ctx, tbx, tby, tbw, 12 * ui, 6 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)'; ctx.fill();
    rr(ctx, tbx, tby, Math.max(12 * ui, tbw * Math.min(1, cur0 * NITRO_MULT / ABS_MAX_KMH)), 12 * ui, 6 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = '#ff9d2e'; ctx.fill();
    rr(ctx, tbx, tby, Math.max(12 * ui, tbw * cur0 / ABS_MAX_KMH), 12 * ui, 6 * ui);
    g = ctx.createLinearGradient(tbx, 0, tbx + tbw, 0); g.addColorStop(0, '#2fd8ff'); g.addColorStop(1, '#ff2d95'); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fill();
    txt('0', tbx, tby + 30 * ui, 11 * ui, '#7f95b8', 'left', 'bold', false);
    txt('LÍMITE ' + ABS_MAX_KMH + ' KM/H', tbx + tbw, tby + 30 * ui, 11 * ui, '#7f95b8', 'right', 'bold', false);
    // ---- cómo se ganan monedas ----
    const cy3 = cy2 + ch2 + 12 * ui;
    coinIcon(lx + 12 * ui, cy3 + 14 * ui, 8 * ui);
    txt('+' + COIN_LAP[diffIdx] + ' por vuelta  ·  +' + COIN_CP[diffIdx] + ' por checkpoint  (según la dificultad)', lx + 30 * ui, cy3 + 19 * ui, 13 * ui, '#cfe0ff', 'left', 'bold', false);
    txt('En 2 jugadores no se ganan monedas ni cuentan las mejoras (300 km/h, 350 con nitro).', lx + 4 * ui, cy3 + 42 * ui, 12 * ui, '#7f95b8', 'left', 'bold', false);

    // ---- códigos ----
    const cy4 = cy3 + 70 * ui;
    txt('CÓDIGOS', lx, cy4, 12 * ui, '#ffb347', 'left', '900', false);
    const fx = lx, fy = cy4 + 12 * ui, fw = lw - 190 * ui, fh = 46 * ui;
    rr(ctx, fx, fy, fw, fh, 10 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = '#000000'; ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineWidth = (codeEdit ? 3 : 1.5) * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = codeEdit ? '#ffd23f' : over(fx, fy, fw, fh) ? '#ffffff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)'; ctx.stroke();
    if (codeText || codeEdit) txt(codeText + (codeEdit && Math.floor(tAnim * 2) % 2 === 0 ? '|' : ''), fx + 16 * ui, fy + fh / 2 + 7 * ui, 20 * ui, '#ffffff', 'left', '900', false);
    else txt('Haz clic y escribe un código…', fx + 16 * ui, fy + fh / 2 + 6 * ui, 15 * ui, 'rgba(159,180,214,0.7)', 'left', 'bold', false);
    hit(fx, fy, fw, fh, function () { codeEdit = true; });
    button('CANJEAR', fx + fw + 14 * ui, fy, 176 * ui, fh, ui, redeemCode, codeText.length > 0);
    if (tAnim < shopMsg.until) txt(shopMsg.s, lx + lw / 2, fy + fh + 26 * ui, 15 * ui, shopMsg.ok ? '#7dff6a' : '#ff7a7a', 'center', '900', false);
    else if (shop.inf) txt('Monedas infinitas activadas', lx + lw / 2, fy + fh + 26 * ui, 13 * ui, '#7dff6a', 'center', 'bold', false);
    button('LISTO', px + pw / 2 - 90 * ui, py + ph - 56 * ui, 180 * ui, 40 * ui, ui, closeShop, true);
  }
  function drawName(ui) {
    dim(0.62);
    const ox = (W - 1280 * ui) / 2, oy = (H - 720 * ui) / 2;
    const X = (v) => ox + v * ui, Y = (v) => oy + v * ui;

      if (sel) { ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd23f'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(tx + tw - 16 * ui, ty + 16 * ui, 11 * ui, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); txt('✓', tx + tw - 16 * ui, ty + 22 * ui, 15 * ui, '#101018', 'center', '900', false); }

      txt(opts[i][2], tx + tw / 2, ty + th + 40 * ui, 12 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'center', 'bold', false);
      hit(tx, ty, tw, th + 48 * ui, (function (f) { return function () { viewPref = f ? 'first' : 'third'; Sound.tone(520, 0.06, 0.12, 'square'); }; })(first));
    }
    const D0 = DIFFS[diffIdx];
    txt('Dificultad: ' + D0.name, X(640), Y(468), 14 * ui, css(D0.col), 'center', 'bold', false);
    txt('← → cambia la vista   ·   V la cambia en carrera', X(640), Y(496), 12 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'center', 'bold', false);

    button('¡A CORRER!', X(640) - 40 * ui, Y(530), 240 * ui, 44 * ui, ui, confirmName, true);
  }
  function drawLobby(ui) {
    dim(0.72);
    txt('MULTIJUGADOR', W / 2, H * 0.085, 54 * ui, '#ffffff', 'center', '900');
    txt('Dos jugadores en el mismo ordenador · gana quien consiga más puntos', W / 2, H * 0.085 + 28 * ui, 15 * ui, '#cfe0ff', 'center', 'bold', false);
    const cw = 400 * ui, ch = 430 * ui, gap = 36 * ui, y0 = H * 0.15;
    const info = [
      { name: 'JUGADOR 1', keys: ['W', 'A', 'S', 'D'], nitro: 'ESPACIO' },
      { name: 'JUGADOR 2', keys: ['↑', '←', '↓', '→'], nitro: 'SHIFT DER.' }
    ];
    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
      const x = W / 2 - cw - gap / 2 + i * (cw + gap), I = info[i], col = MP_COLORS[i], sk = builds[i];
      rr(ctx, x, y0, cw, ch, 16 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,12,24,0.84)'; ctx.fill();
      ctx.lineWidth = 3 * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = css(col, 0.95); ctx.stroke();
      txt(I.name, x + cw / 2, y0 + 38 * ui, 26 * ui, css(col), 'center', '900');
      const cx = x + cw / 2, by = y0 + 238 * ui, pw = 178 * ui;
      // Nombre del piloto (clic y escribe)
      const fx = cx - 150 * ui, fy = y0 + 54 * ui, fw = 300 * ui, fh = 36 * ui, ed = nameEdit === i;
      rr(ctx, fx, fy, fw, fh, 10 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)'; ctx.fill();
      ctx.lineWidth = (ed ? 3 : 1.5) * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = ed ? css(col) : over(fx, fy, fw, fh) ? '#ffffff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)'; ctx.stroke();
      const cur0 = ed && Math.floor(tAnim * 2) % 2 === 0 ? '|' : '';
      if (mpNames[i] || ed) txt(mpNames[i] + cur0, cx, fy + fh / 2 + 7 * ui, 19 * ui, '#ffffff', 'center', '900', false);
      else txt('Nombre (opcional)', cx, fy + fh / 2 + 6 * ui, 15 * ui, 'rgba(159,180,214,0.7)', 'center', 'bold', false);
      hit(fx, fy, fw, fh, function () { nameEdit = i; });
      const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(cx, by, 1, cx, by, pw * 0.8);
      g.addColorStop(0, css(sk.glow, 0.4)); g.addColorStop(1, css(sk.glow, 0));
      ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(cx, by + pw * 0.02, pw * 0.8, pw * 0.15, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      const o = skinOpts(sk); o.roll = Math.sin(tAnim * 1.2 + i) * 0.03; o.brake = true; o.glow = 0.26;
      drawCar(ctx, cx, by + Math.sin(tAnim * 2 + i) * 2 * ui, pw, o);
      arrow(x + 46 * ui, by - 46 * ui, -1, ui, function () { changeMPSkin(i, -1); });
      arrow(x + cw - 46 * ui, by - 46 * ui, 1, ui, function () { changeMPSkin(i, 1); });
      txt(sk.name.toUpperCase() + (sk.custom ? ' ✦' : ''), cx, by + 36 * ui, 20 * ui, css(mixc(sk.color, [255, 255, 255], 0.5)), 'center', '900');
      button('PERSONALIZAR', x + 24 * ui, y0 + 296 * ui, 200 * ui, 34 * ui, ui, function () { editBuild(i); });
      txt('MOVER', x + 28 * ui, y0 + ch - 54 * ui, 12 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'left', 'bold', false);
      let kx = x + 100 * ui;
      for (let k = 0; k < I.keys.length; k++) kx += keycap(I.keys[k], kx, y0 + ch - 74 * ui, ui) + 6 * ui;
      txt('NITRO', x + 28 * ui, y0 + ch - 16 * ui, 12 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'left', 'bold', false);
      keycap(I.nitro, x + 100 * ui, y0 + ch - 36 * ui, ui);
    }
    const my = y0 + ch + 30 * ui;
    arrow(W / 2 - 170 * ui, my - 6 * ui, -1, ui, function () { changeMap(-1); });
    arrow(W / 2 + 170 * ui, my - 6 * ui, 1, ui, function () { changeMap(1); });
    txt('MAPA: ' + curMap.name.toUpperCase(), W / 2, my, 21 * ui, '#ffd23f', 'center', '900');
    txt('Dificultad: ' + DIFFS[diffIdx].name, W / 2, my + 28 * ui, 13 * ui, css(DIFFS[diffIdx].col), 'center', 'bold', false);
    const by2 = my + 46 * ui;
    button('VOLVER', W / 2 - 110 * ui - 160 * ui, by2, 140 * ui, 44 * ui, ui, closeLobby);
    button('¡A CORRER!', W / 2 - 110 * ui, by2, 220 * ui, 44 * ui, ui, lobbyStart, true);
    if (tAnim < gMsg.until) txt(gMsg.s, W / 2, by2 + 66 * ui, 14 * ui, '#ffd23f', 'center', 'bold', false);
  }
  function drawOverMP(ui) {
    dim(0.62);
    const s0 = scoreOf(PL[0]), s1 = scoreOf(PL[1]), win = s0 > s1 ? 0 : s1 > s0 ? 1 : -1;
    const ts = Math.min(76 * ui, W / 13);
    if (win >= 0) txt('GANA EL JUGADOR ' + (win + 1), W / 2, H * 0.25, ts, css(MP_COLORS[win]), 'center', '900');
    else txt('¡EMPATE!', W / 2, H * 0.25, ts, '#ffd23f', 'center', '900');
    const pw = 540 * ui, ph = 214 * ui, px = W / 2 - pw / 2, py = H * 0.25 + 28 * ui;
    panel(px, py, pw, ph, ui);
    const c1 = px + pw * 0.58, c2 = px + pw * 0.86;
    txt('JUGADOR 1' + (win === 0 ? ' ★' : ''), c1, py + 34 * ui, 15 * ui, css(MP_COLORS[0]), 'center', '900');
    txt('JUGADOR 2' + (win === 1 ? ' ★' : ''), c2, py + 34 * ui, 15 * ui, css(MP_COLORS[1]), 'center', '900');
    const rows = [
      ['Puntuación', thousands(s0), thousands(s1)],
      ['Distancia', (PL[0].dist / 1000).toFixed(2) + ' km', (PL[1].dist / 1000).toFixed(2) + ' km'],
      ['Vueltas', String(PL[0].lap), String(PL[1].lap)],
      ['Adelantamientos', String(PL[0].overtakes), String(PL[1].overtakes)]
    ];
    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
      const yy = py + (72 + i * 36) * ui;
      txt(rows[i][0], px + 28 * ui, yy, 17 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'left', 'bold', false);
      txt(rows[i][1], c1, yy, 20 * ui, '#ffffff', 'center', '900');
      txt(rows[i][2], c2, yy, 20 * ui, '#ffffff', 'center', '900');
    }
    const by = py + ph + 26 * ui;
    button('REVANCHA', W / 2 - 267 * ui, by, 170 * ui, 42 * ui, ui, startGame, true);
    button('AJUSTES', W / 2 - 85 * ui, by, 170 * ui, 42 * ui, ui, function () { lobbyFrom = 'menu'; state = 'lobby'; resetKeysExceptNone(); });
    button('MENÚ  [Q]', W / 2 + 97 * ui, by, 170 * ui, 42 * ui, ui, goMenu);
  }
  function drawImgBox(c, b, x, y, w, h, alpha) {
    if (!b || !b.img) return;
    const iw = b.img.width, ih = b.img.height;
    if (!(iw > 0 && ih > 0 && w > 4 && h > 4)) return;
    const k = Math.min(w / iw, h / ih), dw = iw * k, dh = ih * k, dx = x + (w - dw) / 2, dy = y + (h - dh) / 2, r = Math.min(dw, dh) * 0.06;
    c.save(); c.globalAlpha = alpha;
    c.shadowColor = css(b.glow, 0.8); c.shadowBlur = 24;
    rr(c, dx, dy, dw, dh, r); c.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.4)'; c.fill();
    c.shadowBlur = 0;
    c.save(); rr(c, dx, dy, dw, dh, r); c.clip(); c.drawImage(b.img, dx, dy, dw, dh); c.restore();
    rr(c, dx, dy, dw, dh, r); c.lineWidth = 3; c.strokeStyle = css(b.glow, 0.9); c.stroke();
    c.restore();
  }
  function swatches(list, sel, x, y, cols, sz, gap, ui, pick) {
    for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
      const cx = x + (i % cols) * (sz + gap), cy = y + Math.floor(i / cols) * (sz + gap), on = arrEq(list[i], sel), hov = over(cx, cy, sz, sz);
      rr(ctx, cx, cy, sz, sz, 7 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = css(list[i]); ctx.fill();
      ctx.lineWidth = (on ? 3.5 : hov ? 2.5 : 1.2) * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = on ? '#ffd23f' : hov ? '#ffffff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)'; ctx.stroke();
      hit(cx, cy, sz, sz, (function (c) { return function () { pick(c); }; })(list[i]));
    }
  }
  function randomBuild() {
    const b = builds[gp];
    b.color = U.pick(PAINTS).slice(); b.stripe = U.pick(PAINTS).slice(); b.glow = U.pick(NEONS).slice();
    b.livery = U.pick(LIVERIES)[0]; b.finish = U.pick(FINISHES)[0]; b.type = Math.random() < 0.3 ? 'sedan' : 'player'; b.wing = Math.random() < 0.7;
    b.wheelStyle = (Math.random() * WHEEL_STYLES.length) | 0; b.wheelCol = U.pick(WHEEL_COLS).slice(); b.wheelGlow = Math.random() < 0.3; b.headCol = U.pick(HEAD_COLS).slice(); b.tailCol = U.pick(TAIL_COLS).slice();
    touch(gp); Sound.tone(660, 0.07, 0.12, 'square');
  }
  function cycle(list, cur) { let k = 0; for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) if (list[i][0] === cur) k = i; return list[(k + 1) % list.length][0]; }
  function nameOf(list, cur) { for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) if (list[i][0] === cur) return list[i][1]; return ''; }
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------

  let sliderDrag = null, plateFresh = false, nameEditS = false, nameFresh = false;
  function saveName() { nameEditS = false; playerName = playerName.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 12); try { window.localStorage.setItem('turbo_highway_name', playerName); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ } }
  let gYaw = 0.7, gDrag = null, gHold = 0, gT = 0, plateEdit = false, gTab = 0;
  function circlePts(n, r, a0) { const o = []; for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { const a = (a0 || 0) + k * TAU / n; o.push([Math.cos(a) * r, Math.sin(a) * r]); } return o; }
  // Dibujo de la llanta en un disco unidad: lista de polígonos [{p:[[u,v]..], dark}]
  function wheelPolys(style, spin) {
    const P = [], wedge = function (a, hw, r0, r1, sk) {
      sk = sk || 0;
      return [[Math.cos(a - hw) * r0, Math.sin(a - hw) * r0], [Math.cos(a + hw) * r0, Math.sin(a + hw) * r0], [Math.cos(a + hw + sk) * r1, Math.sin(a + hw + sk) * r1], [Math.cos(a - hw + sk) * r1, Math.sin(a - hw + sk) * r1]];
    };
    const ring = function (r0, r1, n) { for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { const a = k * TAU / n, b = (k + 1.06) * TAU / n; P.push({ p: [[Math.cos(a) * r0, Math.sin(a) * r0], [Math.cos(b) * r0, Math.sin(b) * r0], [Math.cos(b) * r1, Math.sin(b) * r1], [Math.cos(a) * r1, Math.sin(a) * r1]] }); } };
    let k;
    if (style === 2) {
      P.push({ p: circlePts(18, 0.66, spin) });
      for (k = 0; k < 5; k++) { const a = spin + k * TAU / 5; P.push({ p: circlePts(6, 0.075, 0).map(function (q) { return [q[0] + Math.cos(a) * 0.4, q[1] + Math.sin(a) * 0.4]; }), dark: true }); }
      P.push({ p: circlePts(10, 0.15, 0), dark: true });
      return P;
    }
    ring(0.6, 0.68, 18);
    if (style === 0) { for (k = 0; k < 5; k++) P.push({ p: wedge(spin + k * TAU / 5, 0.17, 0.1, 0.63) }); }
    else if (style === 1) { for (k = 0; k < 12; k++) P.push({ p: wedge(spin + k * TAU / 12, 0.05, 0.1, 0.63) }); ring(0.33, 0.38, 14); }
    else if (style === 3) { for (k = 0; k < 8; k++) P.push({ p: wedge(spin + k * TAU / 8, 0.11, 0.12, 0.61, 0.5) }); }
    else { for (k = 0; k < 5; k++) { const a = spin + k * TAU / 5; P.push({ p: wedge(a - 0.17, 0.06, 0.1, 0.63) }); P.push({ p: wedge(a + 0.17, 0.06, 0.1, 0.63) }); } }
    P.push({ p: circlePts(10, 0.16, 0) });
    return P;
  }
  function drawWheelIcon(x, y, r, style, col, sel, hov) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#0c0d12'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = css(shade(col, 0.28)); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r * 0.68, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    const polys = wheelPolys(style, 0.3);
    for (let i = 0; i < polys.length; i++) {
      ctx.fillStyle = polys[i].dark ? '#0c0d12' : css(col); ctx.beginPath();
      for (let k = 0; k < polys[i].p.length; k++) { const q = polys[i].p[k]; if (k) ctx.lineTo(x + q[0] * r, y + q[1] * r); else ctx.moveTo(x + q[0] * r, y + q[1] * r); }
      ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.lineWidth = (sel ? 3.5 : hov ? 2.5 : 1.3) * Math.max(0.6, r / 24); ctx.strokeStyle = sel ? '#ffd23f' : hov ? '#ffffff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.32)';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r + 3, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function buildCar3D(b, spin, brake) {
    const F = [], sedan = b.type === 'sedan', col = b.color, dark = shade(col, 0.72), glass = [18, 26, 40], carbon = [24, 24, 28], tire = [14, 14, 17];
    const sub = (a, c) => [a[0] - c[0], a[1] - c[1], a[2] - c[2]];
    const cross = (a, c) => [a[1] * c[2] - a[2] * c[1], a[2] * c[0] - a[0] * c[2], a[0] * c[1] - a[1] * c[0]];
    function face(pts, color, o) {
      o = o || {};
      const e1 = sub(pts[1], pts[0]), e2 = sub(pts[2], pts[0]);
      let n = cross(e1, e2), l = Math.hypot(n[0], n[1], n[2]) || 1; n = [n[0] / l, n[1] / l, n[2] / l];
      if (o.n) n = o.n;
      else if (o.ref) {
        const cx = (pts[0][0] + pts[1][0] + pts[2][0]) / 3, cy = (pts[0][1] + pts[1][1] + pts[2][1]) / 3, cz = (pts[0][2] + pts[1][2] + pts[2][2]) / 3;
        if (n[0] * (cx - o.ref[0]) + n[1] * (cy - o.ref[1]) + n[2] * (cz - o.ref[2]) < 0) n = [-n[0], -n[1], -n[2]];
      }
      F.push({ pts: pts, col: color, n: n, emis: !!o.emis, bias: o.bias || 0, kind: o.kind || '', extra: o.extra || null });
    }
    function box(x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1, color, o) {
      o = o || {}; o.ref = [(x0 + x1) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2];
      const v = [[x0, y0, z0], [x1, y0, z0], [x1, y1, z0], [x0, y1, z0], [x0, y0, z1], [x1, y0, z1], [x1, y1, z1], [x0, y1, z1]];
      [[0, 1, 2, 3], [4, 5, 6, 7], [0, 1, 5, 4], [3, 2, 6, 7], [0, 3, 7, 4], [1, 2, 6, 5]].forEach(function (q) { face(q.map(function (i) { return v[i]; }), color, { ref: o.ref, bias: o.bias, kind: o.kind }); });
    }
    function loft(sec, yb, topCols, sideCol, o) {
      o = o || {}; const ref = [0, (yb + 1) / 2, (sec[0][0] + sec[sec.length - 1][0]) / 2];
      for (let i = 0; i < sec.length - 1; i++) {
        const a = sec[i], c = sec[i + 1];
        face([[-a[2], a[1], a[0]], [a[2], a[1], a[0]], [c[2], c[1], c[0]], [-c[2], c[1], c[0]]], topCols[i], { ref: ref, kind: o.kind, bias: o.bias });
        face([[a[2], a[1], a[0]], [a[2], yb, a[0]], [c[2], yb, c[0]], [c[2], c[1], c[0]]], sideCol[i], { ref: ref, kind: o.kind, bias: o.bias });
        face([[-a[2], a[1], a[0]], [-a[2], yb, a[0]], [-c[2], yb, c[0]], [-c[2], c[1], c[0]]], sideCol[i], { ref: ref, kind: o.kind, bias: o.bias });
      }
      const a0 = sec[0], a1 = sec[sec.length - 1];
      face([[-a0[2], yb, a0[0]], [a0[2], yb, a0[0]], [a0[2], a0[1], a0[0]], [-a0[2], a0[1], a0[0]]], o.rear || sideCol[0], { ref: ref, kind: o.rearKind || o.kind, bias: o.bias });
      face([[-a1[2], yb, a1[0]], [a1[2], yb, a1[0]], [a1[2], a1[1], a1[0]], [-a1[2], a1[1], a1[0]]], o.front || sideCol[sideCol.length - 1], { ref: ref, kind: o.kind, bias: o.bias });
    }
    // carrocería
    const rz = sedan ? 0.86 : 0.8;
    const body = [[-2.2, rz, 0.9], [-2.0, rz + 0.1, 0.96], [-1.0, 0.94, 0.98], [0.3, 0.92, 0.98], [1.5, 0.7, 0.94], [2.2, 0.52, 0.84]];
    const bc = [col, col, col, col, col];
    loft(body, 0.28, bc, bc, { kind: 'body', rearKind: 'rear' });
    // cabina
    const cab = sedan ? [[-1.5, 0.94, 0.8], [-1.1, 1.38, 0.72], [0.4, 1.4, 0.72], [0.95, 0.94, 0.82]] : [[-1.25, 0.94, 0.78], [-0.78, 1.3, 0.7], [0.05, 1.32, 0.7], [0.88, 0.94, 0.8]];
    loft(cab, 0.9, [glass, col, glass], [glass, glass, glass], { kind: 'cab', rear: glass, front: glass });
    // franjas (techo, capó, maletero)
    if (b.stripe && b.livery !== 'none') {
      const spans = b.livery === 'dual' ? [[-0.32, -0.12], [0.12, 0.32]] : b.livery === 'thin' ? [[-0.26, -0.18], [-0.04, 0.04], [0.18, 0.26]] : b.livery === 'edge' ? [[-0.9, -0.78], [0.78, 0.9]] : [[-0.16, 0.16]];
      const strips = [[body, 0, 4], [cab, 1, 1]];
      strips.forEach(function (st) {
        for (let i = st[1]; i <= st[2] && i < st[0].length - 1; i++) {
          const a = st[0][i], c = st[0][i + 1], lift = st[0] === cab ? 0.004 : 0.004;
          spans.forEach(function (sp) {
            const wa = Math.min(a[2] * 0.97, 99), wc = Math.min(c[2] * 0.97, 99);
            const xa0 = Math.max(-wa, sp[0]), xa1 = Math.min(wa, sp[1]), xc0 = Math.max(-wc, sp[0]), xc1 = Math.min(wc, sp[1]);
            if (xa1 > xa0 && xc1 > xc0) face([[xa0, a[1] + lift, a[0]], [xa1, a[1] + lift, a[0]], [xc1, c[1] + lift, c[0]], [xc0, c[1] + lift, c[0]]], b.stripe, { n: [0, 1, 0], bias: 0.3, kind: 'stripe' });
          });
        }
      });
    }
    // espejos
    for (let sd = -1; sd <= 1; sd += 2) box(sd > 0 ? 0.78 : -0.98, sd > 0 ? 0.98 : -0.78, 0.96, 1.06, 0.3, 0.56, col, { bias: 0.4, kind: 'body' });
    // alerón
    if (!sedan && b.wing !== false) {
      box(-0.62, -0.5, 0.82, 1.18, -2.0, -1.88, carbon, { bias: 0.4 }); box(0.5, 0.62, 0.82, 1.18, -2.0, -1.88, carbon, { bias: 0.4 });
      box(-1.0, 1.0, 1.18, 1.25, -2.18, -1.74, carbon, { bias: 0.5 });
      box(-1.04, -0.98, 1.1, 1.3, -2.18, -1.74, col, { bias: 0.55, kind: 'body' }); box(0.98, 1.04, 1.1, 1.3, -2.18, -1.74, col, { bias: 0.55, kind: 'body' });
    }
    // imagen del jugador en la zaga
    if (b.img && b.img.width > 0) F.push({ pts: [[-0.86, 0.3, -2.201], [0.86, 0.3, -2.201], [0.86, rz - 0.02, -2.201], [-0.86, rz - 0.02, -2.201]], col: col, n: [0, 0, -1], bias: 0.45, kind: 'img', extra: { w: 1.72, h: rz - 0.32 } });
    // faros traseros y delanteros
    const tc = brake ? b.tailCol : shade(b.tailCol, 0.6), hc = b.headCol;
    for (let sd = -1; sd <= 1; sd += 2) {
      const xa = sd > 0 ? 0.46 : -0.86, xb = sd > 0 ? 0.86 : -0.46;
      face([[xa, rz - 0.3, -2.206], [xb, rz - 0.3, -2.206], [xb, rz - 0.1, -2.206], [xa, rz - 0.1, -2.206]], tc, { n: [0, 0, -1], emis: true, bias: 0.9, kind: 'tail' });
      const ha = sd > 0 ? 0.42 : -0.8, hb = sd > 0 ? 0.8 : -0.42;
      face([[ha, 0.34, 2.206], [hb, 0.34, 2.206], [hb, 0.47, 2.206], [ha, 0.47, 2.206]], hc, { n: [0, 0, 1], emis: true, bias: 0.9, kind: 'head' });
    }
    face([[-0.46, rz - 0.22, -2.207], [0.46, rz - 0.22, -2.207], [0.46, rz - 0.17, -2.207], [-0.46, rz - 0.17, -2.207]], shade(tc, 0.55), { n: [0, 0, -1], emis: true, bias: 0.9 });
    // parachoques trasero oscuro, como en el coche de carrera
    box(-0.88, 0.88, 0.12, 0.42, -2.27, -2.12, [22, 22, 28], { bias: 0.6, kind: 'bumper' });
    face([[-0.36, 0.3, 2.206], [0.36, 0.3, 2.206], [0.36, 0.42, 2.206], [-0.36, 0.42, 2.206]], [10, 10, 14], { n: [0, 0, 1], bias: 0.8 });
    // matrículas
    F.push({ pts: [[0, rz - 0.46, -2.275]], col: col, n: [0, 0, -1], bias: 1, kind: 'plate', extra: { c: [0, rz - 0.2, -2.275], w: 0.5, h: 0.16, rev: true } });
    F.push({ pts: [[0, 0.2, 2.21]], col: col, n: [0, 0, 1], bias: 1, kind: 'plate', extra: { c: [0, 0.255, 2.21], w: 0.5, h: 0.1, rev: false } });
    // escapes
    for (let sd = -1; sd <= 1; sd += 2) face(circlePts(8, 0.085, 0).map(function (q) { return [sd * 0.6 + q[0], 0.25 + q[1], -2.275]; }), [10, 10, 14], { n: [0, 0, -1], bias: 1 });
    // ruedas
    const WR = 0.37, WH = 0.15, wz = [1.38, -1.35];
    for (let sd = -1; sd <= 1; sd += 2) for (let wi = 0; wi < 2; wi++) {
      const cx = sd * 0.98, cy = WR, cz = wz[wi], ox = sd * (0.98 + WH);
      const ring = circlePts(14, WR, 0.2);
      for (let k = 0; k < 14; k++) {
        const q = ring[k], q2 = ring[(k + 1) % 14];
        face([[cx - sd * WH, cy + q[1], cz + q[0]], [cx + sd * WH, cy + q[1], cz + q[0]], [cx + sd * WH, cy + q2[1], cz + q2[0]], [cx - sd * WH, cy + q2[1], cz + q2[0]]], tire, { ref: [cx, cy, cz], bias: 0.5, kind: 'tread' });
      }
      face(ring.map(function (q) { return [ox, cy + q[1], cz + q[0]]; }), [20, 20, 24], { n: [sd, 0, 0], bias: 0.7, kind: 'tire' });
      face(circlePts(16, WR * 0.7, 0).map(function (q) { return [ox + sd * 0.002, cy + q[1], cz + q[0]]; }), shade(b.wheelCol, 0.3), { n: [sd, 0, 0], bias: 0.75, kind: 'rimbase' });
      const polys = wheelPolys(b.wheelStyle, spin * (wi ? 1 : 1));
      for (let pi2 = 0; pi2 < polys.length; pi2++) {
        const pl = polys[pi2];
        face(pl.p.map(function (q) { return [ox + sd * 0.004, cy + q[1] * WR, cz + q[0] * WR]; }), pl.dark ? [12, 12, 16] : b.wheelCol, { n: [sd, 0, 0], bias: 0.8, kind: pl.dark ? 'rimdark' : 'rim' });
      }
      if (b.wheelGlow) F.push({ pts: [[ox, cy, cz]], col: b.wheelCol, n: [sd, 0, 0], bias: 0.85, kind: 'glow', extra: { r: WR * 1.5 } });
    }
    // halos de faros
    F.push({ pts: [[-0.62, 0.4, 2.2]], col: hc, n: [0, 0, 1], bias: 1.1, kind: 'glow', extra: { r: 0.55, a: 0.45 } });
    F.push({ pts: [[0.62, 0.4, 2.2]], col: hc, n: [0, 0, 1], bias: 1.1, kind: 'glow', extra: { r: 0.55, a: 0.45 } });
    if (brake) {
      F.push({ pts: [[-0.66, rz - 0.2, -2.2]], col: tc, n: [0, 0, -1], bias: 1.1, kind: 'glow', extra: { r: 0.5, a: 0.4 } });
      F.push({ pts: [[0.66, rz - 0.2, -2.2]], col: tc, n: [0, 0, -1], bias: 1.1, kind: 'glow', extra: { r: 0.5, a: 0.4 } });
    }
    return F;
  }
  function drawCar3D(cx, cy, S, yaw, b, spin, op) {
    op = op || {};
    const pitch = op.pitch === undefined ? 0.24 : op.pitch, cp = Math.cos(pitch), sp = Math.sin(pitch), cyw = Math.cos(yaw), syw = Math.sin(yaw), D = op.D || 11;
    const faces = buildCar3D(b, spin, op.brake !== false);
    const view = function (p, flip) {
      const y0 = flip ? -p[1] : p[1], x1 = p[0] * cyw + p[2] * syw, z1 = -p[0] * syw + p[2] * cyw;
      return [x1, y0 * cp + z1 * sp, z1 * cp - y0 * sp];
    };
    const viewN = function (n, flip) { const y0 = flip ? -n[1] : n[1], x1 = n[0] * cyw + n[2] * syw, z1 = -n[0] * syw + n[2] * cyw; return [x1, y0 * cp + z1 * sp, z1 * cp - y0 * sp]; };
    const proj = function (v) { const k = S * D / (v[2] + D); return [cx + v[0] * k, cy - v[1] * k, k]; };
    const L = [-0.45, 0.75, -0.5], ll = Math.hypot(L[0], L[1], L[2]); L[0] /= ll; L[1] /= ll; L[2] /= ll;
    const f0 = 1 / DIM;
    const pass = function (flip, alpha) {
      const list = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < faces.length; i++) {
        const f = faces[i], n = viewN(f.n, flip), vp = f.pts.map(function (p) { return view(p, flip); });
        let zc = 0; for (let k = 0; k < vp.length; k++) zc += vp[k][2]; zc /= vp.length;
        if (n[0] * vp[0][0] + n[1] * vp[0][1] + n[2] * (vp[0][2] + D) >= 0) continue;
        list.push({ f: f, n: n, vp: vp, z: zc - f.bias });
      }
      list.sort(function (a, c) { return c.z - a.z; });
      ctx.save();
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
        const it = list[i], f = it.f, P = it.vp.map(proj);
        if (f.kind === 'glow') {
          const r = f.extra.r * P[0][2] * (flip ? 0.7 : 1), g = ctx.createRadialGradient(P[0][0], P[0][1], 0, P[0][0], P[0][1], r);
          g.addColorStop(0, css(f.col, (f.extra.a || 0.3) * alpha)); g.addColorStop(1, css(f.col, 0));
          ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(P[0][0], P[0][1], r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
          continue;
        }
        if (f.kind === 'plate' || f.kind === 'img') {
          const ex = f.extra, w = ex.w, h = ex.h, c0 = ex.c || [(f.pts[0][0] + f.pts[1][0]) / 2, (f.pts[0][1] + f.pts[2][1]) / 2, f.pts[0][2]];
          const n = f.n, up = [0, 1, 0], r = [-(up[1] * n[2] - up[2] * n[1]), -(up[2] * n[0] - up[0] * n[2]), -(up[0] * n[1] - up[1] * n[0])];
          const tl = [c0[0] - r[0] * w / 2, c0[1] + h / 2, c0[2] - r[2] * w / 2], tr = [c0[0] + r[0] * w / 2, c0[1] + h / 2, c0[2] + r[2] * w / 2], bl = [c0[0] - r[0] * w / 2, c0[1] - h / 2, c0[2] - r[2] * w / 2];
          const A = proj(view(tl, flip)), B = proj(view(tr, flip)), C = proj(view(bl, flip)), lw = 100, lh = 100 * h / w;
          ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
          ctx.transform((B[0] - A[0]) / lw, (B[1] - A[1]) / lw, (C[0] - A[0]) / lh, (C[1] - A[1]) / lh, A[0], A[1]);
          if (f.kind === 'plate') plateDraw(ctx, 0, 0, lw, lh, b.plate, b.plateStyle, 1, f0);
          else {
            const iw = b.img.width, ih = b.img.height, mode = IMG_FITS[b.imgSize | 0] ? IMG_FITS[b.imgSize | 0][1] : 'cover';
            ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(0, 0, lw, lh); ctx.clip();
            let dw = lw, dh = lh; if (mode !== 'stretch') { const k = mode === 'contain' ? Math.min(lw / iw, lh / ih) : Math.max(lw / iw, lh / ih); dw = iw * k; dh = ih * k; }
            ctx.drawImage(b.img, (lw - dw) / 2, (lh - dh) / 2, dw, dh);
          }
          ctx.restore();
          continue;
        }
        let lam = Math.max(0, it.n[0] * L[0] + it.n[1] * L[1] + it.n[2] * L[2]), c;
        if (f.emis) c = [Math.min(255, f.col[0] * 1.05), Math.min(255, f.col[1] * 1.05), Math.min(255, f.col[2] * 1.05)];
        else {
          const k = 0.5 + 0.7 * lam;
          c = [f.col[0] * k, f.col[1] * k, f.col[2] * k];
          if ((f.kind === 'body' || f.kind === 'cab' || f.kind === 'rim') && b.finish !== 'matte') { const sp2 = Math.pow(lam, b.finish === 'metal' ? 4 : 7) * (b.finish === 'metal' ? 0.6 : 0.42) * 255; c = [c[0] + sp2, c[1] + sp2, c[2] + sp2]; }
        }
        const fc = 'rgb(' + Math.max(0, Math.min(255, c[0] | 0)) + ',' + Math.max(0, Math.min(255, c[1] | 0)) + ',' + Math.max(0, Math.min(255, c[2] | 0)) + ')';
        ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
        ctx.fillStyle = fc; ctx.strokeStyle = fc; ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(P[0][0], P[0][1]); for (let k = 1; k < P.length; k++) ctx.lineTo(P[k][0], P[k][1]); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    };
    if (op.reflect) pass(true, 0.12);
    pass(false, 1);
    return { P: function (p) { return proj(view(p, false)); } };
  }
  // Escenario del garaje: plataforma giratoria, focos y reflejo
  function drawTurntable(cx, cy, S, yaw, b, pitchIn) {
    const pitch = pitchIn === undefined ? 0.24 : pitchIn, cp = Math.cos(pitch), sp = Math.sin(pitch), cyw = Math.cos(yaw), syw = Math.sin(yaw), D = 11;
    const P = function (x, y, z) { const x1 = x * cyw + z * syw, z1 = -x * syw + z * cyw, yy = y * cp + z1 * sp, zz = z1 * cp - y * sp, k = S * D / (zz + D); return [cx + x1 * k, cy - yy * k]; };
    const ring = function (r, y, n) { const o = []; for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { const a = k * TAU / n; o.push(P(Math.cos(a) * r, y, Math.sin(a) * r)); } return o; };
    const path = function (pts) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pts[0][0], pts[0][1]); for (let k = 1; k < pts.length; k++) ctx.lineTo(pts[k][0], pts[k][1]); ctx.closePath(); };
    const R = 3.4, glow = b.glow;
    // luz de neón en el suelo
    const c0 = P(0, 0, 0), gr = ctx.createRadialGradient(c0[0], c0[1], 1, c0[0], c0[1], S * R * 1.2);
    gr.addColorStop(0, css(glow, 0.55)); gr.addColorStop(0.6, css(glow, 0.15)); gr.addColorStop(1, css(glow, 0));
    ctx.fillStyle = gr; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(c0[0], c0[1] + S * 0.1, S * R * 1.25, S * R * 0.42, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    // canto de la plataforma
    const top = ring(R, 0, 48), bot = ring(R, -0.16, 48);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#10131c'; path(bot); ctx.fill();
    for (let k = 0; k < 48; k++) {
      const k2 = (k + 1) % 48, a = k * TAU / 48 + yaw;
      if (Math.sin(a + Math.PI / 2) < 0) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = (k % 2 ? '#1b2030' : '#232a3d'); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(top[k][0], top[k][1]); ctx.lineTo(top[k2][0], top[k2][1]); ctx.lineTo(bot[k2][0], bot[k2][1]); ctx.lineTo(bot[k][0], bot[k][1]); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    }
    // superficie
    const sg = ctx.createRadialGradient(c0[0], c0[1], 1, c0[0], c0[1], S * R);
    sg.addColorStop(0, '#2b3347'); sg.addColorStop(1, '#151a28');
    ctx.fillStyle = sg; path(top); ctx.fill();
    // marcas que giran con la plataforma
    ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.strokeStyle = css(glow, 0.9); path(ring(R - 0.08, 0.002, 64)); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.2; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)'; path(ring(R * 0.72, 0.002, 56)); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.16)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
    for (let k = 0; k < 24; k++) { const a = k * TAU / 24, a0 = P(Math.cos(a) * R * 0.72, 0.002, Math.sin(a) * R * 0.72), a1 = P(Math.cos(a) * (R - 0.14), 0.002, Math.sin(a) * (R - 0.14)); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(a0[0], a0[1]); ctx.lineTo(a1[0], a1[1]); ctx.stroke(); }
    ctx.fillStyle = css(glow, 0.9);
    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) { const a = k * TAU / 4, q = P(Math.cos(a) * (R - 0.08), 0.004, Math.sin(a) * (R - 0.08)); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(q[0], q[1], Math.max(2, S * 0.05), 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
  }
  function tabBtn(label, x, y, w, h, ui, sel, fn) {
    const hov = over(x, y, w, h);
    rr(ctx, x, y, w, h, 9 * ui);
    ctx.fillStyle = sel ? 'rgba(255,210,63,0.95)' : hov ? 'rgba(40,52,92,0.92)' : 'rgba(8,12,24,0.7)'; ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineWidth = (sel ? 2.5 : 1.5) * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = sel ? '#ffffff' : hov ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)'; ctx.stroke();
    txt(label, x + w / 2, y + h / 2 + 4.5 * ui, 12 * ui, sel ? '#101018' : '#ffffff', 'center', '900', false);
    hit(x, y, w, h, fn);
  }
  function randomPlate() {
    const A = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPRSTUVWXYZ', N = '0123456789';
    let t = ''; for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) t += N[(Math.random() * 10) | 0]; t += ' '; for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) t += A[(Math.random() * A.length) | 0];
    return t;
  }
  function drawGarage(ui) {
    const b = builds[gp], g = gp;
    const ox = (W - 1280 * ui) / 2, oy = (H - 720 * ui) / 2;
    const X = (v) => ox + v * ui, Y = (v) => oy + v * ui;
    // fondo de showroom
    const bgc = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H); bgc.addColorStop(0, '#04060d'); bgc.addColorStop(0.5, '#0a0f1e'); bgc.addColorStop(1, '#161c30');
    ctx.fillStyle = bgc; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    const wall = ctx.createRadialGradient(X(640), Y(290), 10, X(640), Y(290), 470 * ui); wall.addColorStop(0, css(b.glow, 0.3)); wall.addColorStop(1, css(b.glow, 0));
    ctx.fillStyle = wall; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    for (let sd = -1; sd <= 1; sd += 2) {
      const bg2 = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, Y(360)); bg2.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.13)'); bg2.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle = bg2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(X(640 + sd * 400), 0); ctx.lineTo(X(640 + sd * 460), 0); ctx.lineTo(X(640 + sd * 50), Y(360)); ctx.lineTo(X(640 - sd * 20), Y(360)); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)';
    for (let i = 0; i < 36; i++) {
      const px = X(380 + hash1(i, 3) * 520), py = Y(((hash1(i, 7) * 360 + tAnim * (8 + hash1(i, 9) * 14)) % 360) + 90), r = (0.8 + hash1(i, 11) * 1.6) * ui;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.2 + 0.3 * hash1(i, 13); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    txt('GARAJE', X(640), Y(58), 50 * ui, '#ffffff', 'center', '900');
    txt(garageFrom === 'lobby' ? 'Jugador ' + (g + 1) + ' · personaliza tu coche' : 'Personaliza tu coche: ruedas, luces, matrícula y más', X(640), Y(88), 16 * ui, garageFrom === 'lobby' ? css(MP_COLORS[g]) : '#cfe0ff', 'center', 'bold', false);
    // coche 3D girando
    const dtg = Math.min(0.1, Math.max(0, tAnim - gT)); gT = tAnim;
    if (!gDrag && tAnim > gHold) gYaw += dtg * 0.7;
    const S = 84 * ui, cx0 = X(640), cy0 = Y(360);
    drawTurntable(cx0, cy0, S, gYaw, b, 0.17);
    drawCar3D(cx0, cy0, S, gYaw, b, tAnim * 2.4, { pitch: 0.17, reflect: true, brake: true });
    arrow(X(414), Y(300), -1, ui, function () { changeSkin(-1); });
    arrow(X(866), Y(300), 1, ui, function () { changeSkin(1); });
    txt(b.name.toUpperCase() + (b.custom ? ' ✦' : ''), X(640), Y(478), 26 * ui, css(mixc(b.color, [255, 255, 255], 0.5)), 'center', '900');
    txt(b.custom ? 'Modificado por ti' : b.desc, X(640), Y(502), 14 * ui, '#cfe0ff', 'center', 'bold', false);
    txt('Arrastra con el ratón para girar el coche', X(640), Y(526), 12 * ui, '#7f95b8', 'center', 'bold', false);
    // Panel izquierdo con pestañas
    panel(X(40), Y(108), 340 * ui, 462 * ui, ui);
    const tabs = ['PINTURA', 'RUEDAS', 'LUCES', 'MATRÍCULA'], tws = [74, 72, 62, 92];
    for (let i = 0; i < tabs.length; i++) tabBtn(tabs[i], X(52 + [0, 78, 154, 220][i]), Y(118), tws[i] * ui, 30 * ui, ui, gTab === i, (function (k) { return function () { gTab = k; plateEdit = false; Sound.tone(520, 0.05, 0.1, 'square'); }; })(i));
    const lab = function (t, x, y) { txt(t, x, y, 13 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'left', '900', false); };
    if (gTab === 0) {
      lab('PINTURA', X(58), Y(176));
      swatches(PAINTS, b.color, X(54), Y(188), 8, 32 * ui, 8 * ui, ui, function (c) { b.color = c.slice(); touch(g); });
      lab('FRANJAS', X(58), Y(328));
      swatches(PAINTS, b.stripe, X(54), Y(340), 8, 32 * ui, 8 * ui, ui, function (c) { b.stripe = c.slice(); touch(g); });
    } else if (gTab === 1) {
      lab('ESTILO DE LLANTA', X(58), Y(176));
      for (let i = 0; i < WHEEL_STYLES.length; i++) {
        const wx = X(86) + i * 60 * ui, wy = Y(222), hov = over(wx - 26 * ui, wy - 26 * ui, 52 * ui, 52 * ui);
        drawWheelIcon(wx, wy, 22 * ui, i, b.wheelCol, b.wheelStyle === i, hov);
        hit(wx - 26 * ui, wy - 26 * ui, 52 * ui, 52 * ui, (function (k) { return function () { b.wheelStyle = k; saveBuilds(); }; })(i));
      }
      txt(WHEEL_STYLES[b.wheelStyle], X(210), Y(264), 12 * ui, '#cfe0ff', 'center', 'bold', false);
      lab('COLOR DE LLANTA', X(58), Y(296));

      button('LLANTAS CON LUZ: ' + (b.wheelGlow ? 'SÍ' : 'NO'), X(58), Y(400), 304 * ui, 36 * ui, ui, function () { b.wheelGlow = !b.wheelGlow; saveBuilds(); }, b.wheelGlow);
      txt('La luz usa el color de la llanta', X(210), Y(456), 12 * ui, '#7f95b8', 'center', 'bold', false);

      lab('LUCES TRASERAS', X(58), Y(248));
      swatches(TAIL_COLS, b.tailCol, X(54), Y(260), 8, 32 * ui, 8 * ui, ui, function (c) { b.tailCol = c.slice(); saveBuilds(); });
      lab('NEÓN BAJO EL COCHE', X(58), Y(320));
