    } else {
      lab('TU MATRÍCULA', X(58), Y(176));
      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(X(58), Y(190)); ctx.scale(3.04 * ui, 3.04 * ui);
      plateDraw(ctx, 0, 0, 100, 22, b.plate + (plateEdit && Math.floor(tAnim * 2) % 2 === 0 ? '|' : ''), b.plateStyle, 1, 1 / DIM); ctx.restore();
      const fx = X(58), fy = Y(268), fw = 304 * ui, fh = 40 * ui;
      rr(ctx, fx, fy, fw, fh, 10 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)'; ctx.fill();
      ctx.lineWidth = (plateEdit ? 3 : 1.5) * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = plateEdit ? '#ffd23f' : over(fx, fy, fw, fh) ? '#ffffff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)'; ctx.stroke();
      txt(plateEdit ? 'Escribe… (ENTER para terminar)' : 'Clic aquí y escribe el texto', fx + fw / 2, fy + fh / 2 + 5 * ui, 13 * ui, plateEdit ? '#ffd23f' : '#9fb4d6', 'center', 'bold', false);
      hit(fx, fy, fw, fh, function () { plateEdit = true; plateFresh = true; });
      lab('ESTILO', X(58), Y(336));
      for (let i = 0; i < PLATE_STYLES.length; i++) {
        const bx = X(58) + (i % 2) * 154 * ui, by = Y(346) + Math.floor(i / 2) * 44 * ui;
        button(PLATE_STYLES[i].name, bx, by, 150 * ui, 36 * ui, ui, (function (k) { return function () { b.plateStyle = k; saveBuilds(); }; })(i), b.plateStyle === i);
      }
      button('MATRÍCULA ALEATORIA', X(58), Y(442), 304 * ui, 34 * ui, ui, function () { b.plate = randomPlate(); saveBuilds(); });
      txt('Hasta 8 caracteres: letras, números y guion', X(210), Y(500), 12 * ui, '#7f95b8', 'center', 'bold', false);
    }
    // Panel derecho: estilo
    panel(X(900), Y(108), 340 * ui, 224 * ui, ui);
    lab('ESTILO', X(920), Y(136));
    const rx = X(920), rw = 300 * ui;
    button('CARROCERÍA: ' + (b.type === 'sedan' ? 'BERLINA' : 'DEPORTIVO'), rx, Y(148), rw, 36 * ui, ui, function () { b.type = b.type === 'sedan' ? 'player' : 'sedan'; touch(g); });
    button('FRANJAS: ' + nameOf(LIVERIES, b.livery), rx, Y(192), rw, 36 * ui, ui, function () { b.livery = cycle(LIVERIES, b.livery); touch(g); });
    button('ALERÓN: ' + (b.type === 'sedan' ? 'NO (BERLINA)' : b.wing ? 'SÍ' : 'NO'), rx, Y(236), rw, 36 * ui, ui, function () { if (b.type !== 'sedan') { b.wing = !b.wing; touch(g); } });
    button('ACABADO: ' + nameOf(FINISHES, b.finish), rx, Y(280), rw, 36 * ui, ui, function () { b.finish = cycle(FINISHES, b.finish); touch(g); });
    // Plantillas
    const sw = 38 * ui, sg = 10 * ui, n = SKINS.length, total = n * sw + (n - 1) * sg, sy0 = Y(596);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const x = W / 2 - total / 2 + i * (sw + sg), s = SKINS[i], sel = i === b.preset && !b.custom;
      rr(ctx, x, sy0, sw, sw, 9 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = css(s.color); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = css(s.stripe); ctx.fillRect(x + sw * 0.42, sy0 + 4 * ui, sw * 0.16, sw - 8 * ui);
      rr(ctx, x, sy0, sw, sw, 9 * ui); ctx.lineWidth = (sel ? 4 : over(x, sy0, sw, sw) ? 2.5 : 1.5) * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = sel ? '#ffd23f' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)'; ctx.stroke();
      hit(x, sy0, sw, sw, function () { applyPreset(g, i); });
    }
    txt('PLANTILLAS', X(640), Y(590), 11 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'center', 'bold', false);
    button('ALEATORIO', X(393), Y(650), 150 * ui, 40 * ui, ui, randomBuild);
    button('LISTO', X(555), Y(650), 170 * ui, 40 * ui, ui, closeGarage, true);
    button('RESTABLECER', X(737), Y(650), 150 * ui, 40 * ui, ui, function () { applyPreset(g, b.preset); resetExtras(builds[g]); saveBuilds(); });
    txt('← →: cambiar plantilla   ·   ENTER / ESC: volver', X(640), Y(708), 12 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'center', 'bold', false);
  }
  function polyF(pts, color) {
    ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(pts[0], pts[1]);
    for (let i = 2; i < pts.length; i += 2) ctx.lineTo(pts[i], pts[i + 1]);
    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  }
  function drawMapPreview(map, x, y, w, h, ui) {
    const th = map.themes[map.menuTheme], hz = y + h * 0.56, bot = y + h, cx = x + w / 2;
    ctx.save();
    rr(ctx, x, y, w, h, 12 * ui); ctx.clip();
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, y, 0, hz);
    g.addColorStop(0, css(th.skyTop)); g.addColorStop(1, css(th.skyBot));
    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, hz - y + 1);
    if (th.stars > 0.1) { ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + Math.min(1, th.stars) * 0.8 + ')'; for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) ctx.fillRect(x + hash1(i, 5) * w, y + hash1(i, 9) * (hz - y) * 0.8, 1.6 * ui, 1.6 * ui); }
    ctx.fillStyle = css(th.sun); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x + w * 0.76, y + h * th.sunY * 0.85, h * 0.05, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    // siluetas del fondo
    const sil = function (col, hMax, seed, cwid, lit) {
      ctx.fillStyle = css(col); ctx.beginPath();
      if (map.bg === 'city') {
        for (let bx = x - 4; bx < x + w; bx += cwid) ctx.rect(bx, hz - hMax * (0.25 + 0.75 * hash1(bx / cwid, seed)), cwid * 0.88, hMax + 2);
        ctx.fill();
        if (lit) {
          ctx.fillStyle = css(lit, 0.6); ctx.beginPath();
          for (let bx = x - 4, bi = 0; bx < x + w; bx += cwid, bi++) {
            const bh = hMax * (0.25 + 0.75 * hash1(bx / cwid, seed));
            for (let r = 0; r < Math.floor(bh / (3.4 * ui)); r++) for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) if (hash1(bi * 17 + r * 3 + k, seed + 3) < 0.38) ctx.rect(bx + 2 * ui + k * 3.2 * ui, hz - bh + 3 * ui + r * 3.4 * ui, 1.8 * ui, 1.8 * ui);
          }
          ctx.fill();
        }
      } else {
        ctx.moveTo(x, hz + 2);
        for (let bx = x; bx <= x + w; bx += 6) ctx.lineTo(bx, hz - hMax * (map.bg === 'hills' ? 0.45 : 1) * (map.bg === 'mesas' ? Math.min(0.8, 0.55 + 0.45 * Math.sin(bx * 0.045 / ui + seed) * Math.sin(bx * 0.013 / ui + seed * 2)) : (0.55 + 0.45 * Math.sin(bx * 0.045 / ui + seed) * Math.sin(bx * 0.013 / ui + seed * 2))));
        ctx.lineTo(x + w, hz + 2); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
      }
    };
    sil(th.m2, h * 0.3, 3, 15 * ui, th.neon);
    sil(th.m1, h * 0.2, 11, 12 * ui, [255, 214, 140]);
    ctx.fillStyle = css(th.grassL); ctx.fillRect(x, hz, w, bot - hz);
    // carretera en perspectiva
    const rb = w * 0.5, rt = w * 0.03;
    const U1 = function (z) { return (1 / z - 1 / 16) / (1 - 1 / 16); };
    const hwAt = function (u) { return rt + (rb - rt) * u; };
    if (map.runoff > 0) {
      const k = 1 + map.rumble * 2 + map.runoff;
      polyF([cx - rb * k, bot, cx - rt * k, hz, cx + rt * k, hz, cx + rb * k, bot], css(th.run));
    }
    const k2 = 1 + map.rumble * 2;
    for (let z = 1, i = 0; z < 16; i++) {
      const z2 = z * 1.28, ua = U1(z), ub = U1(z2), ya = hz + (bot - hz) * ua, yb = hz + (bot - hz) * ub;
      const wa = hwAt(ua), wb = hwAt(ub), rc = i % 2 ? th.rumbleD : th.rumbleL;
      polyF([cx - wa * k2, ya, cx - wa, ya, cx - wb, yb, cx - wb * k2, yb], css(rc));
      polyF([cx + wa, ya, cx + wa * k2, ya, cx + wb * k2, yb, cx + wb, yb], css(rc));
      z = z2;
    }
    polyF([cx - rb, bot, cx + rb, bot, cx + rt, hz, cx - rt, hz], css(th.roadL));
    if (map.edge) {
      polyF([cx - rb * 0.95, bot, cx - rb * 0.915, bot, cx - rt * 0.915, hz, cx - rt * 0.95, hz], css(th.neon));
      polyF([cx + rb * 0.915, bot, cx + rb * 0.95, bot, cx + rt * 0.95, hz, cx + rt * 0.915, hz], css(th.neon));
    }
    if (map.dash) {
      for (let z = 1.1; z < 15; z += 2.2) {
        const ua = U1(z), ub = U1(z + 0.9), ya = hz + (bot - hz) * ua, yb = hz + (bot - hz) * ub;
        [-1, 1].forEach(function (sd) {
          const xa = cx + sd * hwAt(ua) / 3, xb = cx + sd * hwAt(ub) / 3, da = hwAt(ua) * 0.012, db = hwAt(ub) * 0.012;
          polyF([xa - da, ya, xa + da, ya, xb + db, yb, xb - db, yb], css(th.lane));
        });
      }
    } else {
      // parrilla de salida y línea de meta
      const ua = U1(1.4), ub = U1(1.9), ya = hz + (bot - hz) * ua, yb = hz + (bot - hz) * ub, wa = hwAt(ua), wb = hwAt(ub);
      for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
        polyF([cx - wa + (2 * wa * i) / 12, ya, cx - wa + (2 * wa * (i + 1)) / 12, ya, cx - wb + (2 * wb * (i + 1)) / 12, yb, cx - wb + (2 * wb * i) / 12, yb], i % 2 ? '#f4f4f4' : '#15151a');
      }
    }
    // elementos laterales
    for (let z = 1.5, i = 0; z < 16; z *= 1.55, i++) {
      const u = U1(z), yy = hz + (bot - hz) * u, sz = 14 * ui + 150 * ui * u * u;
      [-1, 1].forEach(function (sd) {
        const px = cx + sd * (hwAt(u) * (map.id === 'f1' ? 1.5 : map.id === 'city' ? 1.55 : map.id === 'desert' ? 1.5 : 1.4) + sz * 0.6);
        if (map.id === 'highway') {
          polyF([px - sz * 0.4, yy, px + sz * 0.4, yy, px, yy - sz * 1.3], css(i % 2 ? [24, 100, 52] : [32, 124, 64]));
          ctx.fillStyle = css([84, 56, 34]); ctx.fillRect(px - sz * 0.05, yy, sz * 0.1, sz * 0.2);
        } else if (map.id === 'desert') {
          if (i % 2 === 0) {
            const cw2 = sz * 0.2;
            ctx.fillStyle = css([58, 128, 78]); rr(ctx, px - cw2 / 2, yy - sz * 1.4, cw2, sz * 1.4, cw2 / 2); ctx.fill();
            ctx.fillRect(px - sz * 0.38, yy - sz * 0.8, sz * 0.3, cw2 * 0.8); rr(ctx, px - sz * 0.4, yy - sz * 1.1, cw2 * 0.8, sz * 0.4, cw2 * 0.4); ctx.fill();
          } else {
            ctx.fillStyle = css([176, 96, 66]); polyF([px - sz * 0.7, yy, px - sz * 0.5, yy - sz * 1.2, px + sz * 0.5, yy - sz * 1.2, px + sz * 0.7, yy], css([176, 96, 66]));
            ctx.fillStyle = css([132, 70, 52]); ctx.fillRect(px + sz * 0.1, yy - sz * 1.2, sz * 0.4, sz * 1.2);
          }
        } else if (map.id === 'city') {
          const bw = sz * 0.8, bh = sz * (1.6 + 0.5 * hash1(i, sd + 3));
          ctx.fillStyle = css([30, 24, 66]); ctx.fillRect(px - bw / 2, yy - bh, bw, bh);
          ctx.fillStyle = css(th.neon); ctx.fillRect(px - bw / 2, yy - bh, bw, Math.max(1, bw * 0.05));
          ctx.fillStyle = css([255, 214, 140], 0.8);
          for (let r = 0; r < 4; r++) for (let c2 = 0; c2 < 3; c2++) if (hash1(r * 5 + c2, i + sd) < 0.5) ctx.fillRect(px - bw / 2 + bw * 0.14 + c2 * bw * 0.28, yy - bh + bh * 0.1 + r * bh * 0.2, bw * 0.14, bh * 0.08);
        } else {
          ctx.fillStyle = css([20, 20, 24]); ctx.fillRect(px - sz * 0.5, yy - sz * 0.3, sz, sz * 0.3);
          ctx.fillStyle = css(i % 2 ? [240, 240, 240] : [222, 36, 40]); ctx.fillRect(px - sz * 0.5, yy - sz * 0.3, sz, sz * 0.07);
          if (i % 2 === 0) {
            const gx = px + sd * sz * 1.1, gw = sz * 1.4;
            ctx.fillStyle = css([112, 116, 132]); ctx.fillRect(gx - gw / 2, yy - sz * 0.9, gw, sz * 0.9);
            ctx.fillStyle = css(CROWD[(i * 2 + (sd > 0 ? 1 : 0)) % CROWD.length]); ctx.fillRect(gx - gw / 2, yy - sz * 0.75, gw, sz * 0.12); ctx.fillRect(gx - gw / 2, yy - sz * 0.45, gw, sz * 0.12);
          }
        }
      });
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function drawMiniTrack(pts, x, y, size, ui) {
    if (!pts || !pts.length) return;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.lineJoin = 'round'; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    const path = function () { ctx.beginPath(); for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) { const px = x + 6 * ui + pts[i][0] * (size - 12 * ui), py = y + 6 * ui + pts[i][1] * (size - 12 * ui); if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(px, py); else ctx.lineTo(px, py); } ctx.closePath(); };
    path(); ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.7)'; ctx.lineWidth = 5 * ui; ctx.stroke();
    path(); ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)'; ctx.lineWidth = 2.4 * ui; ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function drawMaps(ui) {
    dim(0.6);
    txt('MAPAS', W / 2, H * 0.12, 56 * ui, '#ffffff', 'center', '900');
    txt('Elige dónde correr', W / 2, H * 0.12 + 28 * ui, 16 * ui, '#cfe0ff', 'center', 'bold', false);
    const gap = 22 * ui, cw = Math.min(340 * ui, (W * 0.95 - (MAPS.length - 1) * gap) / MAPS.length), ch = 392 * ui, total = MAPS.length * cw + (MAPS.length - 1) * gap, y0 = H * 0.19;
    for (let i = 0; i < MAPS.length; i++) {
      const m = MAPS[i], x = W / 2 - total / 2 + i * (cw + gap), sel = i === mapIdx;
      rr(ctx, x, y0, cw, ch, 16 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = sel ? 'rgba(10,14,30,0.9)' : 'rgba(8,12,24,0.68)'; ctx.fill();
      drawMapPreview(m, x + 12 * ui, y0 + 12 * ui, cw - 24 * ui, 170 * ui, ui);
      drawMiniTrack(m.pts, x + cw - 12 * ui - 62 * ui, y0 + 12 * ui + 170 * ui - 62 * ui - 4 * ui, 62 * ui, ui);
      if (sel) {
        rr(ctx, x + 22 * ui, y0 + 22 * ui, 118 * ui, 22 * ui, 11 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = '#ffd23f'; ctx.fill();
        txt('SELECCIONADO', x + 22 * ui + 59 * ui, y0 + 38 * ui, 11 * ui, '#101018', 'center', '900', false);
      }
      txt(m.name.toUpperCase(), x + cw / 2, y0 + 226 * ui, 26 * ui, sel ? '#ffd23f' : '#ffffff', 'center', '900');
      txt(m.desc[0], x + cw / 2, y0 + 250 * ui, 13 * ui, '#cfe0ff', 'center', 'bold', false);
      txt(m.desc[1], x + cw / 2, y0 + 268 * ui, 13 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'center', 'bold', false);
      const rows = [
        ['Longitud', (m.len * 0.00625 / 1000).toFixed(1) + ' km'],
        ['Dificultad', '★★★'.slice(0, m.diff) + '☆☆☆'.slice(0, 3 - m.diff)],
        ['Ambiente', m.ambient],
        ['Récord', thousands(sel ? best : (bests[m.id] || 0))]
      ];
      for (let r = 0; r < rows.length; r++) {
        txt(rows[r][0], x + 24 * ui, y0 + (302 + r * 22) * ui, 14 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'left', 'bold', false);
        txt(rows[r][1], x + cw - 24 * ui, y0 + (302 + r * 22) * ui, 15 * ui, r === 3 ? '#ffd23f' : '#ffffff', 'right', '900', false);
      }
      rr(ctx, x, y0, cw, ch, 16 * ui); ctx.lineWidth = (sel ? 4 : over(x, y0, cw, ch) ? 3 : 1.5) * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = sel ? '#ffd23f' : over(x, y0, cw, ch) ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.25)'; ctx.stroke();
      hit(x, y0, cw, ch, function () { if (mapIdx !== i) selectMap(i); });
    }
    button('LISTO', W / 2 - 80 * ui, y0 + ch + 20 * ui, 160 * ui, 40 * ui, ui, closeMaps, true);
    txt('← → / A D: cambiar   ·   ENTER / N / ESC: volver', W / 2, H - 18 * ui, 14 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'center', 'bold', false);
  }
  function drawPause(ui) {
    dim(0.6);
    txt('PAUSA', W / 2, H * 0.42, 90 * ui, '#ffffff', 'center', '900');
    txt('P / ENTER: continuar  ·  R: reiniciar  ·  Q: menú  ·  ESC: salir', W / 2, H * 0.42 + 46 * ui, 18 * ui, '#cfe0ff', 'center', 'bold', false);
    const by = H * 0.42 + 76 * ui;
    button('CONTINUAR  [P]', W / 2 - 267 * ui, by, 170 * ui, 40 * ui, ui, resumeGame, true);
    button('REINICIAR  [R]', W / 2 - 85 * ui, by, 170 * ui, 40 * ui, ui, startGame);
    button('MENÚ  [Q]', W / 2 + 97 * ui, by, 170 * ui, 40 * ui, ui, goMenu);
    if (!focused()) txt('Haz clic en esta pantalla para recuperar el teclado', W / 2, by + 78 * ui, 18 * ui, '#ff9d2e', 'center', 'bold', false);
  }
  function drawOver(ui) {
    dim(0.62);
    const ch = isChase();
    txt(ch ? '¡ARRESTADO!' : 'TIEMPO AGOTADO', W / 2, H * 0.3, Math.min(80 * ui, W / 10), ch ? '#6fa8ff' : '#ff5d5d', 'center', '900');
    const rows = ch ? [['Puntuación', thousands(score())], ['Distancia', (dist / 1000).toFixed(2) + ' km'], ['Tiempo en fuga', fmtT(chase.t)], ['Nivel de búsqueda', '★'.repeat(chase.level)], ['Adelantamientos', String(overtakes)]]
      : [['Puntuación', thousands(score())], ['Distancia', (dist / 1000).toFixed(2) + ' km'], ['Vueltas', String(lap)], ['Adelantamientos', String(overtakes)]];
    if (!mpOn) rows.push(['Monedas ganadas', '+' + thousands(runCoins)]);
    const pw = 400 * ui, ph = (rows.length * 34 + 32) * ui, px = W / 2 - pw / 2, py = H * 0.3 + 30 * ui;
    panel(px, py, pw, ph, ui);
    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
      txt(rows[i][0], px + 26 * ui, py + (42 + i * 34) * ui, 17 * ui, '#9fb4d6', 'left', 'bold', false);
      txt(rows[i][1], px + pw - 26 * ui, py + (42 + i * 34) * ui, 20 * ui, '#ffffff', 'right', '900');
    }
    if (newRecord) txt('¡NUEVO RÉCORD!', W / 2, py + ph + 40 * ui, 30 * ui, '#ffd23f', 'center', '900');
    else txt('Récord: ' + thousands(best), W / 2, py + ph + 36 * ui, 18 * ui, '#ffd23f', 'center', 'bold', false);
    button('GARAJE  [C]', W / 2 - 235 * ui, py + ph + 104 * ui, 150 * ui, 38 * ui, ui, openGarage);
    button('MAPAS  [N]', W / 2 - 75 * ui, py + ph + 104 * ui, 150 * ui, 38 * ui, ui, openMaps);
    button('MENÚ  [Q]', W / 2 + 85 * ui, py + ph + 104 * ui, 150 * ui, 38 * ui, ui, goMenu, true);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.55 + 0.45 * Math.sin(tAnim * 4);
    txt('ENTER / clic: jugar de nuevo  ·  Q: menú  ·  ESC: salir', W / 2, py + ph + 84 * ui, 20 * ui, '#ffffff', 'center', 'bold');
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  Render principal
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  function sceneTheme() {
    cur = themeT >= 1 ? themeTo : blendTheme(themeFrom, themeTo, themeT * themeT * (3 - 2 * themeT));
    C = {
      roadL: css(cur.roadL), roadD: css(cur.roadD), grassL: css(cur.grassL), grassD: css(cur.grassD),
      rumbleL: css(cur.rumbleL), rumbleD: css(cur.rumbleD), lane: css(cur.lane), fog: css(cur.fog),
      run: css(cur.run), runD: css(shade(cur.run, 0.9)), edge: css(cur.neon)
    };
    FOG = cur.fog; DIM = 1 - 0.4 * cur.night;
  }
  function renderScene() {
    const sx = cfg.shake && shake > 0.3 ? (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * shake : 0, sy = cfg.shake && shake > 0.3 ? (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * shake : 0;
    const fp = fpv && (state === 'countdown' || state === 'play' || state === 'pause' || state === 'over');
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(sx, sy);
    if (fp) { ctx.translate(W / 2, H * 0.46); ctx.scale(FPV_ZOOM, FPV_ZOOM); ctx.translate(-W / 2, -H / 2); }
    drawBackground();
    drawWorld();
    ctx.restore();
    drawWeather();
    postFX();
  }
  function scoreOf(p) { return Math.floor(p.dist) + p.bonusScore; }
  // ---- Primera persona ----
  function toggleFpv(i) {
    if (mpOn || state !== 'play' && state !== 'countdown' && state !== 'pause') return;
    fpv = !fpv;
    Sound.tone(fpv ? 700 : 480, 0.06, 0.12, 'square');
  }
  function drawCockpit(ui) {
    const b = builds[mpOn && PL.length ? curP : 0], col = b.color;
    const cx = W / 2, steer = steerVis * 0.7, kmh = Math.round(speed * KMH);
    ctx.save();
    // luna: leve tinte arriba y reflejo
    const tg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H * 0.3); tg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,10,25,0.38)'); tg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,10,25,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = tg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H * 0.3);
    // capó (más bajo y estrecho): color del coche + reflejo del cielo
    const hg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, H * 0.8, 0, H); hg.addColorStop(0, css(shade(col, 1.3))); hg.addColorStop(0.4, css(col)); hg.addColorStop(1, css(shade(col, 0.5)));
    ctx.fillStyle = hg;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(W * 0.1, H); ctx.bezierCurveTo(W * 0.2, H * 0.9, W * 0.3, H * 0.82, W * 0.4, H * 0.805);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W * 0.5, H * 0.795, W * 0.6, H * 0.805); ctx.bezierCurveTo(W * 0.7, H * 0.82, W * 0.8, H * 0.9, W * 0.9, H); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    ctx.save(); ctx.clip();
    const rf = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, H * 0.8, 0, H * 0.88); rf.addColorStop(0, css(cur.skyBot, 0.55)); rf.addColorStop(1, css(cur.skyBot, 0));
    ctx.fillStyle = rf; ctx.fillRect(0, H * 0.8, W, H * 0.09);
    if (b.stripe && b.livery !== 'none') {
      ctx.fillStyle = css(b.stripe, 0.92);
      const sw = b.livery === 'dual' ? [[-0.04, 0.018], [0.022, 0.018]] : b.livery === 'thin' ? [[-0.035, 0.01], [-0.005, 0.01], [0.025, 0.01]] : b.livery === 'edge' ? [[-0.1, 0.012], [0.088, 0.012]] : [[-0.022, 0.044]];
      for (let i = 0; i < sw.length; i++) {
        const o0 = sw[i][0], ww = sw[i][1];
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx + W * o0 * 2.2, H); ctx.lineTo(cx + W * o0 * 0.9, H * 0.8); ctx.lineTo(cx + W * (o0 + ww) * 0.9, H * 0.8); ctx.lineTo(cx + W * (o0 + ww) * 2.2, H); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
      }
    }
    ctx.restore();
    // salpicadero
    const dg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, H * 0.82, 0, H); dg.addColorStop(0, '#20232e'); dg.addColorStop(1, '#06070b');
    ctx.fillStyle = dg;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, H); ctx.lineTo(0, H * 0.915); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W * 0.5, H * 0.8, W, H * 0.915); ctx.lineTo(W, H); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.14)'; ctx.lineWidth = 2 * ui; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, H * 0.915); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W * 0.5, H * 0.8, W, H * 0.915); ctx.stroke();
    // montantes (finos), techo y parasoles
    const pg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, W * 0.07, 0); pg.addColorStop(0, '#05060a'); pg.addColorStop(1, '#161922');
    ctx.fillStyle = pg;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, 0); ctx.lineTo(W * 0.075, 0); ctx.lineTo(W * 0.03, H * 0.62); ctx.lineTo(W * 0.012, H * 0.96); ctx.lineTo(0, H * 0.96); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    const pg2 = ctx.createLinearGradient(W, 0, W * 0.93, 0); pg2.addColorStop(0, '#05060a'); pg2.addColorStop(1, '#161922');
    ctx.fillStyle = pg2;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(W, 0); ctx.lineTo(W * 0.925, 0); ctx.lineTo(W * 0.97, H * 0.62); ctx.lineTo(W * 0.988, H * 0.96); ctx.lineTo(W, H * 0.96); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    const rg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H * 0.09); rg.addColorStop(0, '#05060a'); rg.addColorStop(1, '#14161f');
    ctx.fillStyle = rg;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, 0); ctx.lineTo(W, 0); ctx.lineTo(W, H * 0.02); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W * 0.5, H * 0.085, 0, H * 0.02); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    // retrovisores laterales
    for (let sd = -1; sd <= 1; sd += 2) {
      const mx = cx + sd * W * 0.43, my = H * 0.66, mw = W * 0.05, mh = H * 0.07;
      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(mx, my); ctx.rotate(sd * 0.12);
      rr(ctx, -mw / 2, -mh / 2, mw, mh, mh * 0.3); ctx.fillStyle = css(shade(col, 0.55)); ctx.fill();
      rr(ctx, -mw / 2 + 3 * ui, -mh / 2 + 3 * ui, mw - 6 * ui, mh - 6 * ui, mh * 0.22);
      const sg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, -mh / 2, 0, mh / 2); sg.addColorStop(0, css(cur.skyBot, 0.9)); sg.addColorStop(1, css(cur.roadL, 0.9)); ctx.fillStyle = sg; ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
    // retrovisor interior
    const mw = Math.min(W * 0.17, 170 * ui), mh = mw * 0.24, mx = cx - mw / 2, my = H * 0.03;
    rr(ctx, mx, my, mw, mh, mh * 0.4); ctx.fillStyle = '#090b10'; ctx.fill();
    const mg = ctx.createLinearGradient(mx, my, mx, my + mh); mg.addColorStop(0, css(cur.skyBot, 0.9)); mg.addColorStop(0.6, css(cur.skyBot, 0.9)); mg.addColorStop(1, css(cur.roadL, 0.95));
    rr(ctx, mx + 3 * ui, my + 3 * ui, mw - 6 * ui, mh - 6 * ui, mh * 0.3); ctx.fillStyle = mg; ctx.fill();
    // cuadro de instrumentos (detrás del volante) con velocidad digital
    const cw2 = Math.min(W * 0.14, 165 * ui), ch2 = Math.min(H * 0.08, 52 * ui), cy2 = H * 0.875;
    rr(ctx, cx - cw2 / 2, cy2 - ch2 / 2, cw2, ch2, ch2 * 0.35); ctx.fillStyle = '#05060a'; ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.5 * ui; ctx.stroke();
    const fr = U.limit(kmh / 500, 0, 1), bw0 = cw2 * 0.84, bx0 = cx - bw0 / 2, by0 = cy2 + ch2 * 0.28;
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.1)'; ctx.fillRect(bx0, by0, bw0, 4 * ui);
    ctx.fillStyle = nitroOn ? '#ff9d2e' : '#2fd8ff'; ctx.fillRect(bx0, by0, bw0 * fr, 4 * ui);
    txt(String(kmh), cx - cw2 * 0.04, cy2 + ch2 * 0.16, ch2 * 0.55, '#ffffff', 'center', '900', false);
    txt('KM/H', cx + cw2 * 0.3, cy2 + ch2 * 0.12, ch2 * 0.2, '#9fb4d6', 'center', 'bold', false);
    // volante: más pequeño y más lejos, con las manos
    const R = Math.min(W * 0.17, H * 0.27);
    ctx.translate(cx, H + R * 0.3); ctx.rotate(steer);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#0b0d13'; ctx.lineWidth = R * 0.14; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, R, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.13)'; ctx.lineWidth = R * 0.03; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, R * 1.05, Math.PI * 1.12, Math.PI * 1.88); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#0b0d13'; ctx.lineWidth = R * 0.1;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-R, 0); ctx.lineTo(R, 0); ctx.moveTo(0, 0); ctx.lineTo(0, R); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = css(b.glow); ctx.fillRect(-R * 0.05, -R * 1.07, R * 0.1, R * 0.14);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#11131a'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, R * 0.22, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    for (let hd = -1; hd <= 1; hd += 2) {
      const ang = -Math.PI / 2 + hd * 0.5, hx = Math.cos(ang) * R, hy = Math.sin(ang) * R;
      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(hx, hy); ctx.rotate(ang + Math.PI / 2);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#23252e'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 0, R * 0.12, R * 0.16, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-R * 0.03, -R * 0.04, R * 0.05, R * 0.07, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function drawFpvBtn(ui, off) {
    const sz = 46 * ui, x = W / 2 - sz / 2, y = 12 * ui, hov = over(off + x, y, sz, sz), on = mpOn && PL.length === 2 ? PL[curP].fpv : fpv;
    rr(ctx, x, y, sz, sz, 12 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = hov ? 'rgba(40,52,92,0.9)' : 'rgba(8,12,24,0.65)'; ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineWidth = (on ? 3 : 1.5) * ui; ctx.strokeStyle = on ? '#ffd23f' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.45)'; ctx.stroke();
    const cx = x + sz / 2, cy = y + sz / 2, r = sz * 0.3;
    ctx.strokeStyle = on ? '#ffd23f' : '#ffffff'; ctx.lineWidth = 2.4 * ui;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx - r, cy); ctx.lineTo(cx + r, cy); ctx.moveTo(cx, cy); ctx.lineTo(cx, cy + r); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy, r * 0.22, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
    txt('V', W / 2, y + sz + 14 * ui, 11 * ui, '#cfe0ff', 'center', 'bold', false);
    const who = mpOn ? curP : 0;
    hit(off + x, y, sz, sz, function () { toggleFpv(who); });
  }
  function drawHUDmp(ui, p) {
    const col = MP_COLORS[p], x0 = 12 * ui, y0 = 12 * ui, mine = scoreOf(PL[p]), other = scoreOf(PL[1 - p]);
    panel(x0, y0, 190 * ui, 112 * ui, ui);
    rr(ctx, x0, y0, 6 * ui, 112 * ui, 3 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = css(col); ctx.fill();
    txt('JUGADOR ' + (p + 1), x0 + 18 * ui, y0 + 24 * ui, 13 * ui, css(col), 'left', '900');
    const low = time < 10 && state === 'play';
    const pulse = low ? (0.6 + 0.4 * Math.sin(tAnim * 10)) : 1;
    txt(time.toFixed(1), x0 + 18 * ui, y0 + 66 * ui, 40 * ui, low ? 'rgb(255,' + ((80 + 90 * pulse) | 0) + ',70)' : '#ffffff', 'left', '900');
    txt(thousands(mine) + ' pts', x0 + 18 * ui, y0 + 98 * ui, 20 * ui, '#ffd23f', 'left', '900');
    const lead = mine >= other;
    rr(ctx, W - 12 * ui - 78 * ui, y0, 78 * ui, 54 * ui, 12 * ui); ctx.fillStyle = lead ? 'rgba(255,210,63,0.92)' : 'rgba(8,12,24,0.62)'; ctx.fill();
    txt(lead ? '1º' : '2º', W - 12 * ui - 39 * ui, y0 + 40 * ui, 34 * ui, lead ? '#101018' : '#9fb4d6', 'center', '900');
    txt('VUELTA ' + lap, W - 12 * ui - 39 * ui, y0 + 76 * ui, 14 * ui, '#ffffff', 'center', '900');
    drawSpeedo(ui * 0.8);
    drawNitroBar(ui * 0.8);
    drawOverlays(ui * 0.8);
    if (PL[p].out) {
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(4,6,16,0.6)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      txt('TIEMPO AGOTADO', W / 2, H * 0.42, Math.min(40 * ui, W / 10), '#ff5d5d', 'center', '900');
      txt(thousands(mine) + ' puntos', W / 2, H * 0.42 + 36 * ui, 22 * ui, '#ffd23f', 'center', '900');
      txt('Esperando al otro jugador…', W / 2, H * 0.42 + 64 * ui, 14 * ui, '#cfe0ff', 'center', 'bold', false);
    }
  }
  function renderMP() {
    const fullW = W, half = Math.floor(fullW / 2), ui = U.limit(Math.min(fullW / 1280, H / 720), 0.6, 2);
    const vui = U.limit(Math.min(half / 700, H / 720), 0.55, 1.15);
    mpView = true;
    for (let p = 0; p < 2; p++) {
      loadP(p); W = half;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(p * half, 0);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(0, 0, half, H); ctx.clip();
      sceneTheme(); PL[p].cur = cur;
      renderScene();
      drawHUDmp(vui, p);
      ctx.restore();
    }
    W = fullW; mpView = false;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#05070f'; ctx.fillRect(half - 3, 0, 6, H);
    ctx.fillStyle = css(MP_COLORS[0]); ctx.fillRect(half - 3, 0, 3, H);
    ctx.fillStyle = css(MP_COLORS[1]); ctx.fillRect(half, 0, 3, H);
    if (state === 'pause') drawPause(ui);
    else if (state === 'over') drawOverMP(ui);
  }
  let bgCv = null, bgCx = null, bgFlip = false, fpsN = 0, fpsT = 0, fpsShown = 0;
  function render() {
    if (W < 10 || H < 10) return;
    ctx.setTransform(DPR, 0, 0, DPR, 0, 0);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    hits.length = 0;
    if (mpOn && PL.length === 2 && (state === 'play' || state === 'countdown' || state === 'pause' || state === 'over')) renderMP();
    else {
      sceneTheme();
      const ui = U.limit(Math.min(W / 1280, H / 720), 0.6, 2);
      if (state === 'menu' || state === 'garage' || state === 'maps' || state === 'controls' || state === 'lobby' || state === 'name' || state === 'modes' || state === 'diff' || state === 'settings' || state === 'shop') {
        // Fondo de los menús: se dibuja a menor resolución y a la mitad de fotogramas (los menús lo oscurecen igualmente)
        const q = 0.6, tw = Math.max(1, Math.floor(W * DPR * q)), th = Math.max(1, Math.floor(H * DPR * q));
        if (!bgCv) { bgCv = document.createElement('canvas'); bgCx = bgCv.getContext('2d', { alpha: false }); }
        let dirty = false;
        if (bgCv.width !== tw || bgCv.height !== th) { bgCv.width = tw; bgCv.height = th; dirty = true; }
        bgFlip = !bgFlip;
        if (bgFlip || dirty) {
          ctx = bgCx; ctx.setTransform(DPR * q, 0, 0, DPR * q, 0, 0); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
          try { renderScene(); if (state === 'menu') menuStatic(); } finally { ctx = mainCtx; }
        }
        ctx.drawImage(bgCv, 0, 0, W, H);
      } else renderScene();
      if (state !== 'menu' && state !== 'garage' && state !== 'maps' && state !== 'controls' && state !== 'lobby' && state !== 'name' && state !== 'modes' && state !== 'diff' && state !== 'settings' && state !== 'shop') drawHUD(ui);
      if (state === 'menu') drawMenu(ui);
      else if (state === 'garage') drawGarage(ui);
      else if (state === 'maps') drawMaps(ui);
      else if (state === 'controls') drawControls(ui);
      else if (state === 'lobby') drawLobby(ui);
      else if (state === 'name') drawName(ui);
      else if (state === 'modes') drawModes(ui);
      else if (state === 'diff') drawDiff(ui);
      else if (state === 'settings') drawSettings(ui);
      else if (state === 'shop') drawShop(ui);
      else if (state === 'pause') drawPause(ui);
      else if (state === 'over') drawOver(ui);
    }
    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.font = 'bold 10px Arial,sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'right'; ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.35)'; ctx.fillText(fpsShown + ' FPS', W - 7, 13);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)'; ctx.fillText(fpsShown + ' FPS', W - 8, 12);
    ctx.restore();
    let hov = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < hits.length; i++) if (over(hits[i].x, hits[i].y, hits[i].w, hits[i].h)) { hov = true; break; }
    canvas.style.cursor = hov ? 'pointer' : 'default';
  }
  function audioFrame() {
    if (!Sound.ac) return;
    if (mpOn && PL.length === 2 && (state === 'play' || state === 'countdown' || state === 'pause' || state === 'over')) loadP(PL[0].speed >= PL[1].speed ? 0 : 1);
    const sp = speed / topSpeed, gi = gearInfo();
    const freq = 55 + gi.rpm * 105 + (gi.gear - 1) * 9;
    let vol = 0.05 + 0.03 * Math.min(sp, 1.2) + (keys.up ? 0.015 : 0);
    if (state === 'pause') vol = 0; else if (state === 'menu' || state === 'garage' || state === 'maps' || state === 'controls' || state === 'lobby' || state === 'name' || state === 'modes' || state === 'diff' || state === 'settings' || state === 'shop') vol = 0.02;
    const wind = (state === 'play' || state === 'menu' || state === 'garage' || state === 'maps' || state === 'controls' || state === 'lobby' || state === 'name' || state === 'modes' || state === 'diff' || state === 'settings' || state === 'shop') ? Math.min(0.1, sp * 0.08 + (nitroOn ? 0.05 : 0)) : 0;
    Sound.update(freq, vol, wind, 500 + sp * 1200);
    Sound.musicTick(curMap.id, (state === 'pause' ? 0.25 : 0.8) * cfg.music);
    let sv = 0;
    if (isChase() && state === 'play' && chase.cops.length) {
      let md = 1e9;
      for (let i = 0; i < chase.cops.length; i++) md = Math.min(md, Math.abs(copGap(chase.cops[i])));
      sv = U.limit(1 - md / 9000, 0, 1) * 0.045;
    }
    Sound.sirenUpdate(sv);
    Sound.rainUpdate(cfg.weather && WEATHERS[weatherIdx].id === 'rain' && state !== 'pause' ? 0.05 : 0);
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  Bucle principal
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  function frame(now) {
    if (destroyed) return;
    raf = window.requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    try {
      fpsN++; if (now - fpsT >= 500) { fpsShown = Math.round(fpsN * 1000 / (now - fpsT)); fpsN = 0; fpsT = now; }
      let dt = (now - last) / 1000; last = now;
      if (!(dt > 0)) dt = 0;
      if (dt > 0.25) dt = 0.25;
      if ((state === 'play' || state === 'countdown') && !focused()) pauseGame();
      if (state === 'pause') acc = 0;
      else {
        acc += dt;
        let guard = 0;
        while (acc >= STEP && guard < 10) { tick(STEP); acc -= STEP; guard++; }
        if (guard >= 10) acc = 0;
      }
      render();
      audioFrame();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[Turbo Highway] Error inesperado:', err);
      destroy();
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  Entrada
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  const KEYMAP = {
    ArrowLeft: 'left', KeyA: 'left', ArrowRight: 'right', KeyD: 'right',
    ArrowUp: 'up', KeyW: 'up', ArrowDown: 'down', KeyS: 'down',
    Space: 'nitro', ShiftLeft: 'nitro', ShiftRight: 'nitro'
  };
  function onKeyDown(e) {
    if (state === 'shop' && codeEdit) {
      const k = e.key;
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); if (e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      if (k === 'Enter') { if (!e.repeat) redeemCode(); return; }
      if (k === 'Escape') { if (!e.repeat) codeEdit = false; return; }
      if (k === 'Backspace') { codeText = codeText.slice(0, -1); return; }
      if (typeof k === 'string' && k.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey && codeText.length < 28 && k !== ' ') codeText += k;
      return;
    }
    if (state === 'settings' && nameEditS) {
      const k = e.key;
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); if (e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      if (k === 'Enter' || k === 'Escape') { if (!e.repeat) saveName(); return; }
      if (k === 'Backspace') { playerName = nameFresh ? '' : playerName.slice(0, -1); nameFresh = false; return; }
      if (typeof k === 'string' && k.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
        if (nameFresh) { playerName = ''; nameFresh = false; }
        if (playerName.length < 12 && (k !== ' ' || playerName.length > 0)) playerName += k;
      }
      return;
    }
    if (state === 'garage' && plateEdit) {
      const k = e.key, pb = builds[gp];
      e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); if (e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      if (k === 'Enter' || k === 'Escape') { if (!e.repeat) { plateEdit = false; saveBuilds(); } return; }
      if (k === 'Backspace') { pb.plate = plateFresh ? '' : pb.plate.slice(0, -1); plateFresh = false; return; }
      if (typeof k === 'string' && k.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
        const ch = k.toUpperCase();
        if (plateFresh) { pb.plate = ''; plateFresh = false; }
        if (/[A-Z0-9 \-]/.test(ch) && pb.plate.length < 8 && (ch !== ' ' || pb.plate.length > 0)) pb.plate += ch;
      }
      return;
    }
    if (state === 'name' || (state === 'lobby' && nameEdit >= 0)) {
      const k = e.key, lob = state === 'lobby';
      const stop = function () { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); if (e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation(); };
      if (k === 'Enter' || k === 'Escape') { stop(); if (!e.repeat) { if (lob) endNameEdit(); else if (k === 'Enter') confirmName(); else cancelName(); } return; }
      if (!lob && (k === 'ArrowLeft' || k === 'ArrowRight')) { stop(); viewPref = viewPref === 'first' ? 'third' : 'first'; return; }
      if (lob && k === 'Tab') { stop(); if (!e.repeat) nameEdit = 1 - nameEdit; return; }
      if (k === 'Backspace') { stop(); if (lob) mpNames[nameEdit] = mpNames[nameEdit].slice(0, -1); else nameDraft = nameDraft.slice(0, -1); return; }
      if (typeof k === 'string' && k.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {
        stop();
        const cur = lob ? mpNames[nameEdit] : nameDraft;
        if (cur.length < 12 && (k !== ' ' || cur.length > 0)) { if (lob) mpNames[nameEdit] = cur + k; else nameDraft = cur + k; }
        return;
      }
      return;
    }
    const code = e.code, mapped = KEYMAP[code], mk = mpOn ? MPKEYS[code] : null;
    const special = code === 'Enter' || code === 'NumpadEnter' || code === 'Escape' || code === 'KeyP' || code === 'KeyM' || code === 'KeyR' || code === 'KeyC' || code === 'KeyN' || code === 'KeyQ' || code === 'KeyH' || code === 'KeyV';
    if (!mapped && !special && !mk) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    if (e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    Sound.init(); Sound.resume();
    if (code === 'KeyV') { if (!e.repeat) toggleFpv(0); return; }
    if (state === 'diff') {
      if (!e.repeat) {
        if (code === 'Enter' || code === 'NumpadEnter' || code === 'Space') confirmDiff();
        else if (code === 'Escape') closeDiff();
        else if (mapped === 'up') changeDiff(-1);
        else if (mapped === 'down') changeDiff(1);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (code === 'KeyH') { if (!e.repeat && (state === 'play' || state === 'countdown')) Sound.hornOn(); return; }
    if (state === 'garage') {
      if (!e.repeat) {
        if (mapped === 'left') changeSkin(-1);
        else if (mapped === 'right') changeSkin(1);
        else if (code === 'Space') closeGarage();
      }
      if (mapped) return;
    }
    if (mk && PL.length === 2 && (state === 'play' || state === 'countdown' || state === 'pause' || state === 'over')) {
      if (state === 'pause') { if (code === 'Space') resumeGame(); return; }
      if (state === 'over') { if (code === 'Space' && !e.repeat) startGame(); return; }
      PL[mk[0]].keys[mk[1]] = true;
      return;
    }
    if (state === 'lobby') {
      if (!e.repeat) {
        if (code === 'Enter' || code === 'NumpadEnter') lobbyStart();
        else if (code === 'Escape') closeLobby();
      }
      return;
    }
    if (state === 'controls') {
      if (!e.repeat && (code === 'Enter' || code === 'NumpadEnter' || code === 'Escape' || code === 'Space')) closeControls();
      return;
    }
    if (state === 'maps') {
      if (!e.repeat) {
        if (mapped === 'left') changeMap(-1);
        else if (mapped === 'right') changeMap(1);
        else if (code === 'Space') closeMaps();
      }
      if (mapped) return;
    }
    if (mapped) {
      if (code === 'Space' && state === 'menu') { openModes(); return; }
      if (code === 'Space' && state === 'over') { startGame(); return; }
      if (code === 'Space' && state === 'pause') { resumeGame(); return; }
      keys[mapped] = true;
      return;
    }
    if (e.repeat) return;
    switch (code) {
      case 'Enter': case 'NumpadEnter':
        if (state === 'menu') openModes(); else if (state === 'modes') pickMode('solo'); else if (state === 'diff') confirmDiff(); else if (state === 'settings') closeSettings(); else if (state === 'shop') closeShop(); else if (state === 'over') startGame(); else if (state === 'pause') resumeGame(); else if (state === 'garage') closeGarage(); else if (state === 'maps') closeMaps();
        break;
      case 'KeyN':
        if (state === 'menu' || state === 'over') openMaps(); else if (state === 'maps') closeMaps();
        break;
      case 'KeyC':
        if (state === 'menu' || state === 'over') openGarage(); else if (state === 'garage') closeGarage();
        break;
      case 'Escape':
        if (state === 'garage') closeGarage(); else if (state === 'maps') closeMaps(); else if (state === 'modes') closeModes(); else if (state === 'settings') closeSettings(); else if (state === 'shop') closeShop(); else if (state === 'play' || state === 'countdown') pauseGame(); else destroy();
        break;
      case 'KeyP':
        if (state === 'pause') resumeGame(); else pauseGame();
        break;
      case 'KeyM':
        Sound.toggleMute(); pop(Sound.muted ? 'SONIDO: NO' : 'SONIDO: SÍ', '#9bf6ff');
        break;
      case 'KeyQ':
        goMenu();
        break;
      case 'KeyR':
        if (state !== 'menu' && state !== 'garage' && state !== 'maps' && state !== 'controls' && state !== 'lobby' && state !== 'name' && state !== 'modes' && state !== 'diff' && state !== 'settings' && state !== 'shop') startGame();
        break;
      default: break;
    }
  }
  function onKeyUp(e) {
    if (e.code === 'KeyH') { Sound.hornOff(); return; }
    const mk = mpOn ? MPKEYS[e.code] : null;
    if (mk && PL.length === 2) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); PL[mk[0]].keys[mk[1]] = false; return; }
    const mapped = KEYMAP[e.code];
    if (!mapped) return;
    e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
    keys[mapped] = false;
  }
  function onBlur() { Sound.hornOff(); resetKeysExceptNone(); if (state === 'play' || state === 'countdown') pauseGame(); }
  function pointerPos(e) {
    if (e && typeof e.clientX === 'number') {
      const rect = (canvas.getBoundingClientRect && canvas.getBoundingClientRect()) || null;
      const rw = rect && rect.width ? rect.width : W, rh = rect && rect.height ? rect.height : H;
      return [(e.clientX - (rect ? rect.left : 0)) * (W / rw), (e.clientY - (rect ? rect.top : 0)) * (H / rh)];
    }
    return [-1, -1];
  }
  function onMove(e) { const p = pointerPos(e); mouseX = p[0]; mouseY = p[1]; if (gDrag) { gYaw = gDrag.yaw + (mouseX - gDrag.x) * 0.012; gHold = tAnim + 2; } if (sliderDrag) sliderDrag.set(U.limit((mouseX - sliderDrag.x) / sliderDrag.w, 0, 1)); }
  function onUp() { if (gDrag) { gDrag = null; gHold = tAnim + 1.2; } if (sliderDrag) { sliderDrag = null; saveCfg(); } }
  function onLeave() { mouseX = mouseY = -1; }
  function onPointer(e) {
    try { canvas.focus(); } catch (err) { /* ignorar */ }
    Sound.init(); Sound.resume();
    const pp = pointerPos(e), mx = pp[0], my = pp[1];
    mouseX = mx; mouseY = my;
    if (state === 'lobby' && nameEdit >= 0) endNameEdit();
    if (state === 'garage' && plateEdit) { plateEdit = false; saveBuilds(); }
    if (state === 'settings' && nameEditS) saveName();
    if (state === 'shop') codeEdit = false;
    for (let i = hits.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const h = hits[i];
      if (mx >= h.x && mx <= h.x + h.w && my >= h.y && my <= h.y + h.h) { h.fn(); return; }
    }
    if (state === 'garage') {
      const gu = U.limit(Math.min(W / 1280, H / 720), 0.6, 2), gx = (W - 1280 * gu) / 2, gy = (H - 720 * gu) / 2;
      if (mx >= gx + 380 * gu && mx <= gx + 900 * gu && my >= gy + 120 * gu && my <= gy + 450 * gu) { gDrag = { x: mx, yaw: gYaw }; return; }
    }
    if (state === 'over') startGame(); else if (state === 'pause') resumeGame();
  }
  function onDrop(e) {
    try {
      e.preventDefault();
      const f = e.dataTransfer && e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files[0];
      if (!f) return;
    } catch (err) { /* ignorar */ }
  }
  function onDragOver(e) { try { e.preventDefault(); } catch (err) { /* ignorar */ } }
  function onVisibility() { if (document.hidden) onBlur(); }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  //  Arranque y limpieza
  // ------------------------------------------------------------------
  function destroy() {
    if (destroyed) return;
    destroyed = true;
    try { window.cancelAnimationFrame(raf); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown, true);
    window.removeEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp, true);
    window.removeEventListener('blur', onBlur);
    window.removeEventListener('resize', resize);
    document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    canvas.removeEventListener('mousedown', onPointer);
    canvas.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
    window.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
    canvas.removeEventListener('mouseleave', onLeave);
    canvas.removeEventListener('drop', onDrop);
    canvas.removeEventListener('dragover', onDragOver);
    try { if (fileInput) fileInput.remove(); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    Sound.destroy();
    try { overlay.remove(); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
    if (window.__TURBO_GAME__ && window.__TURBO_GAME__.destroy === destroy) { try { delete window.__TURBO_GAME__; } catch (e) { window.__TURBO_GAME__ = null; } }
  }

  MAPS.forEach(function (m) { buildTrack(m); m.pts = mapPts; m.len = trackLength; });
  selectMap(mapIdx, true);
  updateCamera(0);
  window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown, true);
  window.addEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp, true);
  window.addEventListener('blur', onBlur);
  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
  canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', onPointer);
  canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
  window.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
  canvas.addEventListener('mouseleave', onLeave);
  canvas.addEventListener('drop', onDrop);
  canvas.addEventListener('dragover', onDragOver);
  window.__TURBO_GAME__ = { destroy: destroy };
  try { canvas.focus(); } catch (e) { /* ignorar */ }
  last = (typeof performance !== 'undefined' ? performance.now() : Date.now());
  raf = window.requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  console.log('%c TURBO HIGHWAY %c Haz clic en la pantalla del juego y pulsa ENTER. ESC para salir.',
    'background:#ff2d95;color:#fff;font-weight:bold;padding:3px 6px;border-radius:3px', 'color:#ffd400;font-weight:bold');
})();
